[P25 2] Definition and Acronyms
[TABLE block 26]
Acronym / Term | Definition
BeefCare (BCMS) | On-farm Beef Cattle Care Management System, the product specified in this document (project SEP490-G35).
SRS | Software Requirement Specification.
BF | Business Flow: an end-to-end business process (BF-01 to BF-09).
FE | Feature: a group of related system capabilities (FE-01 to FE-23).
UC | Use Case: a goal an actor achieves through BeefCare (UC-FExx-nn).
SCR | Screen identifier used in the Screen List (I.5.1) and Functional Requirements (III).
GBR / BR-D | General Business Rule (V.1) / Data Business Rule (I.3.3).
MSG | System message code (V.2): MSG-COM-nn for common messages, MSG-FExx-nn for feature messages.
REM | Reminder rule REM-01 to REM-13 (III.14).
JOB / API | Background job (III.24) / External API (III.25).
NFR / OR | Non-Functional Requirement (IV) / Other Requirement (V.3).
Tenant | An isolated farm workspace. One tenant is one farm.
Farm Owner, Farm Manager, Farm Technician | The three farm roles. Owner: owns the farm, manages members, subscription and herd exits. Manager: records and corrects care and reproduction events and maintains farm data. Technician: read-only field user.
CS, PM, SA | Customer Support, Platform Manager and System Admin: platform staff roles independent of any farm.
Membership | The link between an account and one farm, carrying the role and status in that farm.
Invitation | An Owner's invitation for an email address to join one farm with a role; valid for 3, 7 or 14 days (default 7).
Farm service status | PENDING_PAYMENT, ACTIVE, EXPIRED or SUSPENDED (GBR-13).
Internal Cattle ID | The permanent, never-reused system identifier of a cattle.
Display code | The human-readable cattle code (BC-nnnnnn) printed on the ear tag above the QR.
QR tag | The printed cattle ear tag whose QR code encodes the BeefCare address of the tag. It opens the cattle profile only for a signed-in member of the farm (GBR-28); BeefCare has no public cattle page. Different from a payment VietQR.
Herd status | ACTIVE (Đang nuôi), EXITED (Đã xuất, with exit type and date) or VOIDED (Nhập nhầm – đã hủy: herd entry voided as a data-entry mistake); separate from production purpose, life stage, health state and reproductive status (GBR-24, 3.4).
Exit pending | A derived indicator of an ACTIVE cattle on a DRAFT or PENDING herd exit voucher line: Chờ xuất, or Chờ xác nhận chết / mất for a PENDING death voucher, which also blocks new events (GBR-26, GBR-95).
Production purpose | Why the farm raises the cattle, chosen from the farm's purpose catalogue (for example "Bò sinh sản", "Vỗ béo xuất chuồng"); changed by the Owner only.
Purpose type | The fixed business meaning of a purpose catalogue item: BREEDING, FATTENING, SIRE or UNDECIDED. Rules and herd groups use the type, never the free-text purpose name (GBR-25).
Life stage | The cattle's physiological stage, from a fixed list: UNWEANED_CALF (Bê chưa cai sữa), WEANED_CALF (Bê cai sữa), YOUNG_STOCK (Bò tơ / hậu bị), ADULT (Trưởng thành). Use (breeding, fattening) is the purpose type, and pregnancy is a reproductive status, not a life stage.
Herd group | A derived grouping used in herd lists and reports: Calves (Bê), Fattening (Vỗ béo), Breeding bulls (Đực giống), Breeding females (Bò cái sinh sản), Young stock (Bò tơ), Other (Khác). Each ACTIVE cattle is in exactly one group (GBR-24).
Health state | Derived from health cases for an ACTIVE cattle: HEALTHY (Khỏe mạnh), MONITORING (Theo dõi) or UNDER_TREATMENT (Đang điều trị) (GBR-45).
Reproductive status | Derived from reproduction events for an applicable female: AWAITING_BREEDING (Chờ phối), IN_ESTRUS (Động dục), BRED (Đã phối – chờ khám thai), PREGNANT (Mang thai; sub-label Sắp đẻ near the expected date) or POST_CALVING (Sau sinh); other cattle show "Not applicable" (GBR-54, 3.4.3).
Record status | Status of a recorded event (weight, vaccination, estrus, breeding, check, calving, feeding): VALID or VOIDED; a corrected record stays VALID with a "Corrected" marker and a Record Revision; a weight may also be EXCLUDED as an outlier (BR-D15).
Breeding eligibility | An advisory indicator, Met or Not met, from the farm's breeding conditions (minimum age, minimum weight, no OPEN health case); it never blocks a recorded event (GBR-55).
Pen / Reception criteria | A pen or care area with a capacity / advisory purposes, stages, sex and minimum age or weight that the pen accepts.
Herd entry / Herd exit | The record of one cattle entering the herd (EXTERNAL or FARM_BORN) / leaving it (SALE, CULL, DEATH with loss kind Died or Missing, OTHER); each is one line of a voucher.
Herd entry voucher (phiếu nhập) | One receipt of one or more cattle into the farm, by purchase or as farm-born calves (code PN-YYYY-nnnn), with one line per cattle, the total value and the payments to the seller. DRAFT → POSTED → VOIDED; posting creates the cattle (GBR-90).
Herd exit voucher (phiếu xuất) | One departure of one or more cattle (sale, cull, death or missing, other; code PX-YYYY-nnnn), with one line per cattle, the total receivable and the collections from the buyer. DRAFT or PENDING → CONFIRMED → VOIDED; confirmed by the Owner only (GBR-94).
Trading partner | A seller, buyer or feed supplier in the farm's contact list. Partners have no BeefCare account (GBR-97).
Voucher payment | One payment to a seller or supplier, or one collection from a buyer, recorded against a voucher. Farm bookkeeping only; not an invoice, tax or accounting document (GBR-91).
Pricing basis | Per kg (unit price × weight) or per head (unit price per cattle) for the lines of a herd entry or exit voucher (GBR-92).
UT | "Ước tính": marks an estimated weight (basis ESTIMATED) in vouchers and lists.
Draft calf | A live calf saved at calving with status PENDING_CONFIRMATION, waiting for registration by the Owner or a Manager.
BCS | Body Condition Score; default farm scale 1.0–5.0.
Feed item | A feed or supplement in the farm's feed catalogue (code TA-nn), stocked in kg (GBR-98).
Feed receipt / Stock adjustment | A delivery of feed from a supplier that adds stock (PNK-YYYY-nnnn) / a stock count that records the difference between counted and system stock (ĐCK-YYYY-nnnn).
Ration (khẩu phần) | The planned kg of each feed per head and session for the pens it applies to; changed only by saving a new version with an effective date (GBR-101).
Feeding session (cữ cho ăn) | One feeding of one pen on one date (Morning, Afternoon or Evening). Saving it deducts the actual feed from stock (GBR-102).
Weighted average cost | Stock value ÷ stock quantity of a feed item, recalculated at each posted receipt; used to value feeding, adjustments and rations (GBR-98, GBR-105).
ADG | Average Daily Gain in kg/day between two valid weights (GBR-39).
MEASURED / ESTIMATED | Basis of a weight: scale measurement / tape or visual estimate.
Withdrawal period | Days after a treatment or an on-farm vaccination during which meat must not be sold; the profile shows "Meat withdrawal until dd/mm/yyyy" while one is active (GBR-46).
Estrus / Breeding / AI | Heat observed in a female / natural service or insemination / artificial insemination (Vietnamese: TTNT).
Pregnancy episode | One confirmed pregnancy from the confirming check until calving, loss or exit.
Expected calving date | TENTATIVE (from breeding) or CONFIRMED (after a PREGNANT check) date = breeding time + gestation length.
LMLM / FMD | Lở mồm long móng / Foot-and-Mouth Disease.
Plan price version | An immutable published price list for the 1-, 2- and 3-year terms.
Payment request (quote) | An immutable 24-hour transfer instruction for one farm and one term, with a unique payment reference and VietQR.
Purchased period | The service period granted by one accepted payment.
Policy A | The subscription confirmation and period rules (GBR-69 to GBR-75).
Policy B | The support ticket closure rule requiring explicit Owner acknowledgment (GBR-80).
VietQR | A Vietnamese bank-transfer QR code containing receiving account, amount and transfer content.
SePay | External service that reports bank transactions to BeefCare through webhooks.
Webhook | An HTTP call from SePay to the BeefCare backend when a transaction occurs.
HMAC-SHA256 | Keyed signature used to authenticate SePay webhook calls.
Bank transfer evidence | An authenticated bank transaction kept by BeefCare whether or not it matches a payment request.
Internal request | A named request from the assigned CS to a PM or SA about a ticket.
Resolution acknowledgment | The Owner's explicit confirmation that a resolution version solved the issue.
Subscription receipt | A read-only view of one accepted subscription payment.
Manual finance record | Platform income or expense recorded by the PM (DRAFT, POSTED, VOID).
Net cash flow | Receipts + posted other income − posted expenses for a period; not statutory profit.
Snapshot | An immutable result of one report run, used for display and export.
Request key | A client-generated key that makes a command safe to repeat (GBR-88).
Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh | The default time zone (UTC+7) for farm dates and platform finance.
OAuth 2.0 / OIDC | Open standards used for Google sign-in.
[/TABLE]
[P28 2] I. Overall Requirements
[P29 3] 1. Context Diagram
[P30 ] BeefCare is a responsive web SaaS application that helps small and medium beef cattle farms (about 150–300 head, mainly Zebu đỏ and Lai Sind) keep one digital record for every animal, from herd entry to herd exit. Field staff identify an animal by scanning its QR ear tag with a phone browser; the Farm Manager records weights, vaccinations, health cases and reproduction events against that animal and the daily feeding of each pen; the Farm Owner follows the herd through the dashboard, reminders and reports and makes ownership decisions (members, subscription, herd exits).
[P31 ] Each farm is an independent tenant. One Owner may own several farms; each farm has its own members, data, subscription and service status. A farm is registered in status PENDING_PAYMENT and becomes ACTIVE when its first subscription payment (1, 2 or 3 years, paid by bank transfer through VietQR and confirmed by SePay) is accepted. Platform staff operate the service: Customer Support handles Owner tickets, the Platform Manager manages farm service, subscriptions, payment reconciliation, plan prices and basic platform cash records, and the System Admin manages accounts, platform staff access and the audit history. Platform staff never access cattle or care records.
[TABLE block 32]
[Figure to be inserted] Context diagram – BeefCare in the centre; human actors Guest, Farm Owner, Farm Manager, Farm Technician, Customer Support, Platform Manager, System Admin; external systems Google Identity Provider, SePay, Email Delivery Service; label flows as in Table 1-1
[/TABLE]
[P34 ] Table 1-1 External entities and information exchanged
[TABLE block 35]
External entity | Direction | Main information exchanged
Guest | In / Out | Views the public introduction, features, 1/2/3-year prices, FAQ and contact; submits registration, sign-in and password-reset requests.
Farm Owner | In / Out | Owner profile, farm registration and information, invitations and member changes, herd entry vouchers and payments, pen and profile changes, herd exit vouchers and collections, feed stock and rations, catalogues and parameters, announcements, subscription payment requests, support tickets; receives dashboard, reports, reminders, notifications, payment instructions and ticket replies.
Farm Manager | In / Out | Pens, herd entry vouchers, cattle profiles and QR tags, feed receipts, stock counts, rations and feeding sessions, growth, vaccination, health and reproduction records and corrections, catalogues and parameters, announcements to Technicians; receives reminders, dashboard and reports.
Farm Technician | In / Out | QR scans and searches; receives read-only cattle, care, reproduction and today's ration information, reminders and announcements.
Customer Support | In / Out | Ticket assignment, replies, internal notes, resolutions, closure, internal requests; receives the ticket queue and support notifications.
Platform Manager | In / Out | Farm suspension/restoration, payment reconciliation decisions, plan price versions, manual finance records, internal request results; receives farm service overview, subscriptions, bank evidence, receipts and cash reports.
System Admin | In / Out | Account suspension/restoration, platform staff access, internal request results; receives account lists and the audit history.
Google Identity Provider | In / Out | BeefCare sends OAuth 2.0 / OpenID Connect authorization requests; Google returns the authenticated identity (subject, verified email, name).
SePay | In / Out | SePay sends authenticated bank-transaction webhooks; BeefCare looks up transactions for reconciliation and renders VietQR images for payment requests.
Email Delivery Service | Out | BeefCare sends invitation, email-verification, password-reset, password-changed and notification emails.
[/TABLE]
[P37 3] 2. Main Business Flow
[P38 ] The nine business flows below cover the main processes of BeefCare. Each flow lists its trigger, end condition and the ordered steps that the swimlane diagram shows (lanes = Role column). Decision steps state their branches in the Note column.
[P39 4] 2.1 Farm Onboarding, Subscription and Membership (BF-01)
[P40 ] Trigger: A Guest registers as a Farm Owner, or an existing Owner registers an additional farm or renews the subscription of an owned farm.
[P41 ] End condition: The farm is ACTIVE with an accepted purchased period, and each invited staff member has an ACTIVE membership (or the invitation is cancelled or expired).
[TABLE block 42]
[Figure to be inserted] Swimlane BF-01 – lanes Farm Owner, System, SePay, Invitee
[/TABLE]
[TABLE block 44]
Step | Step Name | Detailed Description | Role | Note
1 | Register Account and Farm | The Guest signs up with Google or with a verified email and password, completes the Owner profile and registers a farm; an existing Owner registers another farm or opens an owned farm to renew. | Farm Owner | Start. UC-FE01-01, UC-FE02-01. Renewal starts at Step 3.
2 | Save Farm and Owner Membership | The system creates the farm in PENDING_PAYMENT with its single Owner membership and opens plan selection. | System | A repeated submission returns the same farm.
3 | Select Term and Review | The Owner selects 1, 2 or 3 years and reviews farm, term, amount and price version, or reopens a valid request. | Farm Owner | UC-FE19-02. Renewal opens in the last 90 days of coverage.
4 | Create or Reuse Payment Request | The system issues one immutable 24-hour request with amount, receiving account, unique payment reference and VietQR, or returns the unexpired one. | System | Price change → review again; no duplicate request.
5 | Transfer the Amount | The Owner transfers the exact amount with the payment reference from a banking app. | Farm Owner | Outside BeefCare; displaying the QR confirms nothing.
6 | Send Bank Evidence | SePay sends the transaction to the BeefCare webhook. | SePay | UC-FE19-08, API-02.
7 | Payment Eligible? | The system authenticates the evidence and checks direction, amount, receiving account, payment reference, bank-paid time within the request window and that the evidence is not yet allocated. | System | Yes → Step 9; No → Step 8.
8 | Keep for Review | The system keeps the evidence for PM review (BF-08); the request stays PENDING or becomes EXPIRED at its deadline. | System | No period is granted without confirmation.
9 | Confirm PAID and Activate | The system records PAID once, creates the purchased period, sets the farm ACTIVE (unless suspended), publishes the receipt and notifies the Owner. | System | Payment never lifts a suspension (GBR-15).
10 | Invite Member | The Owner enters the invitee's email and chooses Farm Manager or Farm Technician; the role's fixed permissions are shown. | Farm Owner | UC-FE03-01. ACTIVE farm only.
11 | Create Invitation and Send Email | The system checks service, email and existing membership, creates a PENDING invitation with the validity chosen by the Owner (default 7 days) and sends the email. | System | The Owner may resend or cancel.
12 | Open Link and Sign In | The invitee opens the link, signs in with Google or a verified email and password, reviews the farm and role and selects Accept. | Invitee | UC-FE03-04.
13 | Invitation Valid? | The system checks that the invitation is PENDING and unexpired, the farm is ACTIVE and the signed-in email equals the invited email. | System | Yes → Step 14; No → show the reason, end.
14 | Create Membership | The system creates exactly one membership with the role, marks the invitation ACCEPTED, notifies the Owner and opens the farm. | System | End.
[/TABLE]
[P46 4] 2.2 Farm Setup and Reference Configuration (BF-02)
[P47 ] Trigger: A farm becomes ACTIVE for the first time, or the Owner or a Manager needs to change pens, catalogues or parameters.
[P48 ] End condition: The farm information, pens, catalogues and parameters are saved as valid versions and are available for herd entry, care recording and reminders.
[TABLE block 49]
[Figure to be inserted] Swimlane BF-02 – lanes Farm Owner, Farm Manager, System
[/TABLE]
[TABLE block 51]
Step | Step Name | Detailed Description | Role | Note
1 | Update Farm Information | The Owner completes registration, contact and operating information (time zone, shift start, operating notes). | Farm Owner | Start. UC-FE02-03.
2 | Configure Pens | The Owner or a Manager creates or updates pens with code, name, capacity and advisory reception criteria, and can stop new placements in a pen. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager | UC-FE04-02 to 04.
3 | Maintain Catalogues | The Owner or a Manager maintains breeds, vaccines, reference medicines and production purposes. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager | UC-FE18-02 to 16. Referenced items are deactivated, not deleted.
4 | Configure Parameters | The Owner or a Manager sets life-stage ages, breeding conditions, gestation length, pregnancy-check interval, breeding window, estrus cycle, postpartum interval, BCS scale, weighing interval and reminder lead times, with a source note. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager | UC-FE18-17.
5 | Validate | The system checks the fields, uniqueness, the farm context and the user's role. | System | Merge point after Step 4 or Step 6.
6 | Valid? | The system decides whether the change is valid. | System | Yes → Step 7; No → the user corrects the indicated fields and resubmits (Step 5).
7 | Save with Version | The system saves the change (a new version for criteria and parameters), recomputes future reminders and keeps all recorded events unchanged. | System | End. Vaccination schedules are created in BF-04.
[/TABLE]
[P53 4] 2.3 Cattle Lifecycle: Herd Entry, Placement and Herd Exit (BF-03)
[P54 ] Trigger: Cattle are bought or brought into the farm, live calves are waiting for registration (REM-09), or the Owner sells, culls or loses animals.
[P55 ] End condition: Each received animal has one permanent identity, profile, pen placement and history from a posted herd entry voucher; each departed animal is EXITED with a read-only record on a confirmed herd exit voucher; the amounts paid to sellers and collected from buyers are recorded (or the voucher is voided).
[TABLE block 56]
[Figure to be inserted] Swimlane BF-03 – lanes Farm Owner, Farm Manager, System
[/TABLE]
[TABLE block 58]
Step | Step Name | Detailed Description | Role | Note
1 | Open Herd Entry Voucher | The Owner or a Manager selects "Create entry voucher" on Herd Entry Vouchers, or opens the pending calves from the list or from reminder REM-09. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager | Start. UC-FE06-01.
2 | Entry Type? | The user chooses a purchase voucher or a farm-born calf voucher. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager | Purchase → Step 3; Farm-born → Step 4.
3 | Enter Purchase Voucher | The user enters the entry date, seller (trading partner), quarantine certificate, vehicle and note; adds one line per cattle by hand or from an Excel file (ear tag or "tag later", breed, sex, birth date and accuracy, life stage, purpose, weight and basis, receiving pen, unit price, pre-entry history); chooses per-kg or per-head pricing; enters the transport fee, amount paid, method and due date. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager | A DRAFT can be saved at any time and has no effect (GBR-90).
4 | Select Pending Calves | The user selects draft calves; dam, sire, breed, sex and birth weight come from the calving and are read-only; the user sets the receiving pen and purpose of each calf. No price or payment. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager | Draft calves are created by a Manager in BF-05 (GBR-93).
5 | Check Voucher | The system checks tags, receiving pens (INACTIVE or full blocks; unmet criteria warn), dates, that each calf is still pending, and warns when a purchase has no quarantine certificate. | System | Errors → back to Step 3 or 4.
6 | Post Voucher and Create Cattle | For each line, in one transaction, the system creates the Internal Cattle ID, display code, profile, herd entry, history declarations, first pen assignment and QR tag; the voucher becomes POSTED with its totals and initial payment; REM-09 is resolved and matching vaccination schedules are listed. | System | UC-FE09-03 to add cattle to a schedule.
7 | Record Payment | The user records later payments to the seller until nothing is outstanding. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager | UC-FE06-04. A voucher recorded by mistake is voided with UC-FE06-05.
8 | Print or Replace QR Tag | The user prints the QR tags of the voucher, now or later; a lost or damaged tag is replaced without changing the identity. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager | UC-FE05-04.
9 | Update Stage or Purpose | During the animal's life the user confirms each life-stage change (Unweaned calf → Weaned calf → Young stock → Adult) with effective date and reason; only the Owner changes the production purpose, whose purpose type decides the animal's herd group. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager | UC-FE05-03. From the profile or a REM-08 stage suggestion.
10 | Move to Another Pen | The user selects one animal (from its profile) or several animals (from the pen detail), the destination pen, move date and reason. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager | UC-FE04-05. From the profile or REM-08.
11 | Move Allowed? | The system checks the pen and each animal: INACTIVE pen or not enough free places → blocked; criteria not met → warning, continue only with a reason. | System | Yes → Step 12; Blocked → keep current pen.
12 | Save Pen Move | The system closes the current assignment, opens the new one and updates occupancy and pen history. | System | Steps 9–12 repeat during the animal's life.
13 | Open Herd Exit Voucher | The Owner creates an exit voucher (Sale, Cull or Other) and adds ACTIVE cattle from a filtered list or by QR scan, or opens a PENDING death/missing voucher created when a Manager closed a health case as DIED or MISSING (BF-04, reminder REM-12). | Farm Owner | UC-FE13-01.
14 | Enter Lines and Amounts | The Owner enters the exit date, buyer or destination, transport certificate, vehicle and note; the exit weight of each line (default the latest weight, marked UT when estimated) and the unit price; deductions, deposit collected and due date. For a death: cause and carcass disposal, no amounts. | Farm Owner | Saved as DRAFT until confirmed.
15 | Review Checks and Confirm | The Owner reviews the checks per cattle (meat withdrawal period, OPEN and CHRONIC health cases, future doses and reminders, open pregnancy, draft calves of the dam), ticks the confirmation and confirms, or dismisses a pending voucher with a reason. | Farm Owner | Until confirmed the cattle stay ACTIVE.
16 | Record Exit | For each line the system sets EXITED, closes the pen assignment, cancels future doses and reminders, closes OPEN and CHRONIC health cases and any open pregnancy and locks the record; the voucher becomes CONFIRMED. | System | End. Collections: UC-FE13-04. A mistaken voucher is voided by the Owner (UC-FE13-03).
[/TABLE]
[P60 4] 2.4 Daily Care: Growth, Vaccination and Health (BF-04)
[P61 ] Trigger: The Farm Manager opens the day's reminders (weighing, vaccination, follow-ups) or an abnormal sign is observed in the pen.
[P62 ] End condition: The care performed is recorded against each animal, reminders are updated, and any death or loss is pending the Owner's herd-exit decision.
[TABLE block 63]
[Figure to be inserted] Swimlane BF-04 – lanes Farm Manager, Farm Technician (observer, read-only), System, Farm Owner
[/TABLE]
[TABLE block 65]
Step | Step Name | Detailed Description | Role | Note
1 | Review Reminders | The Manager (and Technicians, read-only) open the home screen or Farm Reminders: doses due or overdue, weighing due, open cases. | Farm Manager / Farm Technician | Start. UC-FE14-01.
2 | Identify the Animal | The user scans the QR tag or searches, and opens the profile. | Farm Manager / Farm Technician | UC-FE05-05. Technicians report what they see to the Manager.
3 | Care Activity? | The Manager chooses the activity to record. | Farm Manager | Growth → Step 4; Health → Step 6; Vaccination → Step 10.
4 | Record Weight and/or BCS | The Manager records time, weight, method, basis and/or BCS; the measurer may be a Technician. | Farm Manager | UC-FE08-01.
5 | Update Growth Indicators | The system saves the assessment, recalculates current weight and ADG, resolves REM-01 and may suggest a pen move or a life-stage change (REM-08). | System | Suggestions only; changes happen in BF-03.
6 | Record Abnormal Signs | The Manager records observed time, temperature, signs, severity and observer, and opens a new case or adds to an OPEN or CHRONIC case; the health state becomes at least Monitoring. | Farm Manager | UC-FE10-02. Also for post-vaccination reactions.
7 | Record Treatment and Follow-up | The Manager records each treatment actually given (medicine, dose, route, time, withdrawal days) and follow-up observations; the health state becomes Under treatment and the meat withdrawal date is updated. | Farm Manager | UC-FE10-04, UC-FE10-03. No diagnosis or prescription.
8 | Case Outcome? | The Manager keeps the case OPEN, moves it to CHRONIC (long-term monitoring) or closes it as RECOVERED, DIED or MISSING. | Farm Manager | OPEN or CHRONIC → Step 7; DIED/MISSING → Step 9; RECOVERED → end of branch.
9 | Create Pending Exit | The system creates a PENDING herd exit for the Owner (BF-03 Step 12); the animal stays ACTIVE until the Owner decides. | System | GBR-47.
10 | Manage Vaccination Schedule | The Manager creates or updates a schedule: vaccine, doses, interval, booster and target cattle by pen, herd group, life stage or selection. | Farm Manager | UC-FE09-02, UC-FE09-03.
11 | Evaluate Doses | The system derives each dose as upcoming, due or overdue and raises REM-06. | System | JOB-01.
12 | Can Vaccinate Now? | For each selected animal the Manager decides whether the dose is given now. | Farm Manager | Yes → Step 13; No → defer with reason and new date (UC-FE09-06).
13 | Record Vaccination | The Manager records vaccine, batch, time, dose, route, withdrawal days and vaccinator for one animal or a selected batch; the system saves one record per animal, closes the doses and updates the meat withdrawal date. | Farm Manager | UC-FE09-05. A reaction goes to Step 6.
14 | Update Timeline and Reports | The system adds the records to each animal's timeline, the dashboard and reports. | System | End.
[/TABLE]
[P67 4] 2.5 Reproduction: Estrus to Calf Registration (BF-05)
[P68 ] Trigger: A female shows estrus, a reproduction reminder falls due (breeding window, pregnancy check, upcoming calving, postpartum), or a calving occurs.
[P69 ] End condition: The calving and every calf outcome are recorded and each live calf is registered (directly or after draft confirmation), or the pregnancy is closed as lost.
[TABLE block 70]
[Figure to be inserted] Swimlane BF-05 – lanes Farm Manager, System, Farm Owner
[/TABLE]
[TABLE block 72]
Step | Step Name | Detailed Description | Role | Note
1 | Open Female Profile | The Manager opens the female from Reproduction Worklist, Cattle List, a reminder or a QR scan and views the Reproduction tab: reproductive status, the actions it allows and the breeding eligibility indicator. | Farm Manager | Start. Only females at stage Young stock or Adult with purpose type Breeding or Undecided, or with an open pregnancy, are followed (GBR-54). The Owner and Technicians view read-only.
2 | Record Estrus | The Manager records the observed time, observer and description. | Farm Manager | UC-FE11-02. Skip when breeding happened without an observed estrus.
3 | Suggest Breeding Window | The system shows the breeding window (estrus + 8 to 12 hours by default, REM-02) and cancels any tentative expected calving date of an earlier breeding. | System | Suggestion only.
4 | Record Breeding | The Manager records bred time, method, sire or semen, performer and the optional linked estrus. | Farm Manager | UC-FE11-03. Eligibility "Not met" is a warning only (GBR-55).
5 | Set Tentative Date | The system sets the TENTATIVE expected calving date and the pregnancy-check reminder (REM-03, breeding + 28 days by default). | System | A newer breeding replaces the date.
6 | Record Pregnancy Check | The Manager records time, result, method and examiner (often an external veterinarian). | Farm Manager | UC-FE12-02.
7 | Result? | The system evaluates the result. | System | PREGNANT → Step 8; NOT_PREGNANT → cancel the tentative date, back to Step 1; INCONCLUSIVE → REM-04, back to Step 6.
8 | Open Pregnancy Episode | The system opens the episode, marks the expected date CONFIRMED and prepares REM-05 (30 days before calving by default). | System | —
9 | Record Pregnancy Update | The Manager records an observation, a complication (optionally linked to a health case) or a loss. | Farm Manager | UC-FE12-03.
10 | Pregnancy Lost? | The system checks the update type. | System | LOSS → close as LOST, cancel REM-05, back to Step 1; otherwise → Step 11.
11 | Record Calving and Calf Outcomes | The Manager records birth time, attendant, course, dam condition and one outcome per calf; for each live calf enters sex, weight, tag/QR, pen and chooses Save as draft calf or Register now. | Farm Manager | UC-FE12-04. Tag must be unique in the farm.
12 | Save Calving | The system closes the episode as CALVED, sets the dam POST_CALVING, resolves REM-05 and prepares REM-07 (postpartum). | System | —
13 | Register Now? | The system applies the choice made for each live calf. | System | Register now → Step 16; Draft → Step 14; no live calf → end.
14 | Save Draft Calf | The system saves the calf as PENDING_CONFIRMATION and creates REM-09 for the Owner and Managers. | System | A draft calf receives no other events.
15 | Confirm Draft Calf | The Owner or a Manager reviews and confirms the draft calf (BF-03 Step 4). | Farm Owner / Farm Manager | UC-FE06-01.
16 | Create Calf Record | The system creates the calf's Internal Cattle ID, links dam and sire, assigns the pen and resolves REM-09. | System | End.
[/TABLE]
[P74 4] 2.6 Farm Coordination and Monitoring (BF-06)
[P75 ] Trigger: The Owner or a Manager needs to inform staff, or wants to review the herd situation; or a member opens an animal in the field.
[P76 ] End condition: Recipients have received the announcement version; the Owner or Manager has reviewed the indicators and opened the records or actions needed.
[TABLE block 77]

[/TABLE]

Step | Step Name | Detailed Description | Role | Note
1 | Compose Announcement | The author writes title, body and recipients: the Owner addresses Managers and Technicians; a Manager addresses Technicians. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager | Start of the coordination path. UC-FE07-01.
2 | Save Draft | The system validates the audience and saves a draft visible only to its author. | System | —
3 | Review and Publish | The author reviews content and resolved recipients and publishes once. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager | UC-FE07-03.
4 | Deliver and Notify | The system saves the version and recipient snapshot and notifies the recipients. | System | Later joiners do not receive it.
5 | Read Announcements and Reminders | Recipients read announcements and reminders and open each reminder's source record. | Farm Manager / Farm Technician | Reading changes no state.
6 | Correction Needed? | The author decides whether the published content needs a correction. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager | Yes → publish a new version with a reason (UC-FE07-02), Step 4; No → end of path.
7 | Open Dashboard or Report | The Owner or a Manager selects period and filters, or a report type and criteria. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager | Start of the management path. UC-FE15-01, 02.
8 | Aggregate Indicators | The system computes herd, growth, health, vaccination and reproduction indicators and attention lists (including draft calves and pending death vouchers) with generation time. | System | Missing data is shown as missing.
9 | Drill Down or Scan | The user drills down from an indicator, searches, or scans an ear tag. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager / Farm Technician | Start of the field path.
10 | Show Profile and Timeline | The system shows the profile and timeline in date order, with occurrence and entry time and details limited by role. | System | UC-FE05-02, UC-FE16-01.
11 | Action Needed? | The user decides whether there is something to record or decide. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager | Yes → Step 12; No → end.
12 | Open Permitted Use Case | The Manager opens a recording use case (BF-04, BF-05); the Owner opens an Owner action (BF-03 herd exit, purpose change). | Farm Owner / Farm Manager | End. Nothing is edited from the dashboard itself.
[/TABLE]
[P81 4] 2.7 Customer Support and Internal Requests (BF-07)
[P82 ] Trigger: A Farm Owner submits a support ticket about their account or an owned farm.
[P83 ] End condition: The ticket is CLOSED after the Owner explicitly confirmed the latest resolution and no internal request is open.
[TABLE block 84]
[Figure to be inserted] Swimlane BF-07 – lanes Farm Owner, Customer Support, Platform Manager / System Admin, System
[/TABLE]
[TABLE block 86]
Step | Step Name | Detailed Description | Role | Note
1 | Submit Ticket | The Owner submits a ticket with category, scope (account or owned farm), subject and description; the system saves it OPEN with priority NORMAL. | Farm Owner | Start. UC-FE20-01. Allowed while the farm is inactive.
2 | Claim Ticket | A CS claims the ticket from the queue (or receives a handover) and becomes responsible; status IN_PROGRESS. | Customer Support | UC-FE20-07.
3 | Investigate and Reply | The assigned CS sets category and priority, replies PUBLIC and writes CS-only notes. | Customer Support | Merge point. UC-FE20-08, 09.
4 | Next Action? | The CS chooses the next action. | Customer Support | Ask Owner → Step 5; internal help → Step 6; ready → Step 8.
5 | Owner Replies | The CS sets WAITING_CUSTOMER; the Owner replies; the system returns the ticket to IN_PROGRESS. | Farm Owner | Back to Step 3. UC-FE20-03.
6 | Send Internal Request | The CS sends an OPEN request to a named PM or SA with the question and permitted context. | Customer Support | UC-FE20-12. One OPEN request per role.
7 | Handle Internal Request | The named PM or SA performs the authorized work in their own use cases and returns a sanitized result (DONE). | Platform Manager / System Admin | UC-FE20-13. Back to Step 3; never resolves the ticket.
8 | Record Resolution | The CS records a PUBLIC resolution (new version) when no internal request is OPEN; the system sets RESOLVED and notifies the Owner. | Customer Support | UC-FE20-10.
9 | Issue Resolved? | The Owner confirms the latest resolution, or reopens with a reason. | Farm Owner | Confirm → Step 10; Reopen → Step 3 (UC-FE20-05).
10 | Close Ticket | The CS reviews the Owner's confirmation and closes; the system rechecks and commits CLOSED. | Customer Support | End. UC-FE20-11. CLOSED is read-only.
[/TABLE]
[P88 4] 2.8 Platform Administration: Payments, Finance and Access (BF-08)
[P89 ] Trigger: SePay delivers a bank transaction that cannot be confirmed automatically, or a Platform Manager or System Admin starts an administrative task.
[P90 ] End condition: Each evidence is accepted, held or rejected with a reason; farm service, prices and finance records are updated with history; account and staff access changes are applied and audited.
[TABLE block 91]
[Figure to be inserted] Swimlane BF-08 – lanes SePay, System, Platform Manager, System Admin
[/TABLE]
[TABLE block 93]
Step | Step Name | Detailed Description | Role | Note
1 | Which Task? | The flow starts from a bank transaction, a PM task or an SA task. | System | Bank evidence → Step 2; PM → Step 4; SA → Step 11.
2 | Receive Bank Evidence | SePay delivers the transaction; the system authenticates and stores it once. | SePay | API-02.
3 | Evaluate Eligibility | The system applies the shared acceptance rules (GBR-72). | System | Eligible → Step 7; otherwise → Step 5 (review list).
4 | Choose PM Operation | The PM chooses farm service, payment reconciliation, plan prices or finance. | Platform Manager | Service/prices → Step 6; reconciliation → Step 5; finance → Step 8.
5 | Review Evidence | The PM reviews the evidence and candidate request and records ACCEPT, HOLD or REJECT with a reason; ACCEPT re-runs the shared rules. | Platform Manager | UC-FE19-06. No manual PAID override; HOLD stays open.
6 | Manage Service and Prices | The PM suspends or restores a farm with a reason, or publishes 1/2/3-year prices now or at a scheduled time. | Platform Manager | UC-FE02-05, UC-FE19-07.
7 | Confirm PAID and Publish Receipt | The system records PAID once, creates the purchased period, publishes the receipt and notifies the Owner. | System | Shared with BF-01 Step 9.
8 | Manage Manual Finance Records | The PM creates, edits, posts, discards or voids manual income and expense records. | Platform Manager | UC-FE21-02 to 07. Receipts are read-only.
9 | Run Cash Report | The PM selects period and farm; the system builds a snapshot of receipts plus posted income minus expense. | Platform Manager | UC-FE21-08.
10 | Export Report | The PM exports the displayed snapshot to XLSX. | Platform Manager | End of the finance branch. UC-FE21-09.
11 | Manage Accounts and Staff Access | The SA suspends or restores accounts and grants, changes, revokes or restores CS, PM or SA access with a reason. | System Admin | UC-FE03-08, 09. At least one ACTIVE SA remains.
12 | Review Audit History | The SA searches the allowlisted platform audit history. | System Admin | End. UC-FE17-01.
[/TABLE]
[P95 4] 2.9 Feed Supply and Daily Feeding (BF-09)
[P96 ] Trigger: Feed is delivered by a supplier, a feeding session is due (REM-11), a ration has to change, the stock of a feed reaches its minimum level (REM-10) or a stock count is planned.
[P97 ] End condition: Feed stock reflects every posted receipt, adjustment and feeding record; each pen's feeding sessions are recorded against its effective ration; consumption and cost are available for the period.
[TABLE block 98]
[Figure to be inserted] Swimlane BF-09 – lanes Farm Owner / Farm Manager, Farm Technician (read-only), System
[/TABLE]
[TABLE block 100]
Step | Step Name | Detailed Description | Role | Note
1 | Review Feed Stock | The Owner or a Manager opens Feed Management; Feed Stock shows the stock, minimum level, average daily use, days of cover and lots near expiry of each feed. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager | Start. UC-FE23-01. Receipt → Step 2; ration → Step 5; feeding → Step 7; stock count → Step 11.
2 | Enter Feed Receipt | The user enters the supplier, receipt date, invoice or delivery note, receiver and storage place, and one line per feed (lot, expiry date, quantity in the purchase unit, conversion to kg, unit price), the transport fee, amount paid and due date. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager | UC-FE23-02. Feed items and suppliers: UC-FE23-10.
3 | Check Receipt | The system checks that each feed is ACTIVE, quantities are positive, prices not negative, expiry dates after the receipt date and the date not in the future. | System | Invalid → Step 2. A DRAFT has no effect.
4 | Post Receipt | The system adds the stock in kg, recalculates the weighted average cost, records the supplier balance and resolves REM-10 for feeds above their minimum level. | System | Later payments in UC-FE23-02; void with UC-FE23-03 only while the stock still covers the receipt.
5 | Create or Update Ration | The user defines the ration name, herd group, pens, sessions per day and kg per head per session of each feed, the effective date (not earlier than tomorrow) and the basis. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager | UC-FE23-06. Editing creates a new version.
6 | Save Ration Version | The system checks that each pen has at most one effective ration per day, saves the version, ends the previous version the day before and shows the estimated daily quantity and cost. | System | Recorded feeding is never changed (GBR-101).
7 | Open Feeding Session | The Manager selects the pen and session; the system shows the effective ration, the planned quantity of each feed (ration × current headcount) and its stock. Technicians follow Today's Rations on the phone. | Farm Manager / Farm Technician | UC-FE23-08; UC-FE23-05 (read-only).
8 | Enter Actual Quantities | The Manager enters the actual kg of each feed (or fills them from the plan), extra feeds outside the ration, leftovers, the member who fed (may be a Technician) and a note. | Farm Manager | 
9 | Valid? | The system checks that no record exists for the pen, date and session, the date is not in the future and no feed exceeds its stock. | System | Yes → Step 10; No → Step 8.
10 | Save and Deduct Stock | The system saves the feeding record, deducts each feed from stock at its average cost, resolves REM-11 and raises REM-10 when a feed reaches its minimum level. A correction or void with a reason adjusts stock by the difference (UC-FE23-09). | System | End of the feeding branch.
11 | Count Stock | The user enters the counted stock of each feed and a reason for every difference. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager | UC-FE23-04.
12 | Post Adjustment | The system records one stock movement per difference, valued at the average cost; no stock becomes negative and earlier documents are not changed. | System | 
13 | Review Consumption and Cost | The user selects a period and views consumption, cost per head per day and the variance from the rations by herd group, pen or feed. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager | End. UC-FE23-11.
[/TABLE]
[P102 3] 3. Conceptual Data Model
[P103 4] 3.1 Entity Relationship Diagram
[P104 ] The conceptual model shows the business entities of BeefCare and the relationships between them. It is drawn at conceptual level: each entity is shown by its name only, in Crow's Foot notation, with a business verb read from Entity A to Entity B and the minimum and maximum cardinality at each end. Attributes, identifiers, foreign keys, line items, histories and versions are not drawn; they are described with their owning entity in 3.2 ("Detail" items), and data integrity rules are listed in 3.3. Every farm-owned entity belongs to exactly one Farm (tenant); only the main ownership relationships of the Farm are drawn. Users are shown by role rather than as one generic user. A person has one User Account; Farm Owner, Farm Manager and Farm Technician (the roles a person holds in farms) and Customer Support, Platform Manager and System Admin (platform staff roles) are specialisations of it, each drawn as an "is a" relationship to User Account with cardinality 0..1 : 1 (R01): a role belongs to exactly one account and an account holds each role at most once. The roles overlap – one account may be the Owner of one farm and a Manager or Technician of another (one role in each farm), and holds at most one platform staff role – and a new account may hold no role yet. An invitation is accepted by the User Account of the invited person, because the Manager or Technician role exists only after acceptance (R07). Relationships that can be derived from other relationships are not drawn (for example, the Owner who sends an invitation is the Owner of the farm that issues it); who may perform each action is given in the Permission Matrix (4.4). Platform entities (accounts, plan prices, payments, tickets and finance records) belong to the platform. Technical and derived records (reminders, audit log, record revisions, notifications, stock movements, receipts and report snapshots) are not part of the conceptual diagram and are described in 3.2 J.
[TABLE block 105]
[Figure to be inserted] Conceptual ERD – overview of all business entities and relationships (Crow's Foot, entity names only)
[/TABLE]
[P107 ] Table 3.1-1 Subject areas and conceptual entities
[TABLE block 108]
# | Subject area | Conceptual entities (shown in the diagram) | Detail kept with the entity (described in 3.2, not drawn) | Main features
A | Users and Farm Membership | User Account, Farm Owner, Farm Manager, Farm Technician, Customer Support, Platform Manager, System Admin, Farm, Farm Invitation | User Account: Sign-in Method; Farm: Farm Membership, Farm Service Change, Farm Parameter Version | FE-01, FE-02, FE-03
B | Farm Reference Data | Breed, Production Purpose, Vaccine, Medicine, Pen | Pen: Pen Reception Criteria | FE-04, FE-18
C | Cattle and Herd Movement | Cattle, Cattle QR Tag, Herd Entry Voucher, Herd Exit Voucher, Trading Partner, Voucher Payment | Cattle: Cattle Profile Change, Pen Assignment; Herd Entry Voucher: Herd Entry, Pre-entry History Declaration; Herd Exit Voucher: Herd Exit | FE-05, FE-06, FE-13
D | Care and Health | Growth Assessment, Vaccination Schedule, Vaccination Record, Health Case, Treatment | Vaccination Schedule: Scheduled Dose; Health Case: Health Observation | FE-08, FE-09, FE-10
E | Reproduction | Estrus Event, Breeding Event, Pregnancy Check, Pregnancy Episode, Calving Event, Calf Outcome | Pregnancy Episode: Pregnancy Update | FE-11, FE-12
F | Feed Management | Feed Item, Feed Receipt, Stock Adjustment, Ration, Feeding Record | Feed Receipt: Feed Receipt Line; Stock Adjustment: Stock Adjustment Line; Ration: Ration Component; Feeding Record: Feeding Record Line | FE-23
G | Farm Announcements | Farm Announcement | Farm Announcement: Announcement Version, Announcement Delivery | FE-07
H | Subscription and Platform Finance | Plan Price Version, Subscription, Payment Request, Bank Transfer Evidence, Manual Finance Record | Payment Request: Purchased Period; Bank Transfer Evidence: Evidence Review | FE-19, FE-21
I | Customer Support | Support Ticket, Ticket Message, Ticket Resolution, Internal Request | Support Ticket: Ticket History Event; Ticket Resolution: Resolution Acknowledgment | FE-20
J | Supporting records | Not drawn: Reminder, Audit Log Entry, Record Revision, Notification, Stock Movement, Subscription Receipt, Cash Report Snapshot, Public Landing Content | — | FE-14, FE-17, FE-21, FE-22, FE-23, all correction UCs
