[P128 ] Table 3.1-2 Relationships
[P129 ] Cardinality is written as "Entity A side : Entity B side" (1 = exactly one, 0..1 = zero or one, 1..* = one or many, 0..* = zero or many). A role in brackets names the part an entity plays in the relationship.
[TABLE block 130]
# | Entity A | Relationship | Entity B | Cardinality | Business meaning
R01 | Farm Owner / Farm Manager / Farm Technician / Customer Support / Platform Manager / System Admin | is a | User Account | 0..1 : 1 | Each role belongs to exactly one User Account and an account holds each role at most once. The roles overlap: one account may be the Owner of one farm and a Manager or Technician of other farms (one role per farm) and may hold one platform staff role; a new account may hold no role yet.
R02 | System Admin | suspends or restores | User Account | 0..* : 0..* | Account suspension and restoration with a reason; at least one ACTIVE SA remains.
R03 | Farm Owner | owns | Farm | 1 : 1..* | An Owner may own several farms; each farm has exactly one Owner (OWNER membership).
R04 | Farm Manager | works in | Farm | 0..* : 1..* | MANAGER membership; one person has one role in each farm.
R05 | Farm Technician | works in | Farm | 0..* : 1..* | TECHNICIAN membership; read-only access to farm records.
R06 | Farm | issues | Farm Invitation | 1 : 0..* | Invitations to join the farm with a role.
R07 | User Account | accepts | Farm Invitation | 0..1 : 0..* | The invited person accepts with the account whose verified email equals the invited email; acceptance creates the MANAGER or TECHNICIAN membership, so the role exists only after acceptance.
R08 | Platform Manager | suspends or restores | Farm | 0..* : 0..* | Farm service suspension and restoration with a reason.
R09 | System Admin | grants access to | Customer Support | 1..* : 0..* | The SA grants, changes, revokes or restores CS access.
R10 | System Admin | grants access to | Platform Manager | 1..* : 0..* | The SA grants, changes, revokes or restores PM access (and SA access of other admins).
R11 | Farm | maintains | Breed | 1 : 0..* | Farm catalogue.
R12 | Farm | maintains | Production Purpose | 1 : 0..* | Farm catalogue with a fixed purpose type.
R13 | Farm | maintains | Vaccine | 1 : 0..* | Farm catalogue.
R14 | Farm | maintains | Medicine | 1 : 0..* | Farm catalogue.
R15 | Farm | contains | Pen | 1 : 0..* | Pens and care areas of the farm.
R16 | Farm | raises | Cattle | 1 : 0..* | Every animal belongs to one farm.
R17 | Breed | classifies | Cattle | 1 : 0..* | Breed of the animal.
R18 | Production Purpose | is assigned to | Cattle | 1 : 0..* | Why the farm raises the animal.
R19 | Pen | houses | Cattle | 0..1 : 0..* | Current placement; the placement history is kept with the cattle.
R20 | Cattle (dam) | is the mother of | Cattle | 0..1 : 0..* | Known dam in the farm.
R21 | Cattle (sire) | is the father of | Cattle | 0..1 : 0..* | Known sire in the farm, otherwise an external sire or semen code.
R22 | Cattle | carries | Cattle QR Tag | 1 : 1..* | One ACTIVE tag at a time; replaced tags are kept.
R23 | Herd Entry Voucher | brings in | Cattle | 1 : 1..* | Each animal enters the herd through exactly one voucher.
R24 | Herd Exit Voucher | removes | Cattle | 0..1 : 1..* | An animal leaves through at most one effective exit voucher.
R25 | Trading Partner | sells in | Herd Entry Voucher | 0..1 : 0..* | Seller of a purchase; none for farm-born calves.
R26 | Trading Partner | buys in | Herd Exit Voucher | 0..1 : 0..* | Buyer of a sale or cull.
R27 | Herd Entry Voucher / Herd Exit Voucher / Feed Receipt | is settled by | Voucher Payment | 1 : 0..* | Payments to sellers and suppliers, collections from buyers.
R28 | Calf Outcome | is registered as | Cattle | 0..1 : 0..1 | A live calf becomes at most one cattle.
R29 | Cattle | is assessed in | Growth Assessment | 1 : 0..* | Weight and body condition history.
R30 | Vaccine | is planned in | Vaccination Schedule | 1 : 0..* | One vaccine per schedule.
R31 | Vaccination Schedule | covers | Cattle | 0..* : 0..* | Planned doses for the targeted animals.
R32 | Cattle | receives | Vaccination Record | 1 : 0..* | Vaccinations given on the farm or declared at entry.
R33 | Vaccine | is given in | Vaccination Record | 1 : 0..* | Vaccine actually used.
R34 | Vaccination Schedule | is fulfilled by | Vaccination Record | 0..1 : 0..* | A planned dose is fulfilled by an on-farm vaccination.
R35 | Cattle | has | Health Case | 1 : 0..* | Health problems of the animal.
R36 | Health Case | is treated by | Treatment | 1 : 0..* | Treatments actually given.
R37 | Medicine | is used in | Treatment | 1 : 0..* | Medicine from the farm catalogue.
R38 | Vaccination Record | causes | Health Case | 0..1 : 0..* | Post-vaccination reaction.
R39 | Health Case | proposes | Herd Exit Voucher | 0..1 : 0..1 | A case closed as died or missing proposes a death voucher.
R40 | Cattle (female) | shows | Estrus Event | 1 : 0..* | Observed heats.
R41 | Cattle (female) | is bred in | Breeding Event | 1 : 0..* | Natural service or AI.
R42 | Cattle (sire) | serves in | Breeding Event | 0..1 : 0..* | Farm bull used for natural service.
R43 | Estrus Event | leads to | Breeding Event | 0..1 : 0..* | Breeding after an observed heat.
R44 | Breeding Event | is checked by | Pregnancy Check | 0..1 : 0..* | Check of the latest breeding.
R45 | Pregnancy Check | confirms | Pregnancy Episode | 1 : 0..1 | A PREGNANT result opens the episode.
R46 | Cattle (female) | carries | Pregnancy Episode | 1 : 0..* | At most one open pregnancy at a time.
R47 | Pregnancy Episode | ends with | Calving Event | 0..1 : 0..1 | Calving closes the pregnancy.
R48 | Cattle (dam) | gives birth in | Calving Event | 1 : 0..* | Calvings of the dam.
R49 | Calving Event | produces | Calf Outcome | 1 : 1..* | One outcome per calf.
R50 | Farm | stocks | Feed Item | 1 : 0..* | Feed catalogue and stock.
R51 | Trading Partner | supplies | Feed Receipt | 1 : 0..* | Feed supplier.
R52 | Feed Receipt | includes | Feed Item | 0..* : 1..* | Received quantity, lot and price per feed.
R53 | Stock Adjustment | counts | Feed Item | 0..* : 1..* | Counted stock and difference per feed.
R54 | Ration | specifies | Feed Item | 0..* : 1..* | Planned kg per head and session.
R55 | Ration | applies to | Pen | 0..* : 1..* | One effective ration per pen and day.
R56 | Pen | is fed in | Feeding Record | 1 : 0..* | One record per pen, date and session.
R57 | Ration | plans | Feeding Record | 0..1 : 0..* | Ration effective at the feeding date.
R58 | Feeding Record | consumes | Feed Item | 0..* : 1..* | Actual kg deducted from stock.
R59 | Farm | publishes | Farm Announcement | 1 : 0..* | Announcements to farm members.
R60 | Farm Owner | authors | Farm Announcement | 0..1 : 0..* | Announcement written by the Owner.
R61 | Farm Manager | authors | Farm Announcement | 0..1 : 0..* | Announcement written by a Manager; each announcement has exactly one author.
R62 | Farm Announcement | is delivered to | Farm Manager | 0..* : 0..* | Owner announcements may address Managers.
R63 | Farm Announcement | is delivered to | Farm Technician | 0..* : 0..* | Owner and Manager announcements may address Technicians.
R64 | Farm | has | Subscription | 1 : 0..1 | One subscription per farm.
R65 | Platform Manager | maintains | Plan Price Version | 1 : 0..* | Plan prices are versioned by the PM.
R66 | Subscription | is paid by | Payment Request | 1 : 0..* | Each accepted request grants one service period.
R67 | Plan Price Version | prices | Payment Request | 1 : 0..* | Price version used by the request.
R68 | Bank Transfer Evidence | funds | Payment Request | 0..1 : 0..1 | One accepted transfer funds one request.
R69 | Platform Manager | reviews | Bank Transfer Evidence | 0..1 : 0..* | Manual reconciliation of transfers not matched automatically.
R70 | Farm | is attributed | Manual Finance Record | 0..1 : 0..* | General records have no farm.
R71 | Platform Manager | records | Manual Finance Record | 1 : 0..* | Basic platform cash records.
R72 | Farm Owner | submits | Support Ticket | 1 : 0..* | Requester of the ticket.
R73 | Farm | concerns | Support Ticket | 0..1 : 0..* | Account-level tickets have no farm.
R74 | Customer Support | is assigned | Support Ticket | 0..1 : 0..* | Responsible Customer Support.
R75 | Support Ticket | contains | Ticket Message | 1 : 0..* | Public replies and CS-only notes.
R76 | Customer Support | writes | Ticket Message | 0..1 : 0..* | PUBLIC replies and CS-only notes written by a CS; messages of the requester have no CS author. Kept per message because the ticket may be handed over to another CS.
R77 | Support Ticket | has | Ticket Resolution | 1 : 0..* | Resolution versions proposed by CS.
R78 | Customer Support | records | Ticket Resolution | 1 : 0..* | Each resolution version is recorded by the CS assigned at that time.
R79 | Support Ticket | raises | Internal Request | 1 : 0..* | Requests to a named PM or SA.
R80 | Customer Support | creates | Internal Request | 1 : 0..* | The CS assigned at that time creates the request to a named PM or SA.
R81 | Platform Manager | handles | Internal Request | 0..1 : 0..* | Named PM recipient.
R82 | System Admin | handles | Internal Request | 0..1 : 0..* | Named SA recipient; each request has exactly one recipient.
[/TABLE]
[P132 4] 3.2 Entity Descriptions
[P133 ] Each conceptual entity of 3.1 is described by its purpose, key business attributes, business identity and lifecycle. "Detail" items are parts, line items, histories or versions that belong to the entity; they are not separate entities in the conceptual diagram.
[P134 5] A. Users and Farm Membership
[P135 ] The six role entities below are overlapping specialisations of the User Account ("is a", 0..1 : 1, R01): the farm roles come from the Farm Membership of the person in each farm (Detail of Farm) and the staff roles from the platform role of the account. A specialisation exists while the account holds the role; a change between Manager and Technician in a farm is a change of the membership role.
[P136 ] Entity: User Account
[TABLE block 137]
Field | Value
Purpose | The one global account of a person, reused for every farm the person owns or joins and for any platform staff role. The six role entities of this area are overlapping specialisations of it ("is a"); the account accepts farm invitations, and the System Admin suspends or restores accounts.
Key business attributes | Full name, verified email, optional contact phone, account status, Owner registration completed (yes/no, time), last sign-in time, created time.
Business identity | Verified email is unique across all accounts (case-insensitive). The contact phone is never an identifier.
Status / lifecycle | ACTIVE ⇄ SUSPENDED (System Admin, with reason). Created ACTIVE at the first Google sign-in or when an email/password registration is verified. Never deleted while it has memberships, farms or history.
Detail – Sign-in Method | A way the account signs in: a Google identity or a BeefCare password. Attributes: Account, method type (GOOGLE / PASSWORD), Google subject (GOOGLE), password hash, failed-attempt count and locked-until time (PASSWORD), linked time, last used time. Identity: Google subject is unique and belongs to exactly one account; an account has at most one method of each type. Lifecycle: GOOGLE is linked at the first Google sign-in and never re-linked. PASSWORD is PENDING_VERIFICATION after email registration, ACTIVE after the email is verified, and is replaced on change or reset. Locked for 15 minutes after 5 failures (GBR-02).
[/TABLE]
[P139 ] Entity: Farm Owner
[TABLE block 140]
Field | Value
Purpose | The person who registers, owns and pays for a farm. Only the Owner manages members and roles, farm information, the subscription and payments, herd exits and production-purpose changes, and only the Owner submits support tickets and confirms their resolution.
Key business attributes | User account (full name, verified email, contact phone), owned farms, Owner registration completed (yes/no, time).
Business identity | The account that holds the OWNER membership of a farm. One person may own several farms and may also be a Manager or Technician of other farms.
Status / lifecycle | Follows the user account (ACTIVE ⇄ SUSPENDED by the System Admin). The OWNER membership is created with the farm and is never transferred.
[/TABLE]
[P142 ] Entity: Farm Manager
[TABLE block 143]
Field | Value
Purpose | A farm member who runs daily operations: records and corrects care, health, reproduction and feeding events, manages pens, catalogues, parameters, herd entry vouchers, cattle profiles and QR tags, and writes announcements to Farm Technicians.
Key business attributes | User account, farm, MANAGER membership (status, source invitation, joined time, last change with effective date and reason).
Business identity | Account + farm; a person has exactly one role in each farm.
Status / lifecycle | Membership ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE and MANAGER ⇄ TECHNICIAN, changed only by the Owner with an effective date and reason. Permissions are fixed by role.
[/TABLE]
[P145 ] Entity: Farm Technician
[TABLE block 146]
Field | Value
Purpose | A farm member with read-only access to farm records. A Technician may be named as observer or weigher in records entered by a Manager and receives announcements from the Owner and Managers.
Key business attributes | User account, farm, TECHNICIAN membership (status, source invitation, joined time, last change with effective date and reason).
Business identity | Account + farm; a person has exactly one role in each farm.
Status / lifecycle | Membership ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE and TECHNICIAN ⇄ MANAGER, changed only by the Owner with an effective date and reason.
[/TABLE]
[P148 ] Entity: Customer Support
[TABLE block 149]
Field | Value
Purpose | Platform staff who handles the support tickets of Farm Owners: public replies, CS-only notes, resolutions and internal requests to a named Platform Manager or System Admin. Customer Support never accesses cattle or care records.
Key business attributes | User account, platform role CS, access status, reason, changed by, changed time, assigned tickets.
Business identity | An account with an ACTIVE CS platform role; at most one ACTIVE platform role per account, independent of any farm.
Status / lifecycle | Access ACTIVE ⇄ REVOKED, changed only by a System Admin with a reason; role changes are kept as history. Tickets of a suspended CS return to the unassigned queue.
[/TABLE]
[P151 ] Entity: Platform Manager
[TABLE block 152]
Field | Value
Purpose | Platform staff who manages farm service (suspension and restoration), subscriptions, plan price versions, bank-transfer reconciliation and basic platform cash records, and handles internal requests addressed to them.
Key business attributes | User account, platform role PM, access status, reason, changed by, changed time.
Business identity | An account with an ACTIVE PM platform role; at most one ACTIVE platform role per account, independent of any farm.
Status / lifecycle | Access ACTIVE ⇄ REVOKED, changed only by a System Admin with a reason; role changes are kept as history.
[/TABLE]
[P154 ] Entity: System Admin
[TABLE block 155]
Field | Value
Purpose | Platform staff who suspends or restores user accounts, grants, changes, revokes or restores CS, PM and SA access, reviews the platform audit history and handles internal requests addressed to them.
Key business attributes | User account, platform role SA, access status, reason, changed by, changed time.
Business identity | An account with an ACTIVE SA platform role; at most one ACTIVE platform role per account.
Status / lifecycle | Access ACTIVE ⇄ REVOKED by another System Admin with a reason. At least one ACTIVE System Admin must always remain, and an SA cannot suspend their own account.
[/TABLE]
[P157 ] Entity: Farm
[TABLE block 158]
Field | Value
Purpose | An independent farm workspace (tenant). All cattle, pens, catalogues, members and the subscription of the farm belong to it.
Key business attributes | Farm name, province, commune, detailed address, contact person and phone, registration certificate information, expected herd size, main breeds, time zone, operating notes, service status (derived), suspension flag, created time.
Business identity | System-generated Farm ID. Farm names need not be unique. One registration request creates at most one farm.
Status / lifecycle | Service status PENDING_PAYMENT → ACTIVE ⇄ EXPIRED, and SUSPENDED whenever suspended by a PM (GBR-13). Never deleted once it has history.
Detail – Farm Membership | The link between an account and one farm, carrying the person's role in that farm. Attributes: Account, farm, role (OWNER / MANAGER / TECHNICIAN), status, source invitation, joined time, last change (effective date, reason). Identity: Account + farm is unique. Exactly one OWNER membership per farm. Lifecycle: ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE (Owner only). OWNER membership is created with the farm and never changes. MANAGER ⇄ TECHNICIAN by the Owner. Never deleted.
Detail – Farm Service Change | One suspension or restoration of a farm's service by a Platform Manager. Attributes: Farm, action (SUSPEND / RESTORE), status before, status after, reason, PM, time. Identity: Ordered by time within a farm. Lifecycle: Immutable once saved; the Owner is notified after each change.
Detail – Farm Parameter Version | One version of the farm's state and reminder parameters used by suggestions and reminders. Attributes: Farm, version number, life-stage ages (weaning age, young-stock age, adult age, in months), breeding conditions (minimum age, minimum weight), gestation length, pregnancy-check interval, re-check interval, calving preparation lead time, breeding window (start and end hours after estrus), estrus cycle length, postpartum interval, BCS scale, weighing interval, vaccination reminder lead time, target finishing weight (optional), source note, effective from, changed by. Identity: Version number is unique within the farm; exactly one version is effective at a time. Lifecycle: A new version applies to evaluations after its effective time; old versions are kept.
[/TABLE]
[P160 ] Entity: Farm Invitation
[TABLE block 161]
Field | Value
Purpose | An Owner's invitation for an email address to join a farm as Farm Manager or Farm Technician; it is accepted by the User Account whose verified email equals the invited email.
Key business attributes | Farm, recipient email, role, invited by, sent time, last sent time, validity (3, 7 or 14 days), expiry time, status, accepted account and membership.
Business identity | At most one PENDING invitation per farm + email (case-insensitive). The link token is single-use and stored only as a hash.
Status / lifecycle | PENDING → ACCEPTED, CANCELLED or EXPIRED (all final).
[/TABLE]
[P163 5] B. Farm Reference Data
[P164 ] Entity: Breed
[TABLE block 165]
Field | Value
Purpose | A cattle breed used by the farm (for example Zebu đỏ, Lai Sind, Brahman đỏ).
Key business attributes | Farm, code, name, group, description, status, number of cattle referencing it.
Business identity | Code is unique within the farm.
Status / lifecycle | ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE. A referenced breed is never deleted.
[/TABLE]
[P167 ] Entity: Production Purpose
[TABLE block 168]
Field | Value
Purpose | The purpose for which the farm raises a cattle (for example "Bò sinh sản", "Vỗ béo xuất chuồng", "Bò đực giống", "Chưa xác định").
Key business attributes | Farm, code, name, purpose type (BREEDING / FATTENING / SIRE / UNDECIDED), description, status.
Business identity | Code is unique within the farm. Several purposes may share one purpose type.
Status / lifecycle | ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE. Deactivation only blocks new selection; existing cattle keep their purpose. The purpose type is fixed once the purpose is referenced (BR-D32).
[/TABLE]
[P170 ] Entity: Vaccine
[TABLE block 171]
Field | Value
Purpose | A vaccine reference used in schedules and vaccination records.
Key business attributes | Farm, code, name, disease prevented, manufacturer, reference dose and route, booster interval (days), meat withdrawal period (days), status.
Business identity | Code is unique within the farm.
Status / lifecycle | ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE. A vaccine never referenced may be deleted.
[/TABLE]
[P173 ] Entity: Medicine
[TABLE block 174]
Field | Value
Purpose | A reference medicine used when recording treatments. Reference only: no stock, purchasing or prescription.
Key business attributes | Farm, code, name, reference dose, route, meat withdrawal period (days), notes, status.
Business identity | Code is unique within the farm.
Status / lifecycle | ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE. A referenced medicine is never deleted.
[/TABLE]
[P176 ] Entity: Pen
[TABLE block 177]
Field | Value
Purpose | A pen or care area of the farm in which cattle are placed.
Key business attributes | Farm, pen code, pen name, capacity, description, availability status, current headcount (derived), current criteria version.
Business identity | Pen code is unique within the farm.
Status / lifecycle | ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE (with effective date and reason). A pen with placement history is never deleted.
Detail – Pen Reception Criteria | One version of the advisory criteria a pen uses to check placements and to suggest pen moves. Attributes: Pen, version number, accepted purpose types, accepted life stages, accepted sex, minimum age (months), minimum weight (kg), effective from, changed by. Identity: Version number is unique within the pen. Lifecycle: A new version applies to future placements and suggestions only.
[/TABLE]
[P179 5] C. Cattle and Herd Movement
[P180 ] Entity: Cattle
[TABLE block 181]
Field | Value
Purpose | One animal of the farm, followed from herd entry to herd exit. Its activity timeline is a derived view of its source records (GBR-37).
Key business attributes | Internal Cattle ID, display code, ear tag number, name, sex, breed, birth date or estimated birth month with accuracy (EXACT / ESTIMATED / UNKNOWN), colour and markings, photo, production purpose, life stage (UNWEANED_CALF / WEANED_CALF / YOUNG_STOCK / ADULT), herd status (ACTIVE / EXITED / VOIDED), exit type and date or void reason, dam, sire (internal cattle or external sire/semen code). Derived: exit pending, current pen, herd group, health state (HEALTHY / MONITORING / UNDER_TREATMENT), meat withdrawal end date, reproductive status (AWAITING_BREEDING / IN_ESTRUS / BRED / PREGNANT / POST_CALVING, or not applicable), breeding eligibility, latest weight, ADG and projected finishing date (FATTENING), reproduction indicators (females). The status dimensions are summarised in 3.4.1.
Business identity | Internal Cattle ID is system-generated, immutable and never reused. Display code is unique within the farm.
Status / lifecycle | Herd status ACTIVE → EXITED (confirmed exit) → ACTIVE (voided exit); ACTIVE → VOIDED (herd entry voided; final). An EXITED or VOIDED cattle is read-only, and a cattle on a PENDING death voucher accepts no new event (GBR-26, 3.4.2).
Detail – Cattle Profile Change | The history of one change of production purpose, life stage, ear tag number or descriptive data. Attributes: Cattle, field group, old value, new value, effective date, reason, changed by, changed time. Identity: Ordered by effective date within a cattle and field. Lifecycle: Immutable once saved.
Detail – Pen Assignment | One period during which a cattle is placed in a pen. Attributes: Cattle, pen, from date, to date, source (HERD_ENTRY / MOVE / CORRECTION), reason, criteria version checked, reception-check result and warning reason, moved by, recorded time. Identity: At most one open assignment per cattle; periods never overlap. Lifecycle: OPEN → CLOSED (move or exit). A closed assignment is never edited.
[/TABLE]
[P183 ] Entity: Cattle QR Tag
[TABLE block 184]
Field | Value
Purpose | A printed QR tag that identifies a cattle in the field.
Key business attributes | Cattle, tag code, status, issued by, issued time, replacement reason (LOST / DAMAGED / OTHER), note, replaced-by tag.
Business identity | Tag code is unique; at most one ACTIVE tag per cattle.
Status / lifecycle | ACTIVE → REPLACED (new tag issued) or RETIRED (herd entry voided); never reactivated. The tag of an EXITED cattle stays ACTIVE so that a scan by a farm member opens its read-only profile.
[/TABLE]
[P186 ] Entity: Herd Entry Voucher
[TABLE block 187]
Field | Value
Purpose | One receipt of cattle into the farm (phiếu nhập), by purchase or as farm-born calves, grouping one or more herd entries with their value and the payments to the seller.
Key business attributes | Voucher code, entry type (PURCHASE / FARM_BORN), entry date, seller (trading partner), quarantine certificate number (PURCHASE, optional), vehicle plate, note, pricing basis (PER_KG / PER_HEAD), cattle amount, transport fee, total payable, amount paid, outstanding amount, payment due date, attachments, created by, posted by and time, void reason, voided by and time. Derived: payment status (UNPAID / PARTLY_PAID / PAID), number of cattle, total weight.
Business identity | Code PN-YYYY-nnnn, unique within the farm and never reused (BR-D34).
Status / lifecycle | DRAFT → POSTED → VOIDED; a DRAFT can be discarded. Only a DRAFT is edited; posting creates the cattle of all lines in one transaction (GBR-90). A FARM_BORN voucher has no amounts (GBR-93).
Detail – Herd Entry | How and when one cattle entered the herd; one line of a herd entry voucher. Attributes: Herd entry voucher, line number, cattle, source (EXTERNAL / FARM_BORN), entry date (from the voucher; the birth date for a farm-born calf), entry weight with basis, unit price, line amount, quarantine until (EXTERNAL, optional), calf outcome (FARM_BORN), initial pen, status. Identity: Exactly one VALID herd entry per cattle; a calf outcome is registered at most once; line number unique within the voucher. Lifecycle: Created VALID when its voucher is posted. VALID → VOID for one line (UC-FE06-03) or for all lines with the voucher (UC-FE06-05), only when the cattle has no other event (GBR-33). Corrections create Record Revisions.
Detail – Pre-entry History Declaration | Declared history of a bought-in cattle before entry, per category. Attributes: Herd entry, category (VACCINATION / DISEASE / REPRODUCTION), status (KNOWN / NONE / UNKNOWN), details (vaccine, date, disease, description; for REPRODUCTION: number of earlier calvings, pregnant at entry, estimated months pregnant), information source. Identity: One declaration per herd entry and category, with item lines for KNOWN. Lifecycle: Corrected only through UC-FE06-03.
[/TABLE]
[P189 ] Entity: Herd Exit Voucher
[TABLE block 190]
Field | Value
Purpose | One departure of cattle from the farm (phiếu xuất) – sale, cull, death or missing, or other – grouping herd exits with their value and the collections from the buyer; confirmed by the Owner.
Key business attributes | Voucher code, exit type (SALE / CULL / DEATH / OTHER), exit date, buyer (trading partner) or destination, transport quarantine certificate, vehicle plate, reason/notes, loss kind (DIED / MISSING), cause and carcass disposal (DEATH), source health case, pricing basis, cattle amount, deductions, total receivable, amount collected, outstanding amount, collection due date, attachments, reported by, confirmed by and time, dismiss or void reason, actor and time.
Business identity | Code PX-YYYY-nnnn, unique within the farm and never reused (BR-D34).
Status / lifecycle | DRAFT → CONFIRMED → VOIDED (Owner); PENDING (created from a health case closed as DIED or MISSING) → CONFIRMED or DISMISSED; a DRAFT can be discarded (GBR-94 to GBR-96). A DEATH voucher has no amounts.
Detail – Herd Exit | The end of one cattle's stay in the herd; one line of a herd exit voucher. Attributes: Herd exit voucher, line number, cattle, exit weight with basis (default the latest weight), unit price, line amount, pre-confirmation check results (withdrawal, open cases, future doses and reminders, pregnancy, draft calves), last pen, status. Identity: A cattle is on at most one DRAFT, PENDING or CONFIRMED exit line at a time (BR-D11, BR-D35). Lifecycle: Follows its voucher: DRAFT or PENDING → CONFIRMED (cattle EXITED) or removed / DISMISSED; CONFIRMED → VOIDED with the voucher (GBR-95).
[/TABLE]
[P192 ] Entity: Trading Partner
[TABLE block 193]
Field | Value
Purpose | A seller, buyer, market, slaughterhouse or feed supplier the farm deals with. Partners have no BeefCare account.
Key business attributes | Name, partner roles (SELLER / BUYER / SUPPLIER), contact phone, area or address, note, status.
Business identity | Name is unique within the farm (case-insensitive).
Status / lifecycle | ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE; never deleted once referenced by a voucher (GBR-97).
[/TABLE]
[P195 ] Entity: Voucher Payment
[TABLE block 196]
Field | Value
Purpose | One payment made to a seller or feed supplier, or one collection received from a buyer, recorded against a voucher.
Key business attributes | Voucher (herd entry voucher, herd exit voucher or feed receipt), direction (PAY / COLLECT), payment date, amount, method (CASH / BANK_TRANSFER), reference or transfer content, attachment, note, recorded by and time, void reason, voided by and time.
Business identity | Ordered by payment date within the voucher; the sum of RECORDED payments never exceeds the voucher total (BR-D39).
Status / lifecycle | RECORDED → VOIDED (with reason; a wrong payment is voided and recorded again). Farm bookkeeping only: not an invoice or tax document and not part of platform finance (GBR-91).
[/TABLE]
[P198 5] D. Care and Health
[P199 ] Entity: Growth Assessment
[TABLE block 200]
Field | Value
Purpose | One measurement of weight and/or body condition score of a cattle.
Key business attributes | Cattle, measured time, weight (kg), method (ELECTRONIC_SCALE / TAPE / OTHER_ESTIMATE), heart girth and body length (cm, TAPE), basis (MEASURED / ESTIMATED, derived from the method), BCS and scale version, measured by, note, entry time, status.
Business identity | No natural key; at least one of weight or BCS is present.
Status / lifecycle | VALID → EXCLUDED (outlier) or VOIDED, with reason; a correction keeps the record VALID with a "Corrected" marker and a Record Revision (GBR-40).
[/TABLE]
[P202 ] Entity: Vaccination Schedule
[TABLE block 203]
Field | Value
Purpose | A plan to vaccinate a defined set of cattle with one vaccine.
Key business attributes | Farm, name, vaccine, target selection (pens, herd groups, life stages, individual cattle), start date, number of doses, days between doses, booster interval (months), status, created by.
Business identity | System-generated ID; name is unique among ACTIVE schedules of the farm.
Status / lifecycle | ACTIVE → STOPPED. Changes affect only doses not yet given.
Detail – Scheduled Dose | One planned dose of one cattle in a schedule. Attributes: Schedule, cattle, dose number (or booster), due date, status (PLANNED / DONE / DEFERRED / CANCELLED), deferral reason, new expected date, fulfilling vaccination record. Identity: Schedule + cattle + dose number is unique. Lifecycle: PLANNED → DONE, DEFERRED (→ PLANNED again with new date, or DONE) or CANCELLED.
[/TABLE]
[P205 ] Entity: Vaccination Record
[TABLE block 206]
Field | Value
Purpose | One vaccination actually given to a cattle, or declared from its history.
Key business attributes | Cattle, vaccine (and name snapshot), administered time, batch number, dose, route, withdrawal days and withdrawal end date (derived, ON_FARM only), vaccinator, source (ON_FARM / HISTORICAL), information source, scheduled dose, entry time, status.
Business identity | No natural key; one record per cattle per administration.
Status / lifecycle | VALID → VOIDED, with reason; a correction keeps the record VALID with a "Corrected" marker and a Record Revision (GBR-44).
[/TABLE]
[P208 ] Entity: Health Case
[TABLE block 209]
Field | Value
Purpose | One health problem of a cattle, grouping its observations and treatments.
Key business attributes | Case code, cattle, opened time, main signs, suspected diagnosis (entered by the user), suspected contagious flag, severity (MILD / MODERATE / SEVERE), status (OPEN / CHRONIC / CLOSED), chronic since, outcome (RECOVERED / DIED / MISSING / CLOSED_BY_EXIT), closed time, closed by, linked vaccination (reaction).
Business identity | Case code is unique within the farm.
Status / lifecycle | OPEN ⇄ CHRONIC (long-term monitoring; a new treatment returns the case to OPEN) → CLOSED with an outcome (GBR-45). DIED or MISSING creates a PENDING herd exit.
Detail – Health Observation | The opening observation or a follow-up observation of a health case. Attributes: Health case, observed time, body temperature, signs or progress description, progress (IMPROVED / UNCHANGED / WORSENED, for follow-ups), observer, recorded by, entry time. Identity: No natural key. Lifecycle: VALID → VOIDED (follow-ups only); a correction keeps it VALID with a "Corrected" marker and a Record Revision (UC-FE10-05). An observation is not a diagnosis.
[/TABLE]
[P211 ] Entity: Treatment
[TABLE block 212]
Field | Value
Purpose | One treatment actually given within a health case.
Key business attributes | Health case, medicine (and name snapshot), dose, route, administered time (first administration), course days (default 1), withdrawal days, withdrawal end date (derived), performer, veterinary advice, recorded by, entry time.
Business identity | No natural key; one record per administration or per daily course of the same medicine, dose and route.
Status / lifecycle | VALID → VOIDED, with reason; a correction keeps the record VALID with a "Corrected" marker and a Record Revision (UC-FE10-05). A change of medicine, dose or route given later is recorded as a new treatment.
[/TABLE]
[P214 5] E. Reproduction
[P215 ] Entity: Estrus Event
[TABLE block 216]
Field | Value
Purpose | An observed estrus of a female cattle.
Key business attributes | Female, observed time, observer, signs (STANDING / MOUNTING / BELLOWING / MUCUS / SWOLLEN_VULVA), description, recorded by, entry time, status.
Business identity | No natural key; a possible duplicate (same female within 12 hours) is flagged.
Status / lifecycle | VALID → VOIDED (Manager, with reason); a correction keeps the record VALID with a "Corrected" marker.
[/TABLE]
[P218 ] Entity: Breeding Event
[TABLE block 219]
Field | Value
Purpose | An actual breeding (natural service or artificial insemination) of a female.
Key business attributes | Female, bred time, data source, method (NATURAL / AI), sire source (INTERNAL / EXTERNAL), internal sire, sire breed, semen straw/batch code, semen supplier, performer, linked estrus, tentative expected calving date, note, recorded by, status.
Business identity | No natural key. An internal sire is a different male cattle of the farm.
Status / lifecycle | VALID → VOIDED; a correction keeps the record VALID with a "Corrected" marker. Sets the female to BRED.
[/TABLE]
[P221 ] Entity: Pregnancy Check
[TABLE block 222]
Field | Value
Purpose | An actual pregnancy examination result.
Key business attributes | Female, checked time, data source, result (PREGNANT / NOT_PREGNANT / INCONCLUSIVE), method (ULTRASOUND / RECTAL / OTHER), estimated months pregnant, examiner, linked breeding, note, recorded by, status.
Business identity | No natural key.
Status / lifecycle | VALID → VOIDED; a correction keeps the record VALID with a "Corrected" marker. A valid PREGNANT result opens or confirms an episode.
[/TABLE]
[P224 ] Entity: Pregnancy Episode
[TABLE block 225]
Field | Value
Purpose | One confirmed pregnancy of a female, from the confirming check to calving or loss.
Key business attributes | Female, pregnancy number (parity sequence), confirming check, linked breeding, expected calving date and basis, status, closed time, closing reason.
Business identity | At most one OPEN episode per female.
Status / lifecycle | OPEN → CALVED, LOST or DAM_EXITED; VOIDED when its confirming check is voided without dependencies (3.4.5).
Detail – Pregnancy Update | An observation, complication or loss recorded during a pregnancy. Attributes: Episode, occurred time, data source, update type (OBSERVATION / COMPLICATION / LOSS), details, linked health case, recorded by, status. Identity: No natural key. Lifecycle: VALID → VOIDED; a correction keeps it VALID with a "Corrected" marker. A LOSS closes the episode as LOST.
[/TABLE]
[P227 ] Entity: Calving Event
[TABLE block 228]
Field | Value
Purpose | An actual birth of a dam, saved with all its calf outcomes.
Key business attributes | Dam, linked episode (optional), birth time, data source, birth attendant, calving course (NORMAL / ASSISTED / CAESAREAN), dam condition (GOOD / NEEDS_MONITORING / CRITICAL), retained placenta, recorded by, status. Derived: number of calves (from the calf outcomes).
Business identity | One calving per dam per birth; a second calving within 30 days is flagged.
Status / lifecycle | VALID → VOIDED; a correction keeps the record VALID with a "Corrected" marker. Saving closes the episode as CALVED.
[/TABLE]
[P230 ] Entity: Calf Outcome
[TABLE block 231]
Field | Value
Purpose | The result for one calf of a calving, including the draft data of a live calf.
Key business attributes | Calving, calf number, outcome (LIVE / STILLBORN), sex (MALE / FEMALE; UNKNOWN only for STILLBORN), birth weight, unit and basis, ear-tag/QR code, proposed pen, breed, registration choice, registration state, registered cattle.
Business identity | Calf number is unique within the calving; ear-tag/QR code is unique within the farm.
Status / lifecycle | LIVE: PENDING_CONFIRMATION → REGISTERED, or DIED_BEFORE_REGISTRATION; Register now goes directly to REGISTERED. STILLBORN is never registered.
[/TABLE]
[P233 5] F. Feed Management
[P234 ] Entity: Feed Item
[TABLE block 235]
Field | Value
Purpose | A feed or supplement the farm stocks and feeds.
Key business attributes | Code, name, feed group (GREEN_ROUGHAGE / DRY_ROUGHAGE / CONCENTRATE / TMR / MINERAL_ADDITIVE / CALF_FEED), stock unit (kg), purchase unit and kg per purchase unit, minimum stock level, nutrition note, status. Derived: current stock, weighted average cost, stock value, average daily use (7 days), days of cover.
Business identity | Code TA-nn, unique within the farm.
Status / lifecycle | ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE; code and stock unit cannot change after the first stock movement; a feed with movements is never deleted (GBR-98).
[/TABLE]
[P237 ] Entity: Feed Receipt
[TABLE block 238]
Field | Value
Purpose | One delivery of feed from a supplier (phiếu nhập kho) that adds stock.
Key business attributes | Receipt code, receipt date, supplier (trading partner), invoice or delivery-note number, receiver, storage location, note, goods amount, transport fee, total payable, amount paid, outstanding amount, payment due date, attachments, posted by and time, void reason, voided by and time.
Business identity | Code PNK-YYYY-nnnn, unique within the farm (BR-D34).
Status / lifecycle | DRAFT → POSTED → VOIDED; a DRAFT changes no stock; a POSTED receipt is voided only while the stock still covers every line (GBR-99).
Detail – Feed Receipt Line | One feed received on a feed receipt. Attributes: Feed receipt, line number, feed item, lot number, expiry date, quantity in the purchase unit, kg per purchase unit, quantity in kg, unit price, line amount. Identity: Line number unique within the receipt. Lifecycle: Follows its receipt; produces one RECEIPT stock movement when posted.
[/TABLE]
[P240 ] Entity: Stock Adjustment
[TABLE block 241]
Field | Value
Purpose | A stock count or loss record (phiếu điều chỉnh kho) that aligns system stock with counted stock.
Key business attributes | Adjustment code, count date, adjustment type (PERIODIC_COUNT / LOSS / SPOILAGE / OTHER), counted by, attachments, total difference in kg and value, posted by and time.
Business identity | Code ĐCK-YYYY-nnnn, unique within the farm (BR-D34).
Status / lifecycle | DRAFT → POSTED. A posted adjustment is immutable; a mistake is reversed by a new adjustment (GBR-100).
Detail – Stock Adjustment Line | The count result of one feed item on a stock adjustment. Attributes: Stock adjustment, feed item, system stock, counted stock, difference, reason, value at weighted average cost. Identity: One line per feed item per adjustment; a reason is required when the difference is not zero. Lifecycle: Follows its adjustment; a non-zero line produces one ADJUSTMENT stock movement.
[/TABLE]
[P243 ] Entity: Ration
[TABLE block 244]
Field | Value
Purpose | A planned feeding (khẩu phần) for one or more pens, kept as versions.
Key business attributes | Ration code, name, version number, herd group, pens, sessions per day and times, effective from, effective to, basis or source, created by and time. Derived: kg and estimated cost per head per day, planned daily quantity and cost.
Business identity | Code + version unique within the farm; at most one version is effective per pen and date (BR-D38).
Status / lifecycle | SCHEDULED → EFFECTIVE → ENDED. Saving a change creates a new version that ends the previous one the day before its effective date; a SCHEDULED version can be cancelled (GBR-101).
Detail – Ration Component | The quantity of one feed in one ration version. Attributes: Ration version, feed item, kg per head for each session, kg per head per day. Identity: One component per feed item per ration version. Lifecycle: Immutable after the version takes effect.
[/TABLE]
[P246 ] Entity: Feeding Record
[TABLE block 247]
Field | Value
Purpose | One feeding session of one pen on one date.
Key business attributes | Pen, feeding date, session (MORNING / AFTERNOON / EVENING), feeding time, ration version, headcount at feeding, planned total kg, actual total kg, leftover kg, fed by (member), recorded by and time, note, status.
Business identity | Pen + date + session unique among VALID records (BR-D37).
Status / lifecycle | VALID → VOIDED (with reason). A correction keeps the record VALID with a "Corrected" marker and saves a Record Revision; stock is adjusted by the difference (GBR-102).
Detail – Feeding Record Line | The planned and actual quantity of one feed in one feeding record. Attributes: Feeding record, feed item, planned kg, actual kg, in-ration flag, unit cost and cost at weighted average cost. Identity: One line per feed item per feeding record. Lifecycle: Follows its record; produces one FEEDING stock movement (and a correction movement when changed).
[/TABLE]
[P249 5] G. Farm Announcements
[P250 ] Entity: Farm Announcement
[TABLE block 251]
Field | Value
Purpose | An administrative message from the Owner or a Manager to members of the farm.
Key business attributes | Farm, author membership, intended audience, state, current published version, created time.
Business identity | No natural key; one farm and one author.
Status / lifecycle | DRAFT → PUBLISHED. Published content is corrected only by new versions.
Detail – Announcement Version | One immutable published content version. Attributes: Announcement, version number, title, body, correction reason, published by, published time. Identity: Version number is unique within the announcement. Lifecycle: Immutable once published.
Detail – Announcement Delivery | The delivery of one version to one recipient, fixed at publication. Attributes: Version, recipient membership, delivered time, read time. Identity: Version + recipient is unique. Lifecycle: DELIVERED → READ.
[/TABLE]
[P253 5] H. Subscription and Platform Finance
[P254 ] Entity: Plan Price Version
[TABLE block 255]
Field | Value
Purpose | An immutable published price list of the single BeefCare plan.
Key business attributes | Version number, price for 1, 2 and 3 years (VND), supported terms, effective from, published by, published time.
Business identity | Version number is unique; exactly one version is effective at any instant.
Status / lifecycle | SCHEDULED → EFFECTIVE → SUPERSEDED, or SCHEDULED → WITHDRAWN before it takes effect; never edited after publication.
[/TABLE]
[P257 ] Entity: Subscription
[TABLE block 258]
Field | Value
Purpose | The billing relationship of one farm with BeefCare and its current coverage.
Key business attributes | Farm, current period (derived), future periods (derived), latest confirmed end, renewal opening date (derived), anniversary anchor.
Business identity | At most one subscription per farm.
Status / lifecycle | Created with the first payment request; coverage is derived from purchased periods.
[/TABLE]
[P260 ] Entity: Payment Request
[TABLE block 261]
Field | Value
Purpose | One Owner-initiated quote for a 1-, 2- or 3-year purchase with immutable transfer instructions.
Key business attributes | Payment reference (e.g. BCF7K2Q9), farm, subscription, requested by, price version, term (years), amount (VND), receiving bank, account number and account name, created time, expiry time (created + 24 h), status, bank-paid time, received time, confirmed time, confirmation source (SEPAY / PM).
Business identity | Payment reference is unique; at most one unexpired PENDING request per farm.
Status / lifecycle | PENDING → PAID or EXPIRED; EXPIRED → PAID only for evidence paid within the original window.
Detail – Purchased Period | The service period granted by one accepted payment. Attributes: Payment request, subscription, start, end, term. Identity: One period per PAID payment request. Lifecycle: Immutable once created.
[/TABLE]
[P263 ] Entity: Bank Transfer Evidence
[TABLE block 264]
Field | Value
Purpose | An authenticated bank transaction received from SePay (or looked up by the PM), kept whether or not it matches a payment.
Key business attributes | Provider, environment, account namespace, provider transaction ID, bank, receiving account, direction, amount, transfer content, extracted payment code, bank reference, bank-paid time, first received time, delivery attempts, match state, review state, allocated payment, handling lock.
Business identity | Provider + environment + namespace + provider transaction ID is unique.
Status / lifecycle | RECEIVED → MATCHED (allocated, terminal) or UNMATCHED / NEEDS_REVIEW → ACCEPTED, HELD, REJECTED or LOCKED_FOR_RETURN.
Detail – Evidence Review | A PM decision or handling event on bank evidence. Attributes: Evidence, related payment request, decision (ACCEPT / HOLD / REJECT / LOCK / UNLOCK / RETURN_VERIFIED), reason, evidence references, PM, time. Identity: One entry per committed decision. Lifecycle: Immutable history; the latest entry gives the current review state.
[/TABLE]
[P266 ] Entity: Manual Finance Record
[TABLE block 267]
Field | Value
Purpose | Platform income or expense other than subscription receipts, recorded by the PM.
Key business attributes | Record code (FR-nnnn), type (INCOME / EXPENSE), category, amount (VND), occurrence date, scope (GENERAL / FARM) and farm, document number, description, source (MANUAL / BANK_TRANSACTION) and bank transaction reference, status, created/posted/voided by and time, void reason, replacement record.
Business identity | Record code is unique; a bank transaction is recognized at most once (GBR-84).
Status / lifecycle | DRAFT → POSTED → VOID; a DRAFT may be discarded.
[/TABLE]
[P269 5] I. Customer Support
[P270 ] Entity: Support Ticket
[TABLE block 271]

Purpose | An Owner's support case about their account or one owned farm.
Key business attributes | Ticket code (TK-nnnn), requester, related farm (optional), category, priority, subject, description, status, assigned CS, latest resolution version, current acknowledgment, created and updated time.
Business identity | Ticket code is unique; the requester never changes.
Status / lifecycle | OPEN → IN_PROGRESS ⇄ WAITING_CUSTOMER → RESOLVED → CLOSED; RESOLVED → OPEN on reopen (GBR-78).
Detail – Ticket History Event | The immutable chronology of assignment and status changes of a ticket and its internal requests. Attributes: Ticket, internal request (optional), event type, before/after status or assignee, actor and role, reason, time. Identity: One event per committed change. Lifecycle: Append-only.

Status / lifecycle | Immutable; no edit or delete.

[P276 ] Entity: Ticket Resolution

Key business attributes | Ticket, version number, resolution text, resolved by, resolved time.
Business identity | Version number is unique within the ticket.
Status / lifecycle | Immutable; a later resolution supersedes it for closure.
Detail – Resolution Acknowledgment | The Owner's explicit confirmation that a resolution version solved the issue. Attributes: Ticket, resolution version, requester, confirmed time, optional comment, reviewed by CS and time. Identity: One effective acknowledgment per resolution version. [P279 ] Entity: Internal Request

Business identity | At most one OPEN request per ticket and target role.
Status / lifecycle | OPEN → DONE or CANCELLED.
[/TABLE]
[P282 5] J. Supporting Records (not drawn in the conceptual diagram)
[P283 ] These records are created by the system from the business entities above for reminders, traceability, notification or reporting. They are not managed as business entities by users and are not drawn in the conceptual diagram.
[P284 ] Entity: Reminder
[TABLE block 285]
Field | Value
Purpose | A system-generated reminder or suggestion derived from a source record and a parameter (rules REM-01 to REM-13).
Key business attributes | Farm, cattle, rule, source record, parameter version, due time or window, basis label (TENTATIVE / CONFIRMED where relevant), audience, lifecycle.
Business identity | Rule + source record + occurrence key is unique.
Status / lifecycle | ACTIVE → RESOLVED, CANCELLED or SUPERSEDED.
[/TABLE]
[P287 ] Entity: Audit Log Entry
[TABLE block 288]
Field | Value
Purpose | An append-only record of one important committed change on the platform, reviewed centrally by the System Admin.
Key business attributes | Time, actor account and role (empty for system actions), farm (when applicable), action code, target type and ID, allowlisted before/after values, correlation ID.
Business identity | One entry per committed change; system-generated ID.
Status / lifecycle | No status. Written in the same transaction as the change; never edited or deleted.
[/TABLE]
[P290 ] Entity: Record Revision
[TABLE block 291]
Field | Value
Purpose | The immutable before/after history of a correction or void of a correctable record (herd entry, growth assessment, vaccination record, reproductive records).
Key business attributes | Record type and ID, action (CORRECT / EXCLUDE / VOID), reason, before values, after values, changed by, changed time.
Business identity | One revision per accepted correction command.
Status / lifecycle | Immutable.
[/TABLE]
[P293 ] Entity: Notification
[TABLE block 294]
Field | Value
Purpose | An in-app notice to one recipient about one business target.
Key business attributes | Recipient account, category (subscription, support, reconciliation, announcement), target type and ID, title, summary, created time, read time.
Business identity | Recipient + source event is unique.
Status / lifecycle | UNREAD → READ.
[/TABLE]
[P296 ] Entity: Stock Movement
[TABLE block 297]
Field | Value
Purpose | The append-only ledger of every stock change of a feed item.
Key business attributes | Feed item, movement time, type (RECEIPT / RECEIPT_VOID / ADJUSTMENT / FEEDING / FEEDING_CORRECTION / FEEDING_VOID), quantity in kg (+/−), unit cost, value, source line, balance after.
Business identity | Ordered by movement time within a feed item.
Status / lifecycle | Immutable. Current stock = sum of movements and is never negative (BR-D36).
[/TABLE]
[P299 ] Entity: Subscription Receipt
[TABLE block 300]
Field | Value
Purpose | A derived, read-only receipt of one accepted PAID payment (not stored as an editable record).
Key business attributes | Receipt code (RC-yy-nnnn), payment reference, farm, term, price version, amount, bank-paid time, confirmed time, purchased period, evidence reference.
Business identity | One receipt per PAID payment request.
Status / lifecycle | No lifecycle; exists while its payment is PAID.
[/TABLE]
[P302 ] Entity: Cash Report Snapshot
[TABLE block 303]
Field | Value
Purpose | One immutable result of a platform cash report run, used for display and export.
Key business attributes | Snapshot code, requested by, generated time, period, farm filter, source filter, rows with source references and versions, totals (receipts, other income, expense, net) per scope.
Business identity | Snapshot code is unique.
Status / lifecycle | AVAILABLE → EXPIRED (after the retention time); a refresh creates a new snapshot.
[/TABLE]
[P305 ] Entity: Public Landing Content
[TABLE block 306]
Field | Value
Purpose | The published static content of the public landing page (introduction, features, how it works, FAQ, contact).
Key business attributes | Content release reference, sections, FAQ items, contact email and phone; prices are read from the effective Plan Price Version.
Business identity | One published release at a time.
Status / lifecycle | Changed by deployment; no end-user content management.
[/TABLE]
[P308 4] 3.3 Data Business Rules
[TABLE block 309]
# | Rule | Description | Enforced On
BR-D01 | Farm-scoped data | Every farm-owned record (pen, catalogue item, parameter version, cattle, event, announcement, reminder) belongs to exactly one farm, and every link between records stays within that farm. | Every save and every read
BR-D02 | Unique account email | No two accounts share the same verified email (case-insensitive). A Google identity or password with that email belongs to the same account. | Registration, sign-in, email verification
BR-D03 | Google identity binding | A Google subject belongs to exactly one account and is never re-linked to another account. | Google sign-in
BR-D04 | Membership uniqueness | One membership per account and farm; exactly one OWNER membership per farm. | Farm registration, invitation acceptance, role change
BR-D05 | Single pending invitation | At most one PENDING invitation per farm and recipient email. | Invitation send
BR-D06 | Platform role uniqueness | At most one ACTIVE platform role per account; at least one ACTIVE System Admin always exists. | Staff access change, account suspension
BR-D07 | Permanent cattle identity | Internal Cattle ID and display code are generated once, never change and are never reused, even after exit or a voided herd entry. | Herd entry, QR replacement, profile update
BR-D08 | Tag uniqueness | QR tag code is unique; at most one ACTIVE tag per cattle. Ear tag number is unique among ACTIVE cattle of the farm. A draft calf's tag code must not be used by any tag of the farm. | QR issue/replace, profile update, herd entry, calving
BR-D09 | Single current pen | At most one open pen assignment per cattle; periods of one cattle never overlap; open assignments of a pen never exceed its capacity. | Herd entry, pen move, pen update, exit, exit void
BR-D10 | One entry per cattle | Exactly one herd entry per cattle; a live calf outcome is linked to at most one cattle. | Herd entry, draft calf confirmation
BR-D11 | One active exit | At most one PENDING or CONFIRMED herd exit per cattle. | Exit voucher confirmation, pending voucher creation, exit void
BR-D12 | Unique codes | Pen code and catalogue codes (breed, vaccine, medicine, production purpose) are unique within the farm. | Create and update
BR-D13 | No deletion of referenced data | Farms, accounts, memberships, cattle with events, pens with history and referenced catalogue items are never deleted; they are deactivated or kept as history. | Delete and deactivate actions
BR-D14 | Event time integrity | Every event stores occurrence time separately from entry time. Occurrence time is not in the future and not before the herd entry, except HISTORICAL records declared at entry. | Every event save and correction
BR-D15 | Corrections keep history | Corrections, exclusions and voids never overwrite the original values; a Record Revision with before/after values, reason, actor and time is saved with the change. | Correction and void UCs
BR-D16 | Growth content | A growth assessment has a weight, a BCS or both; a weight is greater than 0 kg and has a method and basis. | Growth record and correction
BR-D17 | Dose uniqueness | One scheduled dose per schedule, cattle and dose number; a dose is fulfilled by at most one ON_FARM vaccination record. | Schedule save, vaccination record
BR-D18 | Single open pregnancy | At most one OPEN pregnancy episode per female; an episode is opened only by a valid PREGNANT check. | Pregnancy check, update, calving, corrections
BR-D19 | Calving content | A calving has at least one calf outcome; calf numbers are unique within the calving. | Calving record and correction
BR-D20 | Draft and exited cattle locks | A draft calf (PENDING_CONFIRMATION) and an EXITED cattle accept no new events or changes, except confirmation of the draft and voiding of the exit. | Every event save
BR-D21 | Announcement immutability | Published versions are immutable; one delivery per version and recipient. | Publish, correction
BR-D22 | Reminder and notice uniqueness | One reminder per rule, source record and occurrence key; one notification per recipient and source event. | JOB-01, JOB-07
BR-D23 | Subscription per farm | At most one subscription per farm and at most one unexpired PENDING payment request per farm; payment references are unique. | Payment request
BR-D24 | Evidence uniqueness and allocation | Bank evidence is unique by provider, environment, namespace and transaction ID; one evidence funds at most one payment request; a PAID request has exactly one evidence and one purchased period. | Webhook receipt, PM decision, confirmation
BR-D25 | Immutable periods and prices | Purchased periods and published plan price versions are never edited; each price version has exactly three positive whole-VND prices for 1, 2 and 3 years. | Confirmation, price publication
BR-D26 | Ticket integrity | The requester of a ticket never changes; at most one OPEN internal request per ticket and target role; resolution versions are unique and immutable. | Ticket and request commands
BR-D27 | Finance record content | Manual amounts are positive whole VND; FARM scope requires a farm, GENERAL scope has none; POSTED records are immutable. | Finance create, edit, post, void
BR-D28 | Single recognition of money | A bank transaction is recognized at most once: as a subscription receipt or as posted manual income, never both. | Payment acceptance, manual record create/edit/post
BR-D29 | Append-only audit | Audit entries are written in the same transaction as the change and are never edited or deleted. | Every audited change
BR-D30 | Immutable snapshots | A cash report snapshot keeps its criteria, source versions, rows and totals unchanged. | Report run and export
BR-D31 | Optimistic version | Updatable records carry a version; an update with an outdated version is rejected (GBR-89). | Every update and correction
BR-D32 | Fixed purpose type | Every production purpose has exactly one purpose type (BREEDING, FATTENING, SIRE, UNDECIDED); it cannot change once the purpose is referenced by a cattle or a pen criteria version. | Purpose create and update
BR-D33 | Health case status | A CLOSED case has exactly one outcome and a closing time not before its last observation or treatment; OPEN and CHRONIC cases have no outcome. | Case update, close, treatment, herd exit
BR-D34 | Unique voucher codes | Herd entry voucher (PN-YYYY-nnnn), herd exit voucher (PX-YYYY-nnnn), feed receipt (PNK-YYYY-nnnn) and stock adjustment (ĐCK-YYYY-nnnn) codes are unique within the farm, generated in sequence per year and never reused. A voucher has at least one line when it is posted or confirmed. | Voucher create, post, confirm
BR-D35 | One open exit line per cattle | A cattle appears on at most one DRAFT, PENDING or CONFIRMED herd exit line; only an ACTIVE cattle can be added to a voucher. | Exit voucher edit, pending voucher creation, confirmation, void
BR-D36 | Non-negative feed stock | The stock of a feed item is the sum of its stock movements in kg; no receipt void, adjustment, feeding record or correction may make it negative. Movements are never edited or deleted. | Feed receipt void, adjustment, feeding record and correction
BR-D37 | One feeding record per session | At most one VALID feeding record per pen, date and session; the feeding date is not in the future. | Feeding record save and correction
BR-D38 | One effective ration per pen | For each pen and date at most one ration version is effective; versions of one ration never overlap. | Ration save, cancel, stop
BR-D39 | Payments within total | Payments and collections of a voucher are positive whole VND, dated not before the voucher date and not in the future, and the sum of RECORDED payments never exceeds the voucher total. | Voucher save, payment record, line correction or void
[/TABLE]
[P311 4] 3.4 State Models
[P312 ] This section brings together the statuses of a cattle and of the records that change them. A stored status changes only through the use cases named below; a derived status is recalculated after every saved source change and is never edited directly (GBR-24). The state diagrams use UML state machine notation: each transition is labelled with its triggering event and, where needed, a guard in brackets.
[P313 5] 3.4.1 Cattle Status Dimensions
[P314 ] A cattle has several independent status dimensions. Table 3.4-1 lists them with their values and the Vietnamese interface labels.
[P315 ] Table 3.4-1 Cattle status dimensions
[TABLE block 316]
Dimension | Values (interface label) | Stored / derived | Changed by | Shown for
Herd status | ACTIVE (Đang nuôi); EXITED (Đã xuất, with the exit type Bán / Loại thải / Chết / Mất / Khác and the exit date); VOIDED (Nhập nhầm – đã hủy) | Stored | Posting a herd entry voucher → ACTIVE; confirming an exit voucher → EXITED; voiding the exit → ACTIVE; voiding the herd entry → VOIDED (3.4.2) | Every cattle
Exit pending | Chờ xuất (on a DRAFT sale, cull or other voucher); Chờ xác nhận chết / mất (on a PENDING death voucher) | Derived | Herd exit voucher lines (GBR-94, GBR-95) | ACTIVE
Production purpose and purpose type | Purpose from the farm catalogue; type BREEDING, FATTENING, SIRE or UNDECIDED | Stored | Owner only, with effective date and reason; SIRE only for males and BREEDING only for females (GBR-25) | ACTIVE; history for every cattle
Life stage | UNWEANED_CALF (Bê chưa cai sữa); WEANED_CALF (Bê cai sữa); YOUNG_STOCK (Bò tơ / hậu bị); ADULT (Trưởng thành) | Stored | Owner or Manager; suggested by REM-08 from age or a first calving (GBR-25) | ACTIVE
Herd group | Bê; Vỗ béo; Đực giống; Bò cái sinh sản; Bò tơ; Khác | Derived | Life stage, purpose type and sex (GBR-24) | ACTIVE
Current pen | Pen code and name | Derived | The open pen assignment: exactly one for an ACTIVE cattle, none for an EXITED or VOIDED cattle (GBR-24) | ACTIVE
Health state | HEALTHY (Khỏe mạnh); MONITORING (Theo dõi); UNDER_TREATMENT (Đang điều trị) | Derived | Health cases (GBR-45, 3.4.4) | ACTIVE
Meat withdrawal | Cách ly thịt đến dd/mm/yyyy, or none | Derived | VALID treatments and on-farm vaccinations (GBR-46) | ACTIVE
Reproductive status | AWAITING_BREEDING (Chờ phối); IN_ESTRUS (Động dục); BRED (Đã phối – chờ khám thai); PREGNANT (Mang thai, with the sub-label Sắp đẻ near the expected calving date); POST_CALVING (Sau sinh); or Not applicable | Derived | VALID reproduction events (GBR-54, 3.4.3) | Applicable ACTIVE females
Breeding eligibility | Đủ điều kiện phối / Chưa đủ điều kiện (with the failed conditions) | Derived | Farm breeding conditions (GBR-55) | Females AWAITING_BREEDING, IN_ESTRUS or POST_CALVING
QR tag | ACTIVE; REPLACED; RETIRED | Stored | Issue or replace (UC-FE05-04); RETIRED when the herd entry is voided | Every cattle
[/TABLE]
[P318 ] On lists and in the profile header the badges appear in this order: herd status (or Exit pending), health state, meat withdrawal, reproductive status. An EXITED or VOIDED cattle shows only its herd status with the exit type and date or the void reason; its derived states are not shown and its full history stays visible (GBR-26).
[P319 5] 3.4.2 Herd Status
[TABLE block 320]
[Figure to be inserted] State diagram – Herd status of a cattle (ACTIVE, Exit pending, EXITED, VOIDED)
[/TABLE]
[P322 ] Table 3.4-2 Herd status transitions
[TABLE block 323]
From | Event (use case) | To | Rule or guard
— | Herd entry voucher posted, including "Register now" at calving (UC-FE06-01, UC-FE12-04) | ACTIVE | One open pen assignment and one ACTIVE QR tag are created (GBR-90, GBR-93).
ACTIVE | Cattle added to a DRAFT exit voucher, or a PENDING death voucher is created from a health case closed as DIED or MISSING (UC-FE13-01, UC-FE10-03) | ACTIVE with Exit pending | The cattle stays ACTIVE. With a PENDING death voucher no new care or reproduction event, pen move or profile change is accepted; corrections and voids of existing records remain possible (GBR-26, GBR-95).
ACTIVE with Exit pending | Line removed, draft discarded, or pending voucher dismissed with a reason (UC-FE13-01) | ACTIVE | A dismissed death voucher keeps the case outcome.
ACTIVE (with or without Exit pending) | Exit voucher confirmed by the Owner (UC-FE13-01) | EXITED | GBR-35: the pen assignment is closed, future doses and reminders are cancelled, open cases are closed by exit and an open pregnancy is closed as DAM_EXITED.
EXITED | Exit voucher voided (UC-FE13-03) | ACTIVE | A new pen assignment starts on the void date; closed or cancelled items are listed for review and not restored (GBR-36).
ACTIVE | Herd entry or whole entry voucher voided (UC-FE06-03, UC-FE06-05) | VOIDED | Only when the cattle has no event other than its entry and first placement. The QR tag becomes RETIRED; the cattle is excluded from lists, counts and reports and is never shared publicly (GBR-33). Final state.
[/TABLE]
[P325 5] 3.4.3 Reproductive Status
[TABLE block 326]
[Figure to be inserted] State diagram – Reproductive status of a female
[/TABLE]
[P328 ] Reproductive status applies to an ACTIVE female at life stage YOUNG_STOCK or ADULT whose purpose type is BREEDING or UNDECIDED, and to any female with an OPEN pregnancy episode (GBR-54). It is recalculated from her latest VALID events; a voided event is ignored.
[P329 ] Table 3.4-3 Reproductive status transitions
[TABLE block 330]
From | Event | To | Rule or guard
Not applicable | The female becomes applicable (stage or purpose change) or a pregnancy episode is opened | AWAITING_BREEDING, or the status given by her latest events | GBR-54
AWAITING_BREEDING, POST_CALVING | Estrus recorded (UC-FE11-02) | IN_ESTRUS | Breeding window reminder REM-02.
IN_ESTRUS | 24 hours pass without a breeding | AWAITING_BREEDING | —
AWAITING_BREEDING, IN_ESTRUS, POST_CALVING | Breeding recorded (UC-FE11-03) | BRED | Tentative expected calving date; REM-03.
BRED | Estrus recorded again | IN_ESTRUS | The tentative date is cancelled (GBR-53).
BRED | Pregnancy check NOT_PREGNANT (UC-FE12-02) | AWAITING_BREEDING | GBR-51
BRED | Pregnancy check INCONCLUSIVE | BRED | Re-check reminder REM-04 (GBR-51).
BRED or any applicable status | Pregnancy check PREGNANT | PREGNANT | The episode is OPEN and the expected date CONFIRMED (GBR-53).
PREGNANT | Calving recorded (UC-FE12-04) | POST_CALVING | The episode is CALVED; REM-07.
PREGNANT | Pregnancy update LOSS (UC-FE12-03) | AWAITING_BREEDING | The episode is LOST; the loss is shown on the timeline (GBR-56).
POST_CALVING | The post-calving interval ends (farm parameter, default 45 days) | AWAITING_BREEDING | —
Any | Herd exit confirmed, or a stage or purpose change ends applicability without an OPEN episode | Not applicable | GBR-35; MSG-FE05-11 before the change.
[/TABLE]
[P332 ] "Sắp đẻ" is shown on PREGNANT when the expected calving date is within the calving preparation lead time (farm parameter, default 30 days).
[P333 5] 3.4.4 Health Case and Health State
[TABLE block 334]
[Figure to be inserted] State diagram – Health case
[/TABLE]
[P336 ] Table 3.4-4 Health case transitions
[TABLE block 337]
From | Event (use case) | To | Rule or guard
— | Abnormal observation opens a case (UC-FE10-02) | OPEN | An observation may instead be added to an OPEN or CHRONIC case of the same cattle (GBR-45).
OPEN | Manager moves the case to long-term monitoring (UC-FE10-03) | CHRONIC | —
CHRONIC | Treatment recorded (UC-FE10-04) or Manager reopens the case | OPEN | GBR-45
OPEN, CHRONIC | Closed with outcome RECOVERED (UC-FE10-03) | CLOSED | —
OPEN, CHRONIC | Closed with outcome DIED or MISSING | CLOSED | Creates a PENDING death voucher and REM-12 (GBR-95).
OPEN, CHRONIC | Herd exit confirmed | CLOSED | Outcome CLOSED_BY_EXIT (GBR-35).
[/TABLE]
[P339 ] The health state of an ACTIVE cattle is UNDER_TREATMENT while an OPEN case has at least one treatment, otherwise MONITORING while any case is OPEN or CHRONIC, otherwise HEALTHY. It returns to MONITORING when the Manager moves the treated case to CHRONIC, and to HEALTHY when all cases are closed (GBR-45).
[P340 5] 3.4.5 Pregnancy Episode
[TABLE block 341]
[Figure to be inserted] State diagram – Pregnancy episode
[/TABLE]
[P343 ] Table 3.4-5 Pregnancy episode transitions
[TABLE block 344]
From | Event (use case) | To | Rule or guard
— | VALID pregnancy check PREGNANT (UC-FE12-02) | OPEN | At most one OPEN episode per female (GBR-52).
OPEN | Calving recorded (UC-FE12-04) | CALVED | Final.
OPEN | Pregnancy update LOSS (UC-FE12-03) | LOST | Final.
OPEN | Herd exit of the dam confirmed | DAM_EXITED | Final.
OPEN | Confirming check voided without dependent records (UC-FE12-05) | VOIDED | Final; a closed episode is never reopened by a normal update (GBR-52).
[/TABLE]
[P346 5] 3.4.6 Herd Exit Voucher
[TABLE block 347]
[Figure to be inserted] State diagram – Herd exit voucher
[/TABLE]
[P349 ] Table 3.4-6 Herd exit voucher transitions
[TABLE block 350]
From | Event (use case) | To | Rule or guard
— | Owner creates a sale, cull, death or other voucher (UC-FE13-01) | DRAFT | Only ACTIVE cattle on no other open exit line (BR-D35).
— | Health case closed as DIED or MISSING (UC-FE10-03) | PENDING | One death voucher for the cattle (GBR-95).
DRAFT | Owner discards the draft | — (deleted) | The cattle keep no exit effect.
DRAFT | Owner confirms [every cattle still ACTIVE] | CONFIRMED | GBR-94; GBR-35 for every line.
PENDING | Owner completes cause and disposal and confirms | CONFIRMED | GBR-95
PENDING | Owner dismisses with a reason | DISMISSED | The cattle stays ACTIVE. Final.
CONFIRMED | Owner voids with a reason (UC-FE13-03) | VOIDED | Every cattle returns to ACTIVE (GBR-36). Final.
[/TABLE]
[P352 3] 4. User Requirements
[P353 4] 4.1 Actors
[TABLE block 354]
# | Actor | Description
1 | Guest | Unauthenticated visitor. Views the public landing page (introduction, features, 1/2/3-year prices, FAQ, contact), registers as a Farm Owner, signs in and resets a forgotten password. Sees no other farm, ticket or financial data.
2 | Farm Owner | Account holder who completed Owner registration and owns one or more farms. In each owned farm: views all farm data; manages farm information, members and roles; manages the subscription and payments; changes production purposes; records herd entry vouchers and their payments (including farm-born calves); records, confirms, dismisses and voids herd exit vouchers and records collections from buyers; maintains pens, cattle profiles and QR tags; maintains feed stock, feed receipts, rations and the feed catalogue and views the feeding log; writes announcements to Managers and Technicians; uses the dashboard, reports and support. Does not record care or reproduction events or feeding sessions.
3 | Farm Manager | Farm member with the Manager role in one farm. Records and corrects growth, vaccination, health and reproduction events; records daily feeding sessions; maintains pens, pen moves, herd entry vouchers and their payments (including farm-born calves), cattle profiles (except production purpose), QR tags, catalogues and parameters, feed stock, feed receipts, rations and the feed catalogue; writes announcements to Technicians; uses the dashboard and reports. Cannot manage members, the subscription or herd exit vouchers.
4 | Farm Technician | Farm member with the Technician role in one farm. Field user with read-only access: scans QR tags, views cattle, pens, care and reproduction information, today's rations, the feeding log, reminders and received announcements. May be named as observer or performer in records entered by a Manager, or as the person who fed in a feeding record.
5 | Customer Support (CS) | Platform staff. Reads the support queue; the assigned CS claims, processes, replies, writes internal notes, resolves and closes tickets and sends internal requests to a named PM or SA. Has no farm operational access.
6 | Platform Manager (PM) | Platform staff. Views farm service status and suspends or restores farm service; views subscriptions, payments and bank evidence and reconciles payments; publishes plan prices; manages manual finance records and the platform cash report; handles internal requests assigned to them. Has no access to cattle or care data.
7 | System Admin (SA) | Platform staff. Views and suspends or restores user accounts, grants and revokes CS, PM and SA access, reviews the platform audit history and handles internal requests assigned to them. Keeps at least one ACTIVE System Admin.
8 | Google Identity Provider | External system that authenticates a person through OAuth 2.0 / OpenID Connect and returns the verified identity.
9 | SePay | External system that reports bank transactions on the BeefCare receiving account through authenticated webhooks, provides transaction lookup and renders VietQR images.
10 | Email Delivery Service | External system that delivers transactional emails (invitations, verification and reset links, notices).
11 | System Timer | Internal scheduler that runs the background jobs in III.24 (reminder evaluation, expiries, notices).
[/TABLE]
[P356 4] 4.2 Use Cases (UC)
[P357 ] The use cases are grouped by feature (FE-01 to FE-23); Table 4.2-1 maps the features to the business flows. Use case names follow the verb + object form; actor conditions are detailed in the Permission Matrix (4.4) and the specifications (II).
[P358 5] 4.2.1 FE-01 Authentication and Account Access
[TABLE block 359]
ID | Use Case | Feature | Use Case Description
UC-FE01-01 | Register Farm Owner Account | FE-01 | A Guest creates an account with Google, or with an email and password verified through an emailed link, and completes the Owner profile so the account can register farms.
UC-FE01-02 | Sign In | FE-01 | A Guest signs in with Google or with a verified email and password and reaches only the farms and platform areas currently authorized.
UC-FE01-03 | Sign Out | FE-01 | A signed-in user ends the BeefCare session and clears the selected farm on the device.
UC-FE01-04 | View and Update Own Profile | FE-01 | A signed-in user views own profile, sign-in methods, roles and permissions in the current farm, and updates full name and contact phone.
UC-FE01-05 | Reset Forgotten Password | FE-01 | A Guest receives a single-use reset link by email and sets a new password.
UC-FE01-06 | Change Password | FE-01 | A signed-in user changes the password, or adds a first password to an account that uses only Google.
[/TABLE]

[TABLE block 362]
ID | Use Case | Feature | Use Case Description

UC-FE02-02 | View and Select Authorized Farm Workspace | 2.1 Owner Farm Workspace | A farm member views the farms where they have an ACTIVE membership with role and service status and selects one as the current farm.
UC-FE02-03 | Update Farm Information | 2.1 Owner Farm Workspace | The Farm Owner updates registration, contact and operating information of an owned farm.
UC-FE02-04 | View Platform Farm Service Overview | 2.2 Platform Farm Service | The Platform Manager views, searches and filters all farms with Owner, service status and coverage, without farm operational data.
UC-FE02-05 | Suspend or Restore Farm Service | 2.2 Platform Farm Service | The Platform Manager suspends a farm's service with a reason, or restores it so that its status follows the subscription again.
[/TABLE]

[TABLE block 365]
ID | Use Case | Feature | Use Case Description
UC-FE03-01 | Send Farm Member Invitation | 3.1 Farm Membership | The Farm Owner invites an email address to an ACTIVE owned farm as Farm Manager or Farm Technician.
UC-FE03-02 | View Farm Invitations | 3.1 Farm Membership | The Farm Owner views and filters the invitations of an owned farm by status.
UC-FE03-03 | Manage Pending Farm Invitation | 3.1 Farm Membership | The Farm Owner resends or cancels a PENDING invitation.
UC-FE03-04 | Accept Farm Invitation | 3.1 Farm Membership | The invited person signs in with the invited email and accepts; exactly one membership is created.
UC-FE03-05 | View Farm Members | 3.1 Farm Membership | The Farm Owner (full detail) or a Farm Manager (read-only list) views the members of the farm with role, status and the fixed permissions of each role.
UC-FE03-06 | Update Farm Member Role and Access | 3.1 Farm Membership | The Farm Owner changes a member between Manager and Technician, or deactivates or reactivates a membership, with effective date and reason.
UC-FE03-07 | View Platform User Accounts | 3.2 Platform Account Administration | The System Admin searches user accounts and views account status, platform role and farm membership summary.
UC-FE03-08 | Suspend or Restore Platform User Account | 3.2 Platform Account Administration | The System Admin suspends or restores a user account with a reason, keeping at least one ACTIVE System Admin.
UC-FE03-09 | Manage Platform Staff Access | 3.2 Platform Account Administration | The System Admin grants, changes, revokes or restores the CS, PM or SA role of an existing account.
[/TABLE]

UC-FE06-04 | Record Herd Entry Voucher Payment | FE-06 | The Farm Owner or a Manager records a payment to the seller against a posted voucher, or voids a wrong payment with a reason.
UC-FE06-05 | Void Herd Entry Voucher | FE-06 | The Farm Owner or a Manager voids a posted voucher recorded by mistake when none of its cattle has other events.
[/TABLE]
