UC-FE18-07 | Update Vaccine Reference | 18.2 Vaccine | The Farm Owner or a Manager edits a vaccine reference without changing past vaccination records.
UC-FE18-08 | Change Vaccine Availability or Remove Unused Reference | 18.2 Vaccine | The Farm Owner or a Manager deactivates or reactivates a vaccine, or deletes a vaccine that has never been used.
UC-FE18-09 | View Medicine Reference Catalogue | 18.3 Medicine | A farm member views and searches the farm's reference medicines.
UC-FE18-10 | Create Medicine Reference | 18.3 Medicine | The Farm Owner or a Manager adds a reference medicine with reference dose, route and withdrawal period.
UC-FE18-11 | Update Medicine Reference | 18.3 Medicine | The Farm Owner or a Manager edits a medicine reference without changing past treatments.
UC-FE18-12 | Change Medicine Reference Availability | 18.3 Medicine | The Farm Owner or a Manager deactivates or reactivates a reference medicine.
UC-FE18-13 | View Production Purpose Catalogue | 18.4 Production Purpose | The Farm Owner or a Manager views the farm's production purposes.
UC-FE18-14 | Create Production Purpose | 18.4 Production Purpose | The Farm Owner or a Manager adds a production purpose with its purpose type (Breeding, Fattening, Sire, Undecided).
UC-FE18-15 | Update Production Purpose | 18.4 Production Purpose | The Farm Owner or a Manager edits a production purpose; existing cattle keep their purpose and the purpose type is fixed once referenced.
UC-FE18-16 | Change Production Purpose Availability | 18.4 Production Purpose | The Farm Owner or a Manager deactivates or reactivates a production purpose for new selection.
UC-FE18-17 | Configure Farm State and Reminder References | 18.5 Farm Parameters | The Farm Owner or a Manager saves a new version of life-stage ages, breeding conditions, gestation and reminder intervals, BCS scale and weighing interval with a source note.
[/TABLE]

[P464 4] 4.4 Permission Matrix
[P465 ] Full = unrestricted · Restricted = conditions apply (see footnotes) · No = denied · Techn. = Farm Technician. All farm permissions apply only to farms where the user has an ACTIVE membership and, except where noted in GBR-14, an ACTIVE farm service; every request is rechecked (GBR-09). Entity names follow the conceptual model (3.1, 3.2); a Detail item is named together with its owning entity.

[P472 ] ⁵ Read-only list of names, roles and status; no invitations, membership history or actions.
[P473 ] ⁶ Not on the Owner membership; role change and reactivation require an ACTIVE farm (GBR-18).

[P476 ] ⁹ Announcements the user authored or received; drafts only to their author.
[P477 ] ¹⁰ Owner: all farm reminders except REM-11 (feeding session not recorded). Technician: all farm reminders except REM-09 (draft calf awaiting confirmation), REM-10 (low feed stock), REM-12 (death or loss awaiting confirmation) and REM-13 (finishing weight reached).
[P478 ] ¹¹ Only owned farms; available in any service status. A renewal request is allowed only in the renewal window (GBR-71).
[P479 ] ¹² Only own tickets and their PUBLIC content (GBR-81).
[P480 ] ¹³ Only internal requests currently assigned to the user by name.
[P481 ] ¹⁴ Only the CS currently assigned to the ticket, and only while the ticket is not CLOSED (GBR-77).
[P482 ] ¹⁵ Sellers and feed suppliers only; buyers are maintained by the Owner (GBR-97).
[P483 3] 5. System Functionalities
[P484 ] BeefCare is one responsive web application used in desktop, tablet and phone browsers; no native mobile application is required. Farm users work inside the selected farm (left sidebar on desktop, bottom navigation on phone); platform staff use a separate platform console. Scanning a QR ear tag uses the phone camera through the browser. External integrations are Google sign-in, SePay (bank transaction webhooks, transaction lookup and VietQR) and an email delivery service; background jobs evaluate reminders, expiries and notices.

# | Screen Name | Feature | Description
SCR-01 | Public Landing Page (Trang công khai) | FE-22 | Allows a Guest to read the introduction, features, how it works, current 1/2/3-year prices, FAQ and contact, and to start registration or sign-in.
SCR-02 | Sign In (Đăng nhập) | FE-01 | Allows any user to sign in with Google or with email and password and reach their authorized area.

SCR-05 | Forgot Password (Quên mật khẩu) | FE-01 | Allows a Guest to request a password-reset link; shows the same confirmation whether or not the email exists.
SCR-06 | Set New Password (Đặt mật khẩu mới) | FE-01 | Allows a Guest who opened a valid reset link to set a new password; shows "link no longer valid" for an expired or used link.
SCR-07 | Complete Owner Profile (Hoàn tất hồ sơ chủ trại) | FE-01 | Allows a newly authenticated user to complete the Owner profile (step 1 of onboarding).
SCR-08 | My Profile and Permissions (Hồ sơ & quyền của tôi) | FE-01, FE-03 | Allows a signed-in user to view and update own profile, sign-in methods and the fixed permissions of their role in the current farm. Popups: Change Password.
SCR-09 | Select Farm (Chọn trang trại) | FE-02 | Allows a user to see their farms with role and service status, register a new farm (Owner) and enter a farm.
SCR-10 | Register Farm (Đăng ký trang trại) | FE-02 | Allows the Owner to register a farm (step 2 of onboarding).
SCR-11 | Farm Information (Thông tin trại) | FE-02 | Allows the Owner to view and update registration, contact and operating information and see the service summary.
SCR-12 | Farm Service Overview (Dịch vụ trang trại) | FE-02 | Allows the PM to view, search and filter all farms with Owner, service status and coverage end. Popups: Suspend / Restore Farm Service.
SCR-13 | Members and Roles (Thành viên & vai trò) | FE-03 | Allows the Owner to view members and the permission table by role and to start member actions; read-only list for a Manager. Popups: Invite Member, Change Role / Deactivate Member.
SCR-14 | Farm Invitations (Lời mời thành viên) | FE-03 | Allows the Owner to view invitations by status and resend or cancel pending ones. Popups: Invite Member.
SCR-15 | Accept Invitation (Chấp nhận lời mời) | FE-03 | Allows the invited person to review farm, role and permissions and accept; shows "invitation no longer valid" otherwise.
SCR-16 | User Accounts (Tài khoản người dùng) | FE-03 | Allows the SA to search accounts, view account detail and suspend or restore an account. Popups: Platform Staff Access.
SCR-17 | Pens (Chuồng trại) | FE-04 | Allows farm members to view pens, occupancy, criteria and current cattle; Owner and Manager can create, edit, stop pens and move cattle. Popups: Create / Edit Pen, Stop / Resume Pen, Move Pen.
SCR-18 | Cattle List (Đàn bò) | FE-05 | Allows farm members to search and filter cattle by herd group and other criteria and open a profile.
SCR-19 | Cattle Profile – Overview (Hồ sơ bò › Tổng quan) | FE-05 | Allows farm members to view identity, state, parents, care summary and related reminders, and to open the other profile tabs. Popups: Update Cattle Profile, Issue / Replace QR Tag.
SCR-20 | Profile tab – Pen History (Lịch sử chuồng) | FE-04, FE-05 | Allows farm members to view the current pen and pen history; Owner and Manager can start a pen move. Popups: Move Pen.
SCR-21 | Profile tab – QR Tag (Thẻ QR) | FE-05 | Allows farm members to view the current tag and tag history. Popups: Issue / Replace QR Tag.
SCR-22 | Scan QR (Quét thẻ tai QR) | FE-05 | Allows farm members to scan an ear tag with the camera or enter a code and open the profile.
SCR-23 | Herd Entry Vouchers (Phiếu nhập – Danh sách) | FE-06 | Allows the Owner or a Manager to view entry vouchers by status, type and source with totals and outstanding amounts and to start a new voucher. Popups: Correct / Void Herd Entry.
SCR-24 | Herd Entry Voucher – Purchase (Phiếu nhập mới – Mua ngoài) | FE-06 | Allows the Owner or a Manager to enter a purchase voucher with its cattle lines, documents and payment, save it as draft or post it. Popups: Voucher Cattle Line, Import Cattle from Excel.
SCR-25 | Herd Entry Voucher – Farm-born Calves (Phiếu nhập mới – Bê sinh tại trại) | FE-06 | Allows the Owner or a Manager to select pending calves, set pen and purpose and post them; a calf that died before registration is marked here.
SCR-26 | Farm Announcements (Thông báo trại) | FE-07 | Allows farm members to read received and authored announcements, versions and delivery status.
SCR-27 | Announcement Editor (Soạn thông báo) | FE-07 | Allows the Owner or a Manager to write an announcement and save a draft. Popups: Review and Publish.
SCR-28 | Announcement Correction (Đính chính thông báo) | FE-07 | Allows the author to publish a corrected version with a reason.
SCR-29 | Growth and BCS (Tăng trưởng & BCS) | FE-08 | Allows farm members to view growth indicators of the herd and open a cattle's growth tab.
SCR-30 | Profile tab – Growth and BCS (Tăng trưởng & BCS) | FE-08 | Allows farm members to view a cattle's weight chart, ADG, BCS and measurement history; the Manager records and corrects. Popups: Record Weight and BCS, Correct / Exclude Growth Record.
SCR-31 | Vaccination Schedule (Lịch tiêm phòng) | FE-09 | Allows farm members to view schedules and doses by due status; the Manager records or defers doses. Popups: Defer Dose.
SCR-32 | Manage Vaccination Schedule (Quản lý lịch tiêm) | FE-09 | Allows the Manager to create, update or stop a schedule.
SCR-33 | Record Vaccination (Ghi nhận tiêm) | FE-09 | Allows the Manager to record a vaccination for one cattle or a batch, deferring individual cattle.
SCR-34 | Profile tab – Vaccination (Tiêm phòng) | FE-09 | Allows farm members to view a cattle's scheduled doses and vaccination history; the Manager records, defers and corrects. Popups: Defer Dose, Correct / Void Vaccination.
SCR-35 | Vaccination History (Lịch sử tiêm phòng) | FE-09 | Allows farm members to search vaccinations across the herd.
SCR-36 | Health Cases (Sức khỏe & Điều trị) | FE-10 | Allows farm members to view open and closed health cases of the herd.
SCR-37 | Profile tab – Health (Sức khỏe) | FE-10 | Allows farm members to view a cattle's cases, observations, treatments and withdrawal; the Manager opens, follows up, treats and closes. Popups: Open Health Case, Record Follow-up, Record Treatment, Update / Close Health Case.
SCR-38 | Reproduction Worklist (Sinh sản) | FE-11, FE-12 | Allows farm members to view females by reproductive situation and the next action and open a profile.
SCR-39 | Profile tab – Reproduction (Sinh sản) | FE-11, FE-12 | Allows farm members to view reproductive status, breeding eligibility, breeding window, current pregnancy and reproduction timeline; the Manager records and corrects. Popups: Record Estrus, Record Breeding, Correct / Void Estrus or Breeding, Record Pregnancy Check, Record Pregnancy Update, Correct / Void Pregnancy Check, Correct / Void Pregnancy and Calving Record.
SCR-40 | Record Calving (Ghi sự kiện đẻ & kết quả bê) | FE-12 | Allows the Manager to record a calving, calf outcomes and the registration choice for each live calf.
SCR-41 | Herd Exit Vouchers (Phiếu xuất – Danh sách) | FE-13 | Allows the Owner or a Manager to view exit vouchers by status and type with totals and amounts to collect; the Owner starts a new voucher.
SCR-42 | Herd Exit Voucher (Phiếu xuất mới – Bán / Loại thải / Khác) | FE-13 | Allows the Owner to enter an exit voucher with cattle, weights, prices and collection, review the checks and confirm. Popups: Select Cattle for Exit Voucher.
SCR-43 | Death / Missing Exit Voucher (Phiếu xuất – Chết / mất) | FE-13 | Allows the Owner to complete and confirm, or dismiss, a pending voucher created from a health case.
SCR-44 | Farm Reminders (Nhắc việc trang trại) | FE-14 | Allows farm members to view reminders by type and due group and open the reminder detail and source. Popups: Move Pen, Update Cattle Profile.
SCR-45 | My Notifications (Thông báo của tôi) | FE-14 | Allows the Owner to view subscription and support notifications.
SCR-46 | Support Notifications (Thông báo hỗ trợ) | FE-14 | Allows CS, and a PM or SA for their assigned requests, to view support notifications.
SCR-47 | Reconciliation Notifications (Thông báo đối soát) | FE-14 | Allows the PM to view notices about evidence and payments to review.
SCR-48 | Farm Dashboard (Tổng quan trang trại) | FE-15 | Allows the Owner or a Manager to view indicators, attention list and herd structure and drill down.
SCR-49 | Management Reports (Báo cáo quản lý) | FE-15 | Allows the Owner or a Manager to select a report, period and filters and view results.
SCR-50 | Profile tab – Timeline (Dòng thời gian) | FE-16 | Allows farm members to view a cattle's events in date order and filter them. Popups: Correct / Exclude Growth Record, Correct / Void Vaccination, Correct / Void Estrus or Breeding, Correct / Void Pregnancy Check, Correct / Void Pregnancy and Calving Record.
SCR-51 | Platform Audit History (Lịch sử audit nền tảng) | FE-17 | Allows the SA to search audit entries and open their allowlisted detail.
SCR-52 | Breed Catalogue (Giống bò) | FE-18 | Allows farm members to view breeds; the Owner or a Manager creates, edits and changes availability.
SCR-53 | Vaccine Catalogue (Vắc-xin) | FE-18 | Allows farm members to view vaccines; the Owner or a Manager creates, edits, changes availability or removes unused ones.
SCR-54 | Medicine Catalogue (Thuốc tham chiếu) | FE-18 | Allows farm members to view reference medicines; the Owner or a Manager creates, edits and changes availability.
SCR-55 | Production Purpose Catalogue (Mục đích nuôi) | FE-18 | Allows the Owner or a Manager to view and maintain production purposes and their purpose types. Popups: Update Cattle Profile.
SCR-56 | State and Reminder Parameters (Tham số trạng thái & nhắc việc) | FE-18 | Allows the Owner or a Manager to view parameters and save a new version.
SCR-57 | Farm Subscription (Gói dịch vụ của trại) | FE-19 | Allows the Owner to view service status, periods, renewal date, pending request and prices.
SCR-58 | Select Term and Review (Chọn thời hạn / Đăng ký gói) | FE-19 | Allows the Owner to choose 1, 2 or 3 years, review and create a payment request.
SCR-59 | Transfer Instructions (Hướng dẫn chuyển khoản) | FE-19 | Allows the Owner to see VietQR, transfer details, countdown and progress of a payment request.
SCR-60 | Payment History and Detail (Lịch sử thanh toán) | FE-19 | Allows the Owner to view payment requests of a farm and open a payment detail.
SCR-61 | Platform Subscriptions (Gói đăng ký) | FE-19 | Allows the PM to view farm subscriptions, periods and source payments.
SCR-62 | Payments and Reconciliation (Thanh toán & đối soát) | FE-19 | Allows the PM to review bank evidence and payment requests and record ACCEPT, HOLD or REJECT.
SCR-63 | Plan Prices (Bảng giá gói) | FE-19 | Allows the PM to view price history and publish a new price version.
SCR-64 | My Support Tickets (Ticket hỗ trợ của tôi) | FE-20 | Allows the Owner to view and filter own tickets.
SCR-65 | Create Support Ticket (Gửi yêu cầu hỗ trợ) | FE-20 | Allows the Owner to submit a ticket.
SCR-66 | Ticket Detail – Owner (Chi tiết ticket) | FE-20 | Allows the Owner to read the conversation and resolution, reply, confirm the resolution or reopen.
SCR-67 | Ticket Queue (Hàng đợi hỗ trợ) | FE-20 | Allows CS to view, search and filter tickets and claim unassigned ones.
SCR-68 | Ticket Processing (Xử lý ticket) | FE-20 | Allows the assigned CS to reply, add notes, classify, change status, manage internal requests and record a resolution. Popups: Close Ticket.
SCR-69 | Assigned Internal Requests (Yêu cầu nội bộ được giao) | FE-20 | Allows a PM or SA to view internal requests assigned to them and submit the result.
SCR-70 | Subscription Receipts (Biên nhận gói) | FE-21 | Allows the PM to view receipts and trace each to its payment and evidence.
SCR-71 | Manual Finance Records (Thu chi khác) | FE-21 | Allows the PM to view and filter manual income and expense records.
SCR-72 | Manual Finance Record Form (Khoản thu chi) | FE-21 | Allows the PM to create, edit, discard, post or void a manual record.
SCR-73 | Platform Cash Report (Báo cáo dòng tiền nền tảng) | FE-21 | Allows the PM to run the cash report, view totals and details and export XLSX.
SCR-74 | Technician Home (Kỹ thuật viên · Trang chủ) | FE-07, FE-14 | Allows a Technician to scan a tag, see new announcements and today's reminders on the phone.
SCR-75 | Herd Entry Voucher Detail (Chi tiết phiếu nhập) | FE-06 | Allows the Owner or a Manager to view a posted or voided entry voucher with its cattle, payments and history, print it, record a payment or void it. Popups: Correct / Void Herd Entry, Record Voucher Payment, Void Herd Entry Voucher.
SCR-76 | Herd Exit Voucher Detail (Chi tiết phiếu xuất) | FE-13 | Allows the Owner or a Manager to view an exit voucher with its cattle, collections and history; the Owner prints it, records a collection or voids it. Popups: Void Herd Exit Voucher, Record Voucher Payment.
SCR-77 | Feed Stock (Thức ăn – Tồn kho) | FE-23 | Allows the Owner or a Manager to view the stock, minimum level, days of cover and lots near expiry of each feed.
SCR-78 | Feed Receipts (Phiếu nhập kho thức ăn) | FE-23 | Allows the Owner or a Manager to view feed receipts by status and supplier and open or void one. Popups: Record Voucher Payment.
SCR-79 | Feed Receipt Form (Phiếu nhập kho mới) | FE-23 | Allows the Owner or a Manager to create, save as draft and post a feed receipt with lines and payment.
SCR-80 | Stock Adjustment (Kiểm kê / điều chỉnh tồn kho) | FE-23 | Allows the Owner or a Manager to compare counted with system stock and post the differences with reasons.
SCR-81 | Rations (Khẩu phần) | FE-23 | Allows the Owner or a Manager to view rations by pen, version and status with daily quantity and cost.
SCR-82 | Ration Editor (Sửa khẩu phần) | FE-23 | Allows the Owner or a Manager to create a ration or save a new version with its components and effective date.
SCR-83 | Feeding Log (Nhật ký cho ăn) | FE-23 | Allows the Owner, a Manager or a Technician to view feeding sessions per pen and date; the Manager records and corrects. Popups: Record Feeding Session, Correct / Void Feeding Record.
SCR-84 | Feed Catalogue and Suppliers (Danh mục thức ăn & nhà cung cấp) | FE-23 | Allows the Owner or a Manager to maintain feed items and suppliers.
SCR-85 | Feed Consumption and Cost Report (Báo cáo tiêu thụ & chi phí thức ăn) | FE-23 | Allows the Owner or a Manager to view and print consumption and cost by herd group, pen or feed.
SCR-86 | Today's Rations (Kỹ thuật viên · Khẩu phần hôm nay) | FE-23 | Allows a Technician to view today's planned feeding per pen and session on the phone (read-only).
[/TABLE]
[P506 4] 5.2 External API Inventory
[TABLE block 507]
# | API Name | Calling Path | Description
API-01 | VietQR image | BeefCare → SePay QR service | Renders the VietQR image of a payment request from the receiving bank, account number, amount and payment reference.
API-02 | SePay transaction webhook | SePay → BeefCare | Delivers each bank transaction on the BeefCare receiving account; BeefCare authenticates it, stores the evidence and runs payment confirmation.
API-03 | SePay transaction lookup | BeefCare → SePay | Lets the PM reconciliation workbench retrieve transactions of the receiving account for a period or transaction ID.
API-04 | Transactional email | BeefCare → Email Delivery Service | Sends invitation, email-verification, password-reset, password-changed, subscription and support notification emails.
API-05 | Google sign-in (OAuth 2.0 / OpenID Connect) | BeefCare ↔ Google Identity Provider | Redirects the user to Google and validates the returned identity (subject, verified email, name) for registration, sign-in and invitation acceptance.
[/TABLE]
[P509 4] 5.3 Background Job Inventory
[TABLE block 510]
# | Job Name | Group | Description
JOB-01 | Evaluate farm reminders | Scheduled + event | Every 15 minutes and after each saved source change: creates, updates, resolves or cancels reminders REM-01 to REM-13.
JOB-02 | Expire farm invitations | Scheduled | Every 15 minutes: marks PENDING invitations past their expiry time as EXPIRED.
JOB-03 | Expire unpaid payment requests | Scheduled | Every 5 minutes: marks PENDING payment requests past their 24-hour expiry as EXPIRED.
JOB-04 | Evaluate subscription coverage | Scheduled | Every 15 minutes: recalculates farm service status when a purchased period starts or ends.
JOB-05 | Generate subscription notices | Scheduled | Daily at 07:00 (Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh): creates renewal-opening, expiry-approaching and expired notices for Owners.
JOB-06 | Process received bank evidence | Queue | Continuously: processes stored SePay evidence through payment confirmation; retries unfinished work.
JOB-07 | Dispatch notifications and emails | Queue | Every minute: delivers committed in-app notifications and emails with retry and deduplication.
JOB-08 | Purge expired tokens and snapshots | Scheduled | Daily at 02:00: removes expired verification, reset and invitation link tokens and expired report snapshots.
[/TABLE]
[P512 2] II. Use Case Specifications
[P513 ] This part specifies the use cases that belong to the main business flows or contain complex rules. Viewing, search and simple maintenance (CRUD) use cases are described screen by screen in Part III. Every specification uses the same identifiers as I.4.2; messages (MSG-…) are listed in V.2 and rules (GBR-…, BR-D…) in V.1 and I.3.3. Common behaviour that applies to all specifications: the system rechecks permission and farm service on every request (GBR-09), repeated submissions return the first result (GBR-88), and an update based on an outdated version is rejected with MSG-COM-05 (GBR-89).
[P514 ] Table II-1 Use cases specified in this part
[TABLE block 515]

[P517 3] 1. FE-01 Authentication and Account Access
[P518 4] 1.1 Register Farm Owner Account
[TABLE block 519]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE01-01 — Register Farm Owner Account
Created By | Ducnv | Date Created | 17/10/2026
Primary Actor | Guest | Secondary Actors | Google Identity Provider; Email Delivery Service
Trigger | The Guest selects "Register as Farm Owner" (Đăng ký chủ trại) on the landing page or the sign-in page.
Description | As a farm owner, I want to create a BeefCare account with my Google account or with my email and a password, so that I can register and manage my farms.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is not signed in. / PRE-2: The registration page is available.
Postconditions | POST-1: One ACTIVE account with a verified email exists (created or reused) and the user is signed in. / POST-2: Owner registration is completed and the user is on Register Farm (UC-FE02-01). / POST-3: No farm, membership, platform role or subscription has been created.
Normal Flow | 1. Guest opens Register Farm Owner (SCR-03) and selects Continue with Google. / 2. System redirects the Guest to Google (API-05). / 3. Guest authenticates and gives consent at Google. / 4. Google returns the identity (subject, verified email, name) to the System. / 5. System validates the identity and finds the account by Google subject or verified email; when none exists it creates an ACTIVE account with that email and links the Google identity (GBR-01). / 6. System signs the user in and shows Complete Owner Profile (SCR-07): verified email read-only, full name proposed from Google. / 7. Guest confirms the full name, enters an optional contact phone, accepts the Terms of Use and Data Policy and selects "Complete and register farm". / 8. System validates the fields, marks Owner registration complete and opens Register Farm (SCR-10).
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Register with email and password (step 1) / 1. Guest enters full name, email, password and confirmation, accepts the terms and selects Create account. / 2. System validates the fields (GBR-02) and checks that no account uses the email. / 3. System creates the account with a password method pending verification, sends a verification link valid for 24 hours (API-04, GBR-03) and shows Verify Email (SCR-04) with MSG-FE01-04. / 4. Guest opens the link from the email. / 5. System verifies the link, activates the account and its password, signs the user in and continues at step 6 with the name already entered. / AF2 – Account already exists (step 5): the existing account is reused. If Owner registration is already complete, the System opens Select Farm (SCR-09); otherwise it continues at step 6. / AF3 – Guest leaves before step 7: the account remains; the next sign-in resumes Complete Owner Profile. / AF4 – Resend verification email (AF1 step 3): the System invalidates the previous link and sends a new one; at most 5 emails per hour per address.
Exceptions | E1: Google sign-in cancelled or not completed: MSG-FE01-12; no account is created. / E2: AF1 with an email that already has an account: MSG-FE01-10 with links to Sign In and Forgot Password; no second account. / E3: Invalid fields: MSG-COM-06, MSG-COM-10, MSG-FE01-08, MSG-FE01-09 or MSG-FE01-14 under the field. / E4: Verification link expired or already used: MSG-FE01-06 with Resend. / E5: Account SUSPENDED: the Account Suspended screen (MSG-FE01-03) is shown; no session. / E6: Email delivery or save failure: MSG-COM-02; nothing is partially saved.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Once per Farm Owner.
Business Rules | GBR-01, GBR-02, GBR-03, GBR-05; BR-D02, BR-D03. Owner registration grants no farm, role or service. Phone is contact information only.
Other Information | Screens SCR-01, SCR-03, SCR-04, SCR-07. API-04, API-05. Related: UC-FE01-02, UC-FE02-01.
Assumptions | Google manages its own authentication, security and recovery; BeefCare never receives a Google password.
[/TABLE]
[P521 4] 1.2 Sign In
[TABLE block 522]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE01-02 — Sign In
Created By | Ducnv | Date Created | 17/10/2026
Primary Actor | Guest | Secondary Actors | Google Identity Provider
Trigger | The Guest selects "Sign in", or opens a protected page, a notification link or an invitation link while signed out.
Description | As a registered user, I want to sign in with Google or with my email and password, so that I can work in the farms and platform areas I am authorized for.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user has a BeefCare account. / PRE-2: The user is not signed in.
Postconditions | POST-1: A session exists and the user sees only the farms and platform areas currently authorized. / POST-2: Signing in never accepts an invitation, restores revoked access or changes a subscription.
Normal Flow | 1. Guest opens Sign In (SCR-02), enters email and password, optionally ticks "Remember me" and selects Sign in. / 2. System checks that the account exists, its password is active (email verified), password sign-in is not locked and the password matches the stored hash (GBR-02). / 3. System checks that the account is ACTIVE (GBR-04) and resets the failed-attempt counter. / 4. System creates the session: valid until 8 hours of inactivity, or 30 days with "Remember me". / 5. System resolves the user's current platform role and ACTIVE farm memberships (GBR-09). / 6. System opens the destination: the requested page when it is still authorized; otherwise the role home screen of a platform staff member (CS: SCR-67, PM: SCR-12, SA: SCR-16), the farm home screen when the user has exactly one available farm (Owner or Manager: SCR-48, Technician: SCR-74), or Select Farm (SCR-09) when there are several.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Continue with Google (step 1): System redirects to Google (API-05), validates the returned identity and finds the account by Google subject or verified email. When no account exists, the System creates an ACTIVE account without Owner registration and continues at step 4. / AF2 – Started from an invitation link (step 6): System continues with UC-FE03-04. / AF3 – No farm and no platform role (step 6): System shows MSG-FE01-15 with "Register as Farm Owner" (continues at UC-FE01-01 step 6) and guidance to open the invitation email. / AF4 – Owner registration started but not completed (step 6): System opens Complete Owner Profile (SCR-07). / AF5 – Forgot password (step 1): UC-FE01-05. / AF6 – Email not verified (step 2): MSG-FE01-11 with "Resend verification email".
Exceptions | E1: Wrong email or password: MSG-FE01-01; the failed attempt is counted. The message does not reveal which value is wrong. / E2: 5 consecutive failures: password sign-in is locked for 15 minutes (MSG-FE01-02); Google sign-in remains available. / E3: Account SUSPENDED: Account Suspended screen (MSG-FE01-03) with the official contact (GBR-87); no session. / E4: Google sign-in cancelled or failed: MSG-FE01-12. / E5: The requested farm is no longer authorized: MSG-COM-01 and the remaining authorized farms are shown. / E6: Service unavailable: MSG-COM-03; no session.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Several times a day per user.
Business Rules | GBR-01, GBR-02, GBR-04, GBR-09, GBR-14.
Other Information | Screens SCR-02, SCR-09. API-05. Related: UC-FE01-01, UC-FE01-05, UC-FE02-02, UC-FE03-04.
Assumptions | Session duration values are configuration defaults.
[/TABLE]
[P524 4] 1.3 Reset Forgotten Password
[TABLE block 525]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE01-05 — Reset Forgotten Password
Created By | Ducnv | Date Created | 17/10/2026
Primary Actor | Guest | Secondary Actors | Email Delivery Service
Trigger | The Guest selects "Forgot password?" (Quên mật khẩu?) on Sign In.
Description | As a user who forgot my password, I want to receive a single-use link by email and set a new password, so that I can sign in again.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is not signed in.
Postconditions | POST-1: The new password hash is saved, all sessions of the account are ended and a password-changed email is sent. / POST-2: Roles, memberships and the Google sign-in method are unchanged.
Normal Flow | 1. Guest enters the email on Forgot Password (SCR-05) and selects "Send reset link". / 2. System always shows the same confirmation (MSG-FE01-05). When an ACTIVE password method exists for the email, it creates a reset link valid for 30 minutes and invalidates any earlier link (GBR-03). / 3. Email Delivery Service sends the link (API-04). / 4. Guest opens the link. / 5. System checks that the link is unused and unexpired and shows Set New Password (SCR-06) with the email read-only. / 6. Guest enters and confirms the new password and selects "Save new password". / 7. System validates the password (GBR-02), saves only its hash, marks the link used, ends all sessions, clears any lockout and sends a password-changed email. / 8. System shows Sign In with MSG-FE01-07.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Account uses only Google (step 2): the email advises signing in with Google; no password is created. / AF2 – Resend (step 2): Guest selects Resend; a new link replaces the previous one (max 5 per hour). / AF3 – Guest remembers the password: Guest returns to Sign In; nothing changes.
Exceptions | E1: Link expired or already used (step 5): screen "Link no longer valid" (MSG-FE01-06) with "Send new link". / E2: Password rules not met or confirmation different: MSG-FE01-08 / MSG-FE01-09; the old password stays. / E3: Account SUSPENDED: the password can be reset but sign-in stays blocked (GBR-04). / E4: Email delivery or save failure: MSG-COM-02.
Priority | Medium
Frequency of Use | Rare.
Business Rules | GBR-02, GBR-03, GBR-04. The response never reveals whether an email is registered.
Other Information | Screens SCR-02, SCR-05, SCR-06. API-04.
Assumptions | The user has access to the mailbox of the account email.
[/TABLE]
[P527 4] 1.4 Change Password
[TABLE block 528]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE01-06 — Change Password

System saves the new hash, keeps the current session, ends the other sessions and sends a password-changed email. / 5. System closes the dialog and shows MSG-FE01-07.

Exceptions | E1: Current password wrong: MSG-FE01-16; after 5 failures MSG-FE01-02. / E2: New password invalid or confirmation different: MSG-FE01-08 / MSG-FE01-09. / E3: Session expired: the user must sign in again; nothing is saved. / E4: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; the previous password stays.
Priority | Medium
Frequency of Use | Rare.
Business Rules | GBR-02. Passwords are never displayed, logged or returned.
Other Information | Screens SCR-08, popup "Change Password". API-04, API-05.

[TABLE block 532]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE02-01 — Register Farm Workspace
Created By | Ducnv | Date Created | 17/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Owner | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The Owner completes the Owner profile (UC-FE01-01) or selects "Register new farm" (Đăng ký trại mới) on Select Farm.
Description | As a farm owner, I want to register a farm, so that I can subscribe it and manage its herd separately from my other farms.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The Owner is signed in, the account is ACTIVE and Owner registration is complete.
Postconditions | POST-1: One new farm exists in PENDING_PAYMENT with exactly one OWNER membership for this account. / POST-2: The Owner is on plan selection for the new farm (SCR-58). / POST-3: No subscription, payment request or service period is created; other owned farms are unchanged. / POST-4: The farm has its default parameter version and starter catalogue (GBR-109).
Normal Flow | 1. System shows Register Farm (SCR-10) as step 2 of onboarding. / 2. System validates the fields and the request key (GBR-88). / 4. Exceptions | E1: Owner registration not complete: MSG-FE02-02 and Complete Owner Profile opens; no farm. / E2: Missing or invalid fields: MSG-COM-06 / MSG-COM-10; nothing is saved. / E3: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; no partial farm.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Once per farm.
Business Rules | GBR-05, GBR-12, GBR-13; BR-D04. One registration request creates at most one farm.
Other Information | Screens SCR-07, SCR-09, SCR-10, SCR-58. Related: UC-FE19-02.

[TABLE block 538]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE02-05 — Suspend or Restore Farm Service
Created By | Ducnv | Date Created | 17/10/2026
Primary Actor | Platform Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The PM selects "Suspend" or "Restore" (Tạm ngưng / Khôi phục) on a farm in Farm Service Overview.
Description | As a Platform Manager, I want to suspend a farm's service with a reason, or restore it, so that the platform can stop misuse without changing payments.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The PM is signed in with ACTIVE PM access. / PRE-2: Suspend: the farm is not SUSPENDED. Restore: the farm is SUSPENDED.
Postconditions | POST-1: Suspend: the farm is SUSPENDED; members lose farm operations at their next request; the Owner keeps farm information, subscription and support. / POST-2: Restore: the suspension is removed and the status is derived from the subscription (ACTIVE, EXPIRED or PENDING_PAYMENT). / POST-3: A Farm Service Change and an audit entry are saved and the Owner is notified; no payment, period or farm data changes.
Normal Flow | 1. PM selects Suspend or Restore on the farm. / 2. System saves the suspension flag change, the Farm Service Change and the audit entry in one transaction and derives the new status (GBR-13, GBR-15). / 6. System notifies the Owner (JOB-07) and shows MSG-FE02-08.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Cancel: nothing changes. / AF2 – Restore without a current period: the status becomes EXPIRED or PENDING_PAYMENT, not ACTIVE.
Exceptions | E1: No ACTIVE PM access: MSG-COM-01. / E2: Empty reason: MSG-COM-06. / E3: The farm is already in the requested state: MSG-FE02-09; no new record. / E4: Changed in another session: MSG-COM-05. / E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; status unchanged. / E6: Notification failure: the change is kept; the notice is retried without duplicates (GBR-64).
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Rare.
Business Rules | GBR-11, GBR-13, GBR-15, GBR-86; BR-D29.
Other Information | Screens SCR-12, popup "Suspend / Restore Farm Service". Related: UC-FE20-13 (when requested by CS).

UC ID and Name | UC-FE03-01 — Send Farm Member Invitation
Created By | Ducnv | Date Created | 17/10/2026

Exceptions | E1: Not the Owner of the farm: MSG-COM-01. / E2: Farm not ACTIVE: MSG-FE03-04. / E3: Email empty or invalid: MSG-COM-06 / MSG-COM-10. / E4: Email is already an ACTIVE member: MSG-FE03-02. / E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.

[P559 3] 4. FE-04 Pen Management
[P560 4] 4.1 Reassign Cattle to Another Pen
[TABLE block 561]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE04-05 — Reassign Cattle to Another Pen
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 21/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Owner, Farm Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The user selects "Move pen" (Chuyển chuồng) on a cattle profile (Pen History tab), on cattle selected in a pen's detail, or from a pen-move suggestion (REM-08).
Description | As a farm manager, I want to move one or several cattle to another pen with a date and reason, so that pen occupancy and each animal's pen history stay correct.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is the Owner or a Manager of an ACTIVE farm. / PRE-2: The selected cattle are ACTIVE (not EXITED, not draft calves).
Postconditions | POST-1: For each moved cattle the current assignment is closed at the move date and a new assignment is open in the destination pen. / POST-2: Occupancy of both pens is updated; warning reasons are stored with the assignment. / POST-3: A related REM-08 suggestion is resolved.
Normal Flow | 1. System shows popup "Move Pen" with the selected cattle (code, breed, sex, age, latest weight) and the current pen with occupancy. / 2. User selects the destination pen (list shows only ACTIVE pens with headcount/capacity), the move date (default today) and the reason; the performer defaults to the user. / 3. System runs the reception check for each cattle and shows the result table: pen ACTIVE and has enough free places; purpose type, life stage, sex and minimum age/weight against the destination criteria (GBR-21). / 4. User selects "Confirm move". / 5. System rechecks pen status, free places, each cattle's state and versions. / 6. System closes the current assignments and opens the new ones in one transaction (GBR-22), updates occupancy and pen history and resolves REM-08. / 7. System shows MSG-FE04-08.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Criteria not met (step 3): the result shows "Not met" for the failing criteria and MSG-FE04-06; the user must enter a warning reason to continue; the reason is saved with each affected assignment. / AF2 – Correction of a mistaken move: user selects the previous pen with reason "Correction"; the system records a reverse move. / AF3 – Cancel: nothing changes.
Exceptions | E1: Destination INACTIVE or not enough free places: MSG-FE04-05; the move is blocked. / E2: Move date before the start of the current assignment or in the future: MSG-FE04-09. / E3: Cattle already in the destination pen: MSG-FE04-07; that cattle is skipped. / E4: Cattle EXITED or changed meanwhile: MSG-COM-13 / MSG-COM-05. / E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; no assignment changes.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Several times a week.
Business Rules | GBR-20, GBR-21, GBR-22, GBR-26; BR-D09.
Other Information | Screens SCR-17, popup "Move Pen", SCR-20. Related: UC-FE04-01, UC-FE14-01.
Assumptions | —

[P563 3] 5. FE-05 Cattle Profile, Identification and State
[P564 4] 5.1 Update Cattle Profile
[TABLE block 565]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE05-03 — Update Cattle Profile
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 21/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Owner, Farm Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The user selects "Update profile" (Cập nhật hồ sơ) on a cattle profile, or opens a life-stage suggestion (REM-08) from Farm Reminders or the profile.
Description | As a farm owner or manager, I want to change a cattle's life stage, production purpose, ear tag or descriptive data with an effective date and reason, so that the profile is correct and its history explains every change.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is the Owner or a Manager of an ACTIVE farm. / PRE-2: The cattle is ACTIVE.
Postconditions | POST-1: The changed values are current and a Cattle Profile Change with old/new value, effective date, reason and actor is saved for each changed group. / POST-2: The herd group and the applicability of reproductive status are re-derived from the new stage or purpose type (GBR-24, GBR-54); pen, health state and reproduction events are unchanged. / POST-3: A related REM-08 life-stage suggestion is resolved.
Normal Flow | 1. System shows popup "Update Cattle Profile" with tabs Identification & description, Production purpose, Life stage. / 2. User selects a tab and enters the new values: life stage (new stage from UNWEANED_CALF, WEANED_CALF, YOUNG_STOCK, ADULT, effective date, reason); or ear tag number, photo, name, colour/markings and notes; or production purpose (new purpose with its purpose type shown, effective date, reason – Owner only). / 3. User selects Save. / 4. System validates the values, uniqueness of the ear tag among ACTIVE cattle, catalogue availability, the effective date (not in the future and not before herd entry), the user's right to change the group (GBR-25) and the version. / 5. System saves the new values and the change history in one transaction. / 6. System re-derives the herd group and reproduction applicability, re-evaluates reminders that depend on purpose or stage (REM-08) and shows MSG-FE05-01.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Manager opens the profile: the Production purpose tab is read-only with MSG-FE05-03. / AF2 – From a life-stage suggestion: the Life stage tab opens with the suggested stage, the effective date (the date the threshold was reached, or the calving date) and the reason proposed; the user confirms or edits them. / AF3 – Stage change that ends reproduction follow-up: when the new stage or purpose type makes reproductive status "Not applicable" while the female has no OPEN pregnancy, MSG-FE05-11 is shown before saving; reproduction history is kept. / AF4 – Cancel: nothing changes.
Exceptions | E1: Ear tag used by another ACTIVE cattle: MSG-FE05-02. / E2: Inactive catalogue value: MSG-FE18-08. / E3: Missing reason or effective date, or invalid date: MSG-COM-06 / MSG-COM-07 / MSG-COM-15. / E4: Cattle EXITED: MSG-COM-13. / E5: Changed in another session: MSG-COM-05. / E6: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.
Priority | Medium
Frequency of Use | Weekly.
Business Rules | GBR-23, GBR-24, GBR-25, GBR-26, GBR-54; BR-D08.
Other Information | Screens SCR-19, popup "Update Cattle Profile", SCR-44.
Assumptions | —

[P573 3] 6. FE-06 Herd Entry Management
[P574 4] 6.1 Register Herd Entry Voucher
[TABLE block 575]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE06-01 — Register Herd Entry Voucher
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 22/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Owner, Farm Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The user selects "Create entry voucher" (Tạo phiếu nhập) or "Continue" (Tiếp tục) on a DRAFT voucher in Herd Entry Vouchers, "Pending calves" (Bê chờ xác nhận) or reminder REM-09, or "Register now" while recording a calving (UC-FE12-04).
Description | As a farm owner or manager, I want to record every receipt of cattle as one voucher with its cattle, value and payment, so that each animal gets a permanent identity and a complete record from day one and the farm knows what it owes the seller.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is the Owner or a Manager of an ACTIVE farm. / PRE-2: At least one ACTIVE breed, production purpose and pen exist.
Postconditions | POST-1: The voucher is POSTED with its code, lines, totals and initial payment; for each line one cattle exists with a new Internal Cattle ID and display code, its herd entry, history declarations, first pen assignment and ACTIVE QR tag. / POST-2: For farm-born calves, each calf outcome is REGISTERED and linked to its cattle; REM-09 is resolved. / POST-3: Matching vaccination schedules are listed; no dose is created automatically. / POST-4: Until posting, a DRAFT voucher has no effect on cattle, pens, payments or reminders.
Normal Flow | 1. System shows Herd Entry Voucher – Purchase (SCR-24) with the next voucher code, entry type Purchase and today as entry date. / 2. User enters the voucher header: entry date, seller (chosen from the trading partners or added inline), quarantine certificate number (optional; a warning is shown when empty, MSG-FE06-24), vehicle plate and note. / 3. User chooses the pricing basis (per kg or per head) and selects "+ Add cattle"; System opens popup "Voucher Cattle Line". / 4. User enters ear tag number (or leaves it empty to tag later), name, photo, colour and markings, breed, sex, birth date or month with accuracy (or the estimated age in months), life stage (proposed from the age at entry, GBR-25), production purpose, entry weight with basis, receiving pen, unit price and optional quarantine end date, and the pre-entry history (vaccination, disease, reproduction with earlier calvings and pregnancy at entry: KNOWN / NONE / UNKNOWN, default UNKNOWN, GBR-30); selects "Save line". Steps 3–4 repeat for each cattle. / 5. System shows the lines with line amounts, totals (cattle, weight, amount, estimated weights marked UT), the occupancy change of each receiving pen and the pre-save checks (ear tags unique, pen criteria and capacity, entry date not in the future and not before birth dates). / 6. User attaches documents (quarantine certificate, invoice, photos) and enters the transport fee, amount paid, payment method and due date of the balance; System shows total payable and outstanding (GBR-91). / 7. User selects "Save & create n cattle" (Lưu & tạo hồ sơ n con), optionally with "Print QR tags after saving". / 8. System rechecks every line, the header and the version and, in one transaction, posts the voucher, creates each cattle with its herd entry, history declarations (known vaccinations as HISTORICAL vaccination records), first pen assignment and QR tag, records the initial payment and saves the audit entry (GBR-90, GBR-32). / 9. System shows MSG-FE06-01, opens Herd Entry Voucher Detail (SCR-75) and, when schedules match, MSG-FE06-05 with links to add the cattle to them (UC-FE09-03).
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Farm-born calves (type "Farm-born calves" or trigger from pending calves / REM-09): System shows SCR-25 with the PENDING_CONFIRMATION calves; dam, sire, breed, sex, birth date and birth weight are read-only (GBR-31). User selects the calves, checks the tag, receiving pen and production purpose of each (default: the first ACTIVE purpose of type Undecided) and continues at step 7; the entry date of each calf is its birth date, its life stage UNWEANED_CALF and the voucher has no amounts (GBR-93). / AF2 – Register now from a calving (UC-FE12-04): System creates and posts a FARM_BORN voucher for the calf in the calving transaction. / AF3 – Import lines from Excel (step 3): User selects "Import from Excel" and uploads the template; System opens popup "Import Cattle from Excel" with the result of each row (valid, warning, error, GBR-96); User selects "Add n cattle to the voucher"; only valid rows are added (MSG-FE06-18, MSG-FE06-19). / AF4 – Save draft (any step): System saves the voucher as DRAFT (MSG-FE06-10); no cattle is created; the Owner and Managers can continue it later. / AF5 – Edit or remove a line (step 5): User opens the line in popup "Voucher Cattle Line" and saves or removes it. / AF6 – Pen warnings: a criteria warning needs a warning reason (MSG-FE04-06); a pen that becomes full is shown with MSG-FE06-17. / AF7 – Mark a calf as died before registration (AF1): User enters the reason; the calf outcome is closed and REM-09 resolved. / AF8 – Discard draft / Cancel: User discards a DRAFT voucher or leaves without saving (MSG-COM-14).
Exceptions | E1: No cattle line: MSG-FE06-16. / E2: Entry date in the future or before a birth date: MSG-FE06-03. / E3: Ear tag or tag code already used: MSG-FE05-02 / MSG-FE12-08. / E4: Pen INACTIVE or without enough places for its lines: MSG-FE04-05. / E5: Calf already registered or not LIVE: MSG-FE06-02 / MSG-FE06-04. / E6: Amount paid greater than the total payable: MSG-FE06-13. / E7: Missing seller or other required field: MSG-COM-06. / E8: Inactive catalogue value: MSG-FE18-08. / E9: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; nothing is created and the voucher stays DRAFT.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Weekly; several times during calving season.
Business Rules | GBR-20, GBR-21, GBR-25, GBR-29, GBR-30, GBR-31, GBR-32, GBR-58, GBR-90, GBR-91, GBR-92, GBR-93, GBR-96, GBR-97; BR-D07, BR-D08, BR-D10, BR-D34, BR-D39.
Other Information | Screens SCR-23, SCR-24, SCR-25, popup "Voucher Cattle Line", popup "Import Cattle from Excel", SCR-75. Related: UC-FE06-04, UC-FE12-04, UC-FE09-03, UC-FE05-04.
Assumptions | A voucher is entered by one user at a time; the voucher version prevents silent overwrites.
[/TABLE]
[P577 4] 6.2 Correct Herd Entry Record
[TABLE block 578]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE06-03 — Correct Herd Entry Record
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 22/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Owner, Farm Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The user selects "Correct herd entry" (Sửa bản ghi nhập đàn) on the cattle profile or on a cattle line of Herd Entry Voucher Detail (SCR-75).
Description | As a farm owner or manager, I want to correct wrong entry data of one cattle, or void its entry when it was recorded by mistake, so that the herd record and the voucher are accurate while the original values remain traceable.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is the Owner or a Manager of an ACTIVE farm. / PRE-2: The cattle is ACTIVE and its herd entry is VALID on a POSTED voucher.
Postconditions | POST-1: Correct: the corrected values are current, voucher totals are recalculated when the weight or price changed, and a Record Revision keeps the old values, reason, actor and time; the Internal Cattle ID is unchanged. / POST-2: Void: the herd entry is VOIDED, the cattle gets herd status VOIDED and is removed from active lists and counts, its pen assignment is closed and its tag becomes RETIRED, its line amount is removed from the voucher total and its identifiers are never reused.
Normal Flow | 1. System shows popup "Correct / Void Herd Entry" with the entry (cattle, voucher, entry date, recorder) and the action choice "Correct record" / "Void record". / 2. User keeps "Correct record" and enters new values for the fields to correct: entry date (for a purchase voucher it applies to all its cattle), entry weight and basis, unit price, breed, sex, birth date and accuracy, history declarations. / 3. User enters the reason and selects Save. / 4. System validates the new values against later events (entry date not after any recorded event of the cattle concerned; sex not changed when the cattle has reproductive events; birth date not after the entry date), the voucher payments (BR-D39) and the version. / 5. System saves the corrected values, the recalculated voucher totals and the Record Revision in one transaction (GBR-33) and shows MSG-FE06-06.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Void record (step 2): System checks that the cattle has no recorded event other than its herd entry and first pen placement; user enters the reason and confirms MSG-FE06-08; System voids the entry, closes the pen assignment and tag, recalculates the voucher and shows MSG-FE06-12. When it is the last VALID line, the user is directed to UC-FE06-05. / AF2 – Paid amount above the new total: the change is saved and MSG-FE06-23 is shown; the difference is settled outside BeefCare. / AF3 – Cancel: nothing changes.
Exceptions | E1: Correction breaks the date order or changes the sex of a cattle with reproductive events: MSG-FE06-07. / E2: Void not allowed because events exist: MSG-FE06-09. / E3: Missing reason: MSG-COM-06. / E4: Changed in another session: MSG-COM-05. / E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.
Priority | Medium
Frequency of Use | Rare.
Business Rules | GBR-33, GBR-91; BR-D07, BR-D15, BR-D39.
Other Information | Screens popup "Correct / Void Herd Entry", SCR-75. Related: UC-FE06-05, UC-FE05-03.
Assumptions | —
[/TABLE]
[P580 4] 6.3 Void Herd Entry Voucher
[TABLE block 581]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE06-05 — Void Herd Entry Voucher
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 22/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Owner, Farm Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The user selects "Void voucher" (Hủy phiếu) on Herd Entry Voucher Detail (SCR-75).
Description | As a farm owner or manager, I want to void a whole entry voucher recorded by mistake, so that cattle that never entered the herd disappear from active lists while the mistake stays traceable.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is the Owner or a Manager of an ACTIVE farm. / PRE-2: The voucher is POSTED.
Postconditions | POST-1: The voucher is VOIDED with reason, actor and time; every VALID herd entry of the voucher is VOIDED, each cattle gets herd status VOIDED and is removed from active lists, its pen assignment is closed and its QR tag withdrawn; identifiers are never reused. / POST-2: Recorded payments stay in the voucher history; farm-born calves return to PENDING_CONFIRMATION and REM-09 is raised again.

System rechecks that no cattle of the voucher has an event other than its herd entry and first pen placement (GBR-33), the voucher state and version. / 5. System voids the voucher and its entries in one transaction, saves the audit entry and shows MSG-FE06-22.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Some cattle have other events: the void button is disabled and MSG-FE06-21 names them; the user corrects or voids those entries one by one (UC-FE06-03). / AF2 – Close the dialog: nothing changes.
Exceptions | E1: Missing reason: MSG-COM-06. / E2: Voucher already VOIDED or changed: MSG-COM-05. / E3: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; nothing changes.
Priority | Low
Frequency of Use | Rare.
Business Rules | GBR-33, GBR-90, GBR-91; BR-D07, BR-D15.

Trigger | The Manager selects "Record weight & BCS" (Ghi nhận cân & BCS) on the Growth & BCS tab of a cattle profile, or from a weighing reminder (REM-01).
Description | As a farm manager, I want to record an animal's weight and/or body condition score with how it was measured, so that growth can be followed honestly.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is a Manager of an ACTIVE farm. / PRE-2: The cattle is ACTIVE and registered (not a draft calf).
Postconditions | POST-1: A VALID growth assessment is saved with measured time, entry time and recorder. / POST-2: Current weight, ADG and growth reminders are recalculated; REM-01 is resolved and a pen-move or life-stage suggestion (REM-08) may be raised.
Normal Flow | 1. System shows popup "Record Weight and BCS" with the cattle (read-only), measured time (default now) and measurer (default the user). / 2. System saves the assessment and the timeline entry. / 6. System recalculates current weight and ADG (GBR-39), resolves REM-01, re-evaluates REM-08 (JOB-01) and shows MSG-FE08-01.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Large difference from the last weight: when the new weight differs by more than 20 % from the latest valid weight within 30 days, MSG-FE08-04 asks the Manager to confirm before saving. / AF2 – BCS only: no weight fields are required. / AF3 – Measured by a Technician: the Manager selects the Technician as measurer; the Manager remains the recorder. / AF4 – Cancel: nothing is saved.
Exceptions | E1: Neither weight nor BCS: MSG-FE08-02. / E2: Weight not positive: MSG-FE08-03. / E3: Measured time in the future or before herd entry: MSG-COM-07 / MSG-COM-15. / E4: Cattle EXITED: MSG-COM-13. / E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Daily on the farm; monthly per animal.
Business Rules | GBR-08, GBR-26, GBR-38, GBR-39; BR-D14, BR-D16, BR-D20.
Other Information | Screens SCR-29, SCR-30, popup "Record Weight and BCS". Related: UC-FE14-01.

[P594 4] 8.2 Correct Growth Assessment Record
[TABLE block 595]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE08-03 — Correct Growth Assessment Record
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 21/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The Manager selects "Edit · Exclude" (Sửa · Loại trừ) on a row of the measurement history.
Description | As a farm manager, I want to correct or exclude a wrong measurement with a reason, so that charts and indicators are right while the original stays in the history.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is a Manager of an ACTIVE farm. / PRE-2: The cattle is ACTIVE and the record is VALID.
Postconditions | POST-1: Correct: the record shows the new values with a "Corrected" marker and stays VALID; a Record Revision keeps the old values. / POST-2: Exclude: the record is EXCLUDED and no longer used in calculations. / POST-3: Indicators and reminders are recalculated.
Normal Flow | 1. System shows popup "Correct / Exclude Growth Record" with the record (values, method, measurer) and the action choice. / 2. Manager keeps "Correct values" and enters the new weight, BCS, method or measured time. / 3. Manager enters the reason and selects Save. / 4. System validates the new values as in UC-FE08-01 and the version. / 5. System saves the correction and the Record Revision in one transaction, recalculates indicators and shows MSG-FE08-05.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Exclude record (step 2): the Manager selects "Exclude record", enters the reason and saves; the record becomes EXCLUDED. / AF2 – Cancel: nothing changes.
Exceptions | E1: Missing reason: MSG-COM-06. / E2: Invalid values: MSG-FE08-02 / MSG-FE08-03 / MSG-COM-07. / E3: Cattle EXITED: MSG-COM-13. / E4: Changed in another session: MSG-COM-05. / E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.
Priority | Medium
Frequency of Use | Rare.
Business Rules | GBR-08, GBR-39, GBR-40; BR-D15.
Other Information | Screens SCR-30, popup "Correct / Exclude Growth Record".
Assumptions | —
[/TABLE]
[P597 3] 9. FE-09 Vaccination Schedule and Recording
[P598 4] 9.1 Create Vaccination Schedule
[TABLE block 599]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE09-02 — Create Vaccination Schedule
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 21/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The Manager selects "Manage schedule" → "New schedule" (Quản lý lịch) on Vaccination Schedule.
Description | As a farm manager, I want to plan a vaccine for a defined group of cattle with its doses and booster, so that every planned dose is reminded and tracked per animal.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is a Manager of an ACTIVE farm. / PRE-2: The vaccine exists and is ACTIVE in the farm catalogue.
Postconditions | POST-1: An ACTIVE schedule exists with one PLANNED dose per target cattle and dose number. / POST-2: Vaccination reminders (REM-06) are evaluated for the new doses.
Normal Flow | 1. System shows Manage Vaccination Schedule (SCR-32) for a new schedule. / 2. Manager enters the schedule name, selects the vaccine (the reference disease, dose, route and withdrawal are shown), the start date, the number of doses, the days between doses and the optional booster interval in months. / 3. Manager selects the target: one or more pens, herd groups and life stages (GBR-24); the System shows the number of matching ACTIVE cattle and the list, from which the Manager may remove or add individual cattle. / 4. System shows the planned doses table (dose number, planned date, number of cattle). / 5. Manager selects "Save schedule". / 6. System validates the fields, the vaccine availability, the target (at least one cattle) and name uniqueness among ACTIVE schedules. / 7. System saves the schedule and the PLANNED doses in one transaction (GBR-41), triggers JOB-01 and shows MSG-FE09-01.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Cattle already received the same vaccine dose recently: the System marks them in the list; the Manager decides to keep or remove them. / AF2 – Cancel: nothing is saved. / AF3 – Booster: when the last dose of the series (or a booster) is recorded, the System plans the next booster at administered date + booster interval while the schedule is ACTIVE.
Exceptions | E1: Missing required field or no cattle match the target: MSG-COM-06 / MSG-FE09-07. / E2: Vaccine inactive: MSG-FE18-08. / E3: Name already used by an ACTIVE schedule: MSG-FE09-10. / E4: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Several times a year.
Business Rules | GBR-08, GBR-41, GBR-43; BR-D17.
Other Information | Screens SCR-31, SCR-32. Related: UC-FE09-03, UC-FE09-04, UC-FE14-01.
Assumptions | New cattle that later match the target are added only by the Manager (GBR-32).
[/TABLE]
[P601 4] 9.2 Record Vaccination
[TABLE block 602]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE09-05 — Record Vaccination
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 21/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The Manager selects "Record vaccination" (Ghi nhận tiêm) on Vaccination Schedule (for selected doses) or on the Vaccination tab of a cattle profile.
Description | As a farm manager, I want to record the vaccinations actually given, for one animal or a batch, so that each animal's vaccination history and schedule status are correct.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is a Manager of an ACTIVE farm. / PRE-2: The selected cattle are ACTIVE and registered.
Postconditions | POST-1: One VALID vaccination record exists per vaccinated cattle; matching scheduled doses are DONE and next doses (and boosters) are planned. / POST-2: Cattle marked "Defer" have DEFERRED doses with reason and new date. / POST-3: Reminders are updated (REM-06). / POST-4: For on-farm vaccination with withdrawal days above 0, each vaccinated cattle's meat withdrawal end date is updated (GBR-46).
Normal Flow | 1. System shows Record Vaccination (SCR-33) with the schedule and dose (when started from a schedule) and the selected cattle (code, pen). / 2. Manager confirms or selects the vaccine and enters the batch number, administration time (default now), dose, route, withdrawal days (proposed from the vaccine, editable), vaccinator and source "On-farm vaccination". / 3. For each cattle the result is "Vaccinate" by default; the Manager may switch a cattle to "Defer" and enter the reason and new expected date. / 4. System shows the summary (vaccinated, deferred). / 5. Manager selects "Save n vaccinations". / 6. System validates the fields, each cattle's state, that the dose is not already DONE and the versions. / 7. System saves, in one transaction, one vaccination record per vaccinated cattle, marks the doses DONE, recalculates the next dose dates, saves the deferrals (GBR-42, GBR-43), updates each cattle's meat withdrawal end date (GBR-46) and adds the timeline entries. / 8. System triggers JOB-01 and shows MSG-FE09-04.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Single cattle from the profile: the same dialog with one cattle; when the cattle has a matching PLANNED or DEFERRED dose, it is linked automatically. / AF2 – Historical vaccination: the Manager selects source "Historical entry"; batch number and exact time may be unknown; no scheduled dose is closed and no withdrawal is calculated. / AF3 – Reaction after vaccination: the Manager opens a health case linked to the record (UC-FE10-02). / AF4 – Cancel: nothing is saved.
Exceptions | E1: Batch number missing for on-farm vaccination: MSG-FE09-05. / E2: Administration time in the future or before herd entry: MSG-COM-07 / MSG-COM-15. / E3: Dose already done for a cattle: MSG-FE09-09; that cattle is skipped. / E4: Vaccine inactive: MSG-FE18-08. / E5: A cattle EXITED meanwhile: MSG-COM-13; that cattle is skipped. / E6: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; nothing is saved.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Weekly; seasonal campaigns.
Business Rules | GBR-08, GBR-26, GBR-42, GBR-43, GBR-46; BR-D14, BR-D17.
Other Information | Screens SCR-31, SCR-33, SCR-34. Related: UC-FE09-06, UC-FE10-02.
Assumptions | —
[/TABLE]

[P610 3] 10. FE-10 Health Case and Treatment Management
[P611 4] 10.1 Open Health Case
[TABLE block 612]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE10-02 — Open Health Case
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 21/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The Manager selects "Open health case" (Mở ca sức khỏe) on the Health tab of a cattle profile, or "Record reaction" on a vaccination record.
Description | As a farm manager, I want to record abnormal signs of an animal and open or update a health case, so that its health status, treatments and outcome are tracked in one place.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is a Manager of an ACTIVE farm. / PRE-2: The cattle is ACTIVE and registered.
Postconditions | POST-1: A new OPEN case with its opening observation exists, or the observation is added to the selected OPEN or CHRONIC case. / POST-2: The health state is recalculated (GBR-45): a new case without treatment makes it at least "Monitoring".
Normal Flow | 1. System shows popup "Open Health Case" with the cattle (read-only), observed time (default now) and observer (default the user). / 2. Manager enters the body temperature (optional), the signs/description, an optional suspected diagnosis and contagious flag, and the severity (mild, moderate, severe), and selects "Open new case" (default) or "Add to an existing case" with the case selected from the cattle's OPEN and CHRONIC cases. / 3. Manager optionally selects a Technician as observer and selects "Save". / 4. System validates the fields and cattle state. / 5. System creates the case with a case code (HC-nnnn) and its opening observation, or adds the observation to the selected case, in one transaction. / 6. System updates the health state and shows MSG-FE10-01.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Reaction after vaccination: the vaccination record is linked to the case (GBR-47). / AF2 – Cancel: nothing is saved.
Exceptions | E1: Missing signs or severity: MSG-COM-06. / E2: Temperature out of range: MSG-FE10-06. / E3: Observed time in the future or before herd entry: MSG-COM-07 / MSG-COM-15. / E4: Cattle EXITED: MSG-COM-13. / E5: Selected case already CLOSED: MSG-COM-05. / E6: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Several times a week.
Business Rules | GBR-08, GBR-45, GBR-47; BR-D14.
Other Information | Screens SCR-36, SCR-37, popup "Open Health Case". Related: UC-FE10-03, UC-FE10-04.
Assumptions | BeefCare records observations; it does not diagnose.
[/TABLE]
[P614 4] 10.2 Update Health Case Status and Observation
[TABLE block 615]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE10-03 — Update Health Case Status and Observation
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 21/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The Manager selects "Record follow-up" (Ghi theo dõi) or "Update status" (Cập nhật trạng thái) on an OPEN or CHRONIC case.
Description | As a farm manager, I want to record the progress of a case and close it with an outcome, so that the animal's health history is complete and deaths are passed to the Owner.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is a Manager of an ACTIVE farm. / PRE-2: The case is OPEN or CHRONIC and the cattle is ACTIVE.
Postconditions | POST-1: Follow-up: a follow-up observation is added to the case. / POST-2: Close: the case is CLOSED with outcome, closing time and closer; the health state is recalculated. / POST-3: Outcome DIED or MISSING: a PENDING death voucher is created for the Owner (GBR-95); the cattle stays ACTIVE. / POST-4: Chronic monitoring: the case is CHRONIC with its "chronic since" date, or OPEN again; the health state is recalculated (GBR-45).
Normal Flow | 1. Manager selects "Record follow-up"; System shows popup "Record Follow-up" with the case. / 2. Manager enters the time, optional temperature, the progress (improved, unchanged, worsened) and the description and selects Save. / 3. System validates and saves the observation; MSG-FE10-02. / 4. Later the Manager selects "Update status"; System shows popup "Update / Close Health Case". / 5. Manager enters the latest progress, selects "Close case", the outcome (recovered, died, missing), the closing time and the confirming person, and selects Save. / 6. System validates the outcome, time (not before the last observation or treatment) and version. / 7. System closes the case and recalculates the health state in one transaction; MSG-FE10-04.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Keep OPEN or Keep CHRONIC (step 5): the Manager saves the progress without changing the status; only an observation is added. / AF2 – Move to chronic monitoring (step 5): for an OPEN case the Manager selects "Move to chronic monitoring"; the System sets the case CHRONIC with the date, recalculates the health state (Monitoring when no other OPEN case has a treatment) and shows MSG-FE10-08. / AF3 – Return to OPEN (step 5): for a CHRONIC case the Manager selects "Return to OPEN"; the System sets the case OPEN, recalculates the health state and shows MSG-FE10-09. / AF4 – Outcome DIED or MISSING (step 7): in the same transaction the System creates a PENDING herd exit (type DEATH, or OTHER with reason "Missing") linked to the case and notifies the Owner (GBR-47); MSG-FE10-05. / AF5 – Cancel: nothing changes.
Exceptions | E1: Missing progress, description or outcome: MSG-COM-06. / E2: Time in the future or before earlier case entries: MSG-COM-07 / MSG-FE10-07. / E3: Case already CLOSED or changed: MSG-COM-05. / E4: Cattle EXITED: MSG-COM-13. / E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Daily while cases are open.
Business Rules | GBR-08, GBR-34, GBR-45, GBR-47; BR-D11, BR-D33.
Other Information | Screens SCR-37, popup "Record Follow-up", popup "Update / Close Health Case". Related: UC-FE13-01.
Assumptions | —
[/TABLE]
[P617 4] 10.3 Record Treatment
[TABLE block 618]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE10-04 — Record Treatment
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 21/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The Manager selects "Record treatment" (Ghi điều trị) on an OPEN or CHRONIC case.
Description | As a farm manager, I want to record each treatment actually given with its withdrawal period, so that treatment history and meat-safety dates are known.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is a Manager of an ACTIVE farm. / PRE-2: The case is OPEN or CHRONIC and the cattle is ACTIVE.
Postconditions | POST-1: A treatment record is saved in the case with the withdrawal end date. / POST-2: The cattle's meat withdrawal end date is updated (GBR-46). / POST-3: The case is OPEN (a CHRONIC case returns to OPEN) and the health state is "Under treatment" (GBR-45).
Normal Flow | 1. System shows popup "Record Treatment" with the case. / 2. Manager selects the medicine from the farm catalogue (reference dose, route and withdrawal days are proposed), enters the dose given, route, administration time (default now), course days (default 1), withdrawal days (editable), performer and optional veterinary advice. / 3. System shows the withdrawal end date = last administration (administration time + course days − 1) + withdrawal days. / 4. Manager selects "Save treatment". / 5. System validates the fields, the case and cattle state. / 6. System saves the treatment and timeline entry, updates the withdrawal end date, recalculates the health state and shows MSG-FE10-03.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Daily course: a course of the same medicine, dose and route is recorded once with its number of days; a change of medicine or dose is recorded as a new treatment. / AF2 – Treatment on a CHRONIC case: MSG-FE10-10 is shown in the dialog; on save the case returns to OPEN in the same transaction. / AF3 – Cancel: nothing is saved.
Exceptions | E1: Missing medicine, dose, route or time: MSG-COM-06. / E2: Medicine inactive: MSG-FE18-08. / E3: Time in the future or before the case was opened: MSG-COM-07 / MSG-FE10-07. / E4: Case CLOSED or cattle EXITED: MSG-COM-05 / MSG-COM-13. / E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Daily while cases are open.
Business Rules | GBR-08, GBR-45, GBR-46; BR-D14.
Other Information | Screens SCR-37, popup "Record Treatment".
Assumptions | Stock of medicines is not tracked.
[/TABLE]
[P620 4] 10.4 Correct or Void Treatment or Follow-up
[TABLE block 621]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE10-05 — Correct or Void Treatment or Follow-up
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 28/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The Manager selects "Edit · Void" (Sửa · Hủy) on a treatment or follow-up row in the Health tab of the cattle profile.
Description | As a farm manager, I want to correct a wrong treatment or follow-up, or void one recorded by mistake, so that the treatment history and the meat withdrawal date are right before any sale.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is a Manager of an ACTIVE farm. / PRE-2: The record is VALID, its case is not CLOSED_BY_EXIT and the cattle is ACTIVE and not on a PENDING death voucher for a new event; corrections remain possible on a PENDING death voucher (GBR-26).
Postconditions | POST-1: Correct: the record shows the new values with a "Corrected" marker and stays VALID; a Record Revision keeps the old values, reason, actor and time. / POST-2: Void: the record is VOIDED, greyed in the history and excluded from calculations. / POST-3: The cattle's meat withdrawal end date and health state are recalculated from the VALID records (GBR-45, GBR-46); reminders and the herd exit checks use the new values.
Normal Flow | 1. System shows popup "Correct / Void Treatment or Follow-up" with the record (type, case, time, recorder) and the action choice. / 2. Manager keeps "Correct record" and edits the fields allowed for the record type: treatment – medicine, dose, route, first administration time, course days, withdrawal days, performer; follow-up – observed time, temperature, progress, description, observer. / 3. System shows the meat withdrawal end date before and after the change. / 4. Manager enters the reason and selects Save. / 5. System validates the values (as in UC-FE10-04 or UC-FE10-03), chronology within the case and the version. / 6. System saves the correction and the Record Revision, recalculates the withdrawal end date and health state in one transaction and shows MSG-FE10-11, with MSG-FE10-12 when the withdrawal end date changed.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Void record (step 2): the Manager selects "Void record", enters the reason and confirms; the System voids the record, recalculates the withdrawal end date and health state and shows MSG-FE10-11 (and MSG-FE10-12). / AF2 – Last treatment of an OPEN case voided: the health state becomes Monitoring when no other OPEN case has a treatment (GBR-45). / AF3 – Opening observation: the observation that opened the case can be corrected but not voided (MSG-FE10-13); a case opened by mistake is closed with a reason through UC-FE10-03. / AF4 – Cancel: nothing changes.
Exceptions | E1: Missing reason or required field: MSG-COM-06. / E2: Invalid values or time: MSG-COM-07 / MSG-FE10-06 / MSG-FE10-07. / E3: Cattle EXITED or VOIDED: MSG-COM-13 / MSG-COM-18. / E4: Changed in another session: MSG-COM-05. / E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; nothing changes.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Rare.
Business Rules | GBR-08, GBR-26, GBR-45, GBR-46; BR-D15.
Other Information | Screen SCR-37, popup "Correct / Void Treatment or Follow-up".
[/TABLE]
[P623 3] 11. FE-11 Estrus and Breeding Recording
[P624 4] 11.1 Record Estrus Event
[TABLE block 625]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE11-02 — Record Estrus Event
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 21/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The Manager selects "Record estrus" (Ghi động dục) on the Reproduction tab of a female's profile.
Description | As a farm manager, I want to record an observed estrus, so that the breeding window is suggested and the reproduction history is complete.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is a Manager of an ACTIVE farm. / PRE-2: The cattle is an ACTIVE, registered female and its reproductive status offers this action (GBR-54).
Postconditions | POST-1: A VALID estrus event exists with observed time, observer, description, recorder and entry time. / POST-2: The reproductive status becomes IN_ESTRUS; the breeding window reminder REM-02 is created when the parameters exist; a TENTATIVE expected calving date of an earlier breeding is cancelled (GBR-53).
