System validates the time (not in the future, not before herd entry) and checks for a possible duplicate (same female within 12 hours). / 4. System saves the event, updates the reproductive status and reminders (JOB-01) and shows MSG-FE11-01 with the suggested breeding window.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Possible duplicate (step 3): MSG-FE11-03 asks whether to save anyway; No returns to the form. / AF2 – Female PREGNANT: the action is not offered; if a pregnancy exists when saving, MSG-FE11-05 asks the Manager to review the status first. / AF3 – Breeding parameters not configured: the event is saved; MSG-FE11-08 explains that no window is suggested. / AF4 – Cancel: nothing is saved.
Exceptions | E1: Missing observed time: MSG-COM-06. / E2: Time in the future or before herd entry: MSG-COM-07 / MSG-COM-15. / E3: Cattle EXITED: MSG-COM-13. / E4: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Several times a week.
Business Rules | GBR-08, GBR-48, GBR-49, GBR-53, GBR-54, GBR-63.
Other Information | Screens SCR-38, SCR-39, popup "Record Estrus". REM-02.
Assumptions | Estrus is observed by people; no sensor input.
[/TABLE]
[P627 4] 11.2 Record Breeding Event
[TABLE block 628]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE11-03 — Record Breeding Event
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 21/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The Manager selects "Record breeding" (Ghi phối giống) on the Reproduction tab of a female in status AWAITING_BREEDING, IN_ESTRUS or POST_CALVING (GBR-54).
Description | As a farm manager, I want to record a breeding with method and sire or semen, so that a tentative expected calving date and the pregnancy-check reminder are prepared.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is a Manager of an ACTIVE farm. / PRE-2: The cattle is an ACTIVE, registered female to which reproductive status applies, without an OPEN pregnancy episode (GBR-54).
Postconditions | POST-1: A VALID breeding event exists; the female is BRED. / POST-2: A TENTATIVE expected calving date (breeding time + gestation length) is shown and REM-03 is created when parameters exist; an earlier tentative date is replaced.
Normal Flow | 1. System shows popup "Record Breeding" with the female (read-only), the latest estrus of the last 72 hours proposed as link and the breeding eligibility indicator with each condition (GBR-55). / 2. Manager enters bred time, data source, method (natural service or AI) and sire source (internal or external). / 3. Internal sire: Manager selects a male ACTIVE cattle of the farm. External / AI: Manager enters sire breed, semen straw/batch code and supplier. / 4. Manager selects the performer (person or AI technician name) and an optional note; the System shows the tentative expected calving date. / 5. Manager selects "Save breeding". / 6. System validates the fields, chronology (not before the linked estrus, not in the future), the sire (different male of the same farm) and the female's state. / 7. System saves the event, sets the female BRED, the tentative date and REM-03 (JOB-01) and shows MSG-FE11-02.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – No observed estrus: the Manager clears the estrus link; no estrus is invented (GBR-50). / AF2 – Breeding eligibility Not met: MSG-FE11-09 is shown; the Manager may still save (GBR-55). / AF3 – Second insemination in the same estrus: allowed; the latest breeding is the basis of the tentative date. / AF4 – Cancel: nothing is saved.
Exceptions | E1: Missing required field: MSG-COM-06. / E2: Internal sire not a different male of the farm: MSG-FE11-04. / E3: Female has an OPEN pregnancy: MSG-FE11-05. / E4: Time invalid: MSG-COM-07 / MSG-FE11-10. / E5: Gestation parameter missing: saved without expected date (MSG-FE11-08). / E6: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Several times a week.
Business Rules | GBR-08, GBR-48, GBR-49, GBR-50, GBR-51, GBR-53, GBR-54, GBR-55.
Other Information | Screens SCR-39, popup "Record Breeding". REM-03.
Assumptions | —
[/TABLE]
[P630 4] 11.3 Correct or Void Estrus and Breeding Record
[TABLE block 631]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE11-04 — Correct or Void Estrus and Breeding Record
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 21/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The Manager selects "Edit · Void" (Sửa · Hủy) on an estrus or breeding row of the reproduction timeline.
Description | As a farm manager, I want to correct or void a wrong estrus or breeding record with a reason, so that reproduction data and dates stay correct without losing history.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is a Manager of an ACTIVE farm. / PRE-2: The record is VALID and the female is ACTIVE.
Postconditions | POST-1: Correct: the record has the new values with a "Corrected" marker and stays VALID; a Record Revision keeps the old values. / POST-2: Void: the record is VOIDED and excluded from status and dates. / POST-3: Reproductive status, tentative date and reminders are recalculated.
Normal Flow | 1. System shows popup "Correct / Void Estrus or Breeding" with the record and the action choice. / 2. Manager keeps "Correct record" and changes the allowed fields (estrus: observed time, observer, description; breeding: time, source, method, sire, semen, performer, linked estrus). / 3. Manager enters the reason and selects Save. / 4. System checks the version and the dependencies: a breeding linked to a pregnancy check or episode cannot change in a way that contradicts them (GBR-59). / 5. System saves the change and the Record Revision in one transaction, recalculates status, dates and reminders and shows MSG-FE11-06.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Void record (step 2): the Manager selects "Void record", enters the reason and confirms; dependencies are checked as in step 4. / AF2 – Cancel: nothing changes.
Exceptions | E1: Dependent records block the change: MSG-FE11-07 lists them. / E2: Missing reason: MSG-COM-06. / E3: Invalid values: as in UC-FE11-02 / UC-FE11-03. / E4: Changed in another session: MSG-COM-05. / E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.
Priority | Medium
Frequency of Use | Rare.
Business Rules | GBR-08, GBR-59; BR-D15.
Other Information | Screens SCR-39, popup "Correct / Void Estrus or Breeding".
Assumptions | —
[/TABLE]
[P633 3] 12. FE-12 Pregnancy and Calving Recording
[P634 4] 12.1 Record Pregnancy Check Result
[TABLE block 635]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE12-02 — Record Pregnancy Check Result
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 21/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The Manager selects "Record pregnancy check" (Ghi khám thai) on the Reproduction tab of a female in status BRED or PREGNANT, from "More actions" for any ACTIVE female at stage Young stock or Adult (GBR-54), or from reminder REM-03 / REM-04.
Description | As a farm manager, I want to record the result of a pregnancy examination, so that pregnancy is confirmed only by an actual check and the calving date becomes reliable.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is a Manager of an ACTIVE farm. / PRE-2: The cattle is an ACTIVE, registered female.
Postconditions | POST-1: A VALID pregnancy check is saved. / POST-2: PREGNANT: a pregnancy episode is OPEN (or confirmed) with a CONFIRMED expected calving date and REM-05. NOT_PREGNANT: the female returns to OPEN and the tentative date is cancelled. INCONCLUSIVE: status unchanged and REM-04 is created. / POST-3: REM-03 is resolved.
Normal Flow | 1. System shows popup "Record Pregnancy Check" with the female (read-only) and the latest breeding proposed as link. / 2. Manager enters checked time, result (pregnant, not pregnant, inconclusive), method (ultrasound, rectal, other), estimated months pregnant (optional), examiner and optional note; the data source is Direct observation unless changed; for PREGNANT the System shows the expected calving date from the linked breeding or from the estimated months (GBR-53). / 3. Manager selects "Save check result". / 4. System validates the fields, chronology (not before the linked breeding) and the female's state; it serializes changes for the female (GBR-52). / 5. System saves the check and applies the result (GBR-51, GBR-53) in one transaction, updates reminders (JOB-01) and shows MSG-FE12-01.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – No linked breeding: a PREGNANT result opens an episode with an ESTIMATED expected date from the estimated months, or without expected date when no months are entered (MSG-FE12-11). / AF2 – PREGNANT while an episode is already OPEN: the check confirms the existing episode. / AF3 – Cancel: nothing is saved.
Exceptions | E1: Missing required field: MSG-COM-06. / E2: Conflict with an existing check or newer episode: MSG-FE12-02. / E3: Time invalid: MSG-COM-07 / MSG-FE11-10. / E4: Cattle EXITED: MSG-COM-13. / E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Weekly.
Business Rules | GBR-08, GBR-49, GBR-50, GBR-51, GBR-52, GBR-53; BR-D18.
Other Information | Screens SCR-39, popup "Record Pregnancy Check". REM-03, REM-04, REM-05.
Assumptions | Results may come from an external veterinarian; no veterinarian account exists.
[/TABLE]
[P637 4] 12.2 Update Pregnancy Status
[TABLE block 638]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE12-03 — Update Pregnancy Status
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 21/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The Manager selects "Record pregnancy update" (Ghi diễn biến thai) on the Reproduction tab of a PREGNANT female.
Description | As a farm manager, I want to record observations, complications or a loss during pregnancy, so that the pregnancy history is complete and a loss closes the pregnancy.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is a Manager of an ACTIVE farm. / PRE-2: The female has an OPEN pregnancy episode.
Postconditions | POST-1: A VALID pregnancy update is saved against the episode. / POST-2: LOSS: the episode is LOST, REM-05 is cancelled and the female returns to OPEN.
Normal Flow | 1. System shows popup "Record Pregnancy Update" with the episode (pregnancy number, expected date). / 2. Manager selects the update type (observation, complication, loss), enters the time, data source and details and, for a complication, optionally links an existing health case of the female. / 3. Manager selects "Save update". / 4. System validates the fields, chronology (after the confirming check) and episode state. / 5. System saves the update in one transaction and shows MSG-FE12-03.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Loss (step 5): the System closes the episode as LOST, cancels REM-05, updates status and shows MSG-FE12-04 (GBR-56). / AF2 – Complication needing treatment: the Manager opens or updates the case in FE-10 (UC-FE10-02). / AF3 – Cancel: nothing is saved.
Exceptions | E1: Missing required field: MSG-COM-06. / E2: Episode no longer OPEN: MSG-FE12-12. / E3: Time invalid: MSG-COM-07. / E4: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.
Priority | Medium
Frequency of Use | Weekly.

[P640 4] 12.3 Record Calving Event and Outcome
[TABLE block 641]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE12-04 — Record Calving Event and Outcome
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 21/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The Manager selects "Record calving" (Ghi sự kiện đẻ) on the Reproduction tab of a female in status BRED or PREGNANT, or "Record calving (untracked pregnancy)" under More actions for any ACTIVE female at stage Young stock or Adult (GBR-54).
Description | As a farm manager, I want to record a calving with one outcome per calf and register live calves now or as drafts, so that births are complete and every live calf becomes one cattle record.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is a Manager of an ACTIVE farm. / PRE-2: The dam is an ACTIVE, registered female.
Postconditions | POST-1: A VALID calving with at least one calf outcome is saved; the linked episode is CALVED and the dam is POST_CALVING. / POST-2: Each LIVE calf is REGISTERED (Register now) or PENDING_CONFIRMATION with REM-09 (draft). / POST-3: REM-05 is resolved and REM-07 is created when parameters exist. / POST-4: Registered calves have life stage UNWEANED_CALF; a dam whose life stage is not ADULT gets a REM-08 suggestion to change it to ADULT (GBR-25).
Normal Flow | 1. System opens Record Calving (SCR-40) with the dam (read-only) and the OPEN episode proposed as linked pregnancy. / 2. Manager enters birth time, birth attendant, calving course (normal, assisted, caesarean), dam condition (good, needs monitoring, critical) and retained placenta; the data source is Direct observation unless changed; the System shows one calf block and the Manager adds a block for each further calf. / 3. For each calf the Manager enters the outcome (live, stillborn), sex (male, female; unknown only for a stillborn calf), birth weight with unit and basis (measured, estimated). / 4. For each live calf the Manager enters the tag/QR code, initial pen (default: the dam's pen), breed (default from dam and sire) and chooses "Save as draft calf" or "Register now"; for "Register now" the Manager also selects the production purpose (default: the first ACTIVE purpose of type UNDECIDED). / 5. Manager selects "Save calving". / 6. System validates the fields, chronology, tag uniqueness in the farm, pen availability for calves to register and the dam's state; it serializes changes for the dam (GBR-52). / 7. System saves, in one transaction, the calving, the outcomes, the episode closure and the dam status; registers each "Register now" calf with UC-FE06-01 logic (FARM_BORN, GBR-31); saves each draft calf as PENDING_CONFIRMATION and creates REM-09 for the Owner and Managers (GBR-58). / 8. System updates reminders (JOB-01) and shows MSG-FE12-05 with MSG-FE12-09 and/or MSG-FE12-10.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Pregnancy not tracked: the Manager selects "No tracked pregnancy"; the calving is saved without an episode (GBR-57). / AF2 – Stillborn calf: no tag, pen or registration choice is asked. / AF3 – Calf dies before registration (later): the Owner or a Manager marks the draft calf DIED_BEFORE_REGISTRATION from the pending calves list (SCR-25). / AF4 – Add / remove calf blocks: "+ Add calf" / "Remove calf" before saving. / AF5 – Cancel: nothing is saved.
Exceptions | E1: No calf outcome: MSG-FE12-06. / E2: Birth weight not positive or without unit/basis: MSG-FE12-07. / E3: Tag already used in the farm: MSG-FE12-08. / E4: Pen INACTIVE or full for a calf to register: MSG-FE04-05. / E5: Duplicate calving, concurrent loss or newer episode: MSG-FE12-12. / E6: Time invalid: MSG-COM-07. / E7: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; nothing is saved.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Several times a month.
Business Rules | GBR-08, GBR-31, GBR-49, GBR-52, GBR-57, GBR-58; BR-D08, BR-D10, BR-D19.
Other Information | Screens SCR-39, SCR-40, SCR-25. Related: UC-FE06-01. REM-05, REM-07, REM-09.
Assumptions | —
[/TABLE]

[P649 3] 13. FE-13 Herd Exit Recording
[P650 4] 13.1 Record and Confirm Herd Exit Voucher
[TABLE block 651]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE13-01 — Record and Confirm Herd Exit Voucher
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 22/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Owner | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The Owner selects "Create exit voucher" (Tạo phiếu xuất) or "Continue" on a DRAFT voucher in Herd Exit Vouchers, or "View & confirm" (Xem & xác nhận) on a PENDING death voucher, the dashboard list A03 or a notification.
Description | As a farm owner, I want to record each departure of cattle as one voucher with weights, prices and the money to collect, and confirm it after reviewing open items, so that the herd, the records of each animal and the buyer's balance are correct.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is the Owner of an ACTIVE farm. / PRE-2: The cattle to add are ACTIVE and on no other DRAFT, PENDING or CONFIRMED exit line, or a PENDING voucher exists.
Postconditions | POST-1: The voucher is CONFIRMED with its lines, totals and collected deposit; each cattle on it is EXITED and read-only. / POST-2: For each cattle: the open pen assignment is closed at the exit date; future doses and reminders are cancelled; OPEN and CHRONIC health cases are closed "Closed by herd exit"; an open pregnancy is closed "Dam exited" (GBR-35). / POST-3: Until confirmation a DRAFT or PENDING voucher has no effect; its cattle stay ACTIVE.
Normal Flow | 1. System shows Herd Exit Voucher (SCR-42) with the next voucher code, exit type Sale and today as exit date. / 2. Owner chooses the exit type (Sale, Cull or Other) and enters the exit date, buyer (trading partner, added inline when new) or destination, transport quarantine certificate, vehicle plate and note. / 3. Owner selects "+ Select cattle"; System opens popup "Select Cattle for Exit Voucher" with ACTIVE cattle not on another open voucher, filters (pen, herd group, minimum weight) and warnings per cattle; Owner selects cattle and confirms, or scans their QR tags. / 4. System adds one line per cattle with the latest weight as exit weight (basis ESTIMATED, marked UT) and the check result. / 5. Owner chooses the pricing basis, enters the unit price and, where weighed, the measured exit weight of each line; System calculates line amounts and totals (GBR-91, GBR-92). / 6. Owner attaches documents and enters deductions, the deposit collected, method and due date of the balance; System shows total receivable and outstanding. / 7. System shows the pre-confirmation checks per item and cattle: active meat withdrawal period (MSG-FE13-07), OPEN and CHRONIC health cases, future doses and reminders that will be cancelled, open pregnancy and draft calves of a dam, and the occupancy change of each pen. / 8. Owner ticks "I have reviewed the items above and confirm the exit" and selects "Confirm exit of n cattle" (Xác nhận xuất n con). / 9. System validates the date (not in the future, not before the herd entry or the latest recorded event of each cattle), the state of each cattle, the amounts and the version. / 10. System confirms the voucher and applies all effects to every line in one transaction (GBR-35, GBR-94); the audit entry is saved. / 11. System shows MSG-FE13-02 and opens Herd Exit Voucher Detail (SCR-76).
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Confirm a pending death voucher: System opens Death / Missing Exit Voucher (SCR-43) with the cattle, source health case, reporter and date of death or loss; the Owner completes the cause and carcass disposal, attaches evidence, reviews the checks and continues at step 8; the voucher has no amounts (GBR-95). / AF2 – Dismiss a pending voucher: the Owner selects "Dismiss" with a reason; the voucher becomes DISMISSED, the cattle stays ACTIVE and the health case keeps its outcome (MSG-FE13-04). / AF3 – Save draft (any step): the voucher is saved as DRAFT (MSG-FE13-06); the cattle stay ACTIVE and cannot be added to another voucher. / AF4 – Remove a line: the Owner removes a cattle from a DRAFT voucher; totals are recalculated. / AF5 – Warnings present: MSG-FE13-01 lists them per cattle; warnings never block, the Owner can still confirm after ticking the confirmation. / AF6 – Death recorded directly: the Owner chooses type "Death" for a loss not reported through a health case and continues as AF1. / AF7 – Discard draft / Cancel: the Owner discards a DRAFT voucher or leaves without saving (MSG-COM-14).
Exceptions | E1: Exit date invalid: MSG-FE13-03. / E2: Cattle already on another open voucher: MSG-FE13-08. / E3: No cattle line: MSG-FE06-16. / E4: Deposit greater than the total receivable: MSG-FE06-13. / E5: Missing type, date, buyer or destination, cause or required field: MSG-COM-06. / E6: Confirmation not ticked: MSG-COM-16. / E7: A cattle is no longer ACTIVE or the voucher changed: MSG-COM-05. / E8: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; nothing changes.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Weekly.
Business Rules | GBR-07, GBR-34, GBR-35, GBR-46, GBR-91, GBR-92, GBR-94, GBR-95, GBR-97; BR-D09, BR-D11, BR-D34, BR-D35, BR-D39.
Other Information | Screens SCR-41, SCR-42, SCR-43, popup "Select Cattle for Exit Voucher", SCR-76. Related: UC-FE10-03, UC-FE13-04.
Assumptions | Collections from the buyer are recorded in BeefCare as farm bookkeeping; invoices and tax documents are outside BeefCare.
[/TABLE]
[P653 4] 13.2 Void Herd Exit Voucher
[TABLE block 654]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE13-03 — Void Herd Exit Voucher
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 22/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Owner | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The Owner selects "Void voucher" (Hủy phiếu) on a CONFIRMED voucher in Herd Exit Voucher Detail (SCR-76).
Description | As a farm owner, I want to void a mistaken exit voucher with a reason, so that its animals return to the herd while the mistake stays traceable.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is the Owner of an ACTIVE farm. / PRE-2: The voucher is CONFIRMED.
Postconditions | POST-1: The voucher is VOIDED with reason, actor and time; every cattle on it is ACTIVE again with an open pen assignment in its last pen or in the pen chosen by the Owner. / POST-2: Items closed or cancelled by the exit are listed for review and are not restored automatically; recorded collections stay in the voucher history.
Normal Flow | 1. System shows popup "Void Herd Exit Voucher" with the voucher (type, date, number of cattle, buyer) and the effects: cattle back to ACTIVE, last pen and its occupancy after the void, cancelled items not restored, collections kept. / 2. Owner enters the void reason and selects "Void voucher". / 3. System checks the voucher state and version, that no cattle of the voucher is on another open exit line, and that each last pen is ACTIVE with enough free places. / 4. System voids the voucher, sets each cattle ACTIVE, opens its pen assignment from the void date and saves the audit entry in one transaction (GBR-36). / 5. System shows MSG-FE13-05 with the list of items closed or cancelled by the exit.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Last pen unavailable or full (step 3): MSG-FE13-09; the Owner chooses an ACTIVE pen with free places for each affected cattle (reception warnings need a reason) and continues at step 4. / AF2 – Close the dialog: nothing changes.
Exceptions | E1: Missing reason: MSG-COM-06. / E2: Voucher already VOIDED or changed: MSG-COM-05. / E3: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; nothing changes.
Priority | Low
Frequency of Use | Rare.
Business Rules | GBR-07, GBR-20, GBR-26, GBR-36, GBR-91; BR-D09, BR-D11, BR-D35.

[P656 3] 14. FE-18 Farm Master Data and Reference Configuration
[P657 4] 14.1 Configure Farm State and Reminder References
[TABLE block 658]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE18-17 — Configure Farm State and Reminder References
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 21/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Owner, Farm Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The user opens "State & reminder parameters" (Tham số trạng thái & nhắc việc) in Catalogues & Parameters and selects "Save new version".
Description | As a farm owner or manager, I want to set the farm's life-stage ages, breeding conditions, gestation and reminder intervals with their source, so that suggestions and reminders fit our farm.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is the Owner or a Manager of an ACTIVE farm.
Postconditions | POST-1: A new parameter version with source note, effective date and author is saved and becomes effective at its effective time. / POST-2: Reminders evaluated after that time use the new values; recorded events are unchanged (GBR-68).
Normal Flow | 1. System shows State and Reminder Parameters (SCR-56) with the current version, its source and the version history. / 2. User edits the values in the groups: life stages (weaning age, young-stock age, adult age, in months); breeding conditions (minimum age, minimum weight); gestation and checks (gestation length, pregnancy check after breeding, re-check after inconclusive, calving preparation lead time, postpartum interval); breeding window (start and end hours after estrus, estrus cycle length); growth and vaccination (BCS scale, weighing interval, vaccination reminder lead time, optional target finishing weight). / 3. System validates each value against its allowed range and the version. / 5. System saves the new version and, when effective now, triggers JOB-01 to recompute future reminders. / 6. System shows MSG-FE18-05.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Future effective date: the version is saved as scheduled and applied by JOB-01 at that date. / AF2 – View an older version: the user opens the history; old versions are read-only. / AF3 – Cancel: nothing changes.
Exceptions | E1: Value out of range, end hour not after start hour, or life-stage ages not in increasing order (weaning < young stock < adult): MSG-FE18-06. / E2: Missing source note: MSG-COM-06. / E3: Changed in another session: MSG-COM-05. / E4: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.
Priority | Medium
Frequency of Use | A few times a year.
Business Rules | GBR-25, GBR-55, GBR-63, GBR-68.
Other Information | Screen SCR-56 (default values and allowed ranges in III.18.5). Related: JOB-01.
Assumptions | Parameter values are agreed with the farm; BeefCare provides decision support only (OR-06).

[P660 3] 15. FE-19 Subscription and Payment Management
[P661 4] 15.1 Request Subscription Payment
[TABLE block 662]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE19-02 — Request Subscription Payment
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 21/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Owner | Secondary Actors | SePay (VietQR image)
Trigger | The Owner selects "Renew plan" (Gia hạn gói) on Farm Subscription, "Pay to activate" on Select Farm, or continues from Register Farm (first purchase).
Description | As a farm owner, I want to choose a 1-, 2- or 3-year term and get transfer instructions with VietQR, so that I can pay for my farm by bank transfer.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is the Owner of the farm (any service status except SUSPENDED). / PRE-2: A price version is effective and the receiving account is configured.
Postconditions | POST-1: One PENDING payment request with immutable term, amount, price version, receiving account, unique payment reference and 24-hour expiry exists for the farm (or the existing valid request is reopened). / POST-2: The Owner sees the transfer instructions; no service period changes until the payment is confirmed (UC-FE19-08 / UC-FE19-06).
Normal Flow | 1. System shows Select Term and Review (SCR-58): the three terms with total prices from the effective price version, the plan terms (same functions for all terms, one payment per farm, no automatic renewal, request valid 24 hours) and the farm's service status and current period. / 2. Owner selects a term; the System shows the review: farm, term, amount, estimated service period and price version. / 3. Owner selects "Create payment request". / 4. System checks ownership, service status, the renewal window (GBR-71), that no unexpired PENDING request exists and that the reviewed price version is still effective (GBR-70). / 5. System creates the request with a unique payment reference (e.g. BCF7K2Q9), expiry = now + 24 hours and the receiving account snapshot; it creates the farm's Subscription record if none exists. / 6. System shows Transfer Instructions (SCR-59): VietQR (API-01), status PENDING, countdown, expiry time, bank, account number, account name, amount and transfer content with copy buttons, and the progress steps (request created, bank transaction received, confirmed and period granted), with MSG-FE19-05.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – A valid PENDING request exists (step 4): MSG-FE19-01 offers to reopen its instructions; no new request. / AF2 – Price changed since review (step 4): MSG-FE19-02 shows old and new prices; the Owner reviews again (step 2). / AF3 – "Later" / "Close instructions": the request stays PENDING until it expires; it can be reopened from Payment History. / AF4 – Check again (step 6): the System refreshes the status; when PAID it shows the purchased period (MSG-FE19-06). / AF5 – Request expired: MSG-FE19-07 offers a new request.
Exceptions | E1: Not the Owner: MSG-COM-01. / E2: Outside the renewal window: MSG-FE19-03 with the opening date. / E3: Farm SUSPENDED: MSG-FE19-09 with "Send support ticket". / E4: Prices or receiving account unavailable: MSG-FE19-04 with Retry. / E5: VietQR image cannot be rendered: the transfer details remain shown as text. / E6: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; no request is created.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Once every 1–3 years per farm.
Business Rules | GBR-69, GBR-70, GBR-71, GBR-75; BR-D23.
Other Information | Screens SCR-57, SCR-58, SCR-59, SCR-60. API-01. JOB-03 expires requests.
Assumptions | The Owner transfers from a Vietnamese bank account that supports VietQR.
[/TABLE]
[P664 4] 15.2 Reconcile Subscription Payment
[TABLE block 665]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE19-06 — Reconcile Subscription Payment
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 21/10/2026
Primary Actor | Platform Manager | Secondary Actors | SePay (transaction lookup)
Trigger | The PM opens an evidence item marked for review in Payments and Reconciliation, from a reconciliation notification or an internal request.
Description | As a Platform Manager, I want to review bank evidence that was not confirmed automatically and accept, hold or reject it with a reason, so that every transfer is handled exactly once.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The PM is signed in with ACTIVE PM access. / PRE-2: The evidence is authenticated and not allocated.
Postconditions | POST-1: ACCEPT: the selected request is PAID with its purchased period and receipt, exactly as in UC-FE19-08. / POST-2: HOLD or REJECT: the decision with reason is saved; no service is granted. / POST-3: Every decision is kept in the evidence history and audit.
Normal Flow | 1. System shows Payments and Reconciliation (SCR-62) with evidence needing review (unmatched code, expired request, no request, amount mismatch) and the selected evidence detail: provider, environment, transaction ID, amount and direction, receiving account check, transfer content, bank-paid time and candidate requests. / 2. PM selects the candidate request (when the reference is mistyped but one candidate clearly corresponds) and the decision ACCEPT, and enters the reason and evidence references. / 3. PM selects "Check and accept". / 4. System runs the shared confirmation rules (GBR-72) on the evidence and the selected request: amount, receiving account, bank-paid time within the request window, no allocation, no posted manual income on the same transaction (GBR-84); confirmations for the farm are serialized. / 5. System allocates the evidence, marks the request PAID, creates the purchased period (GBR-73), derives the farm service status, saves the review and audit in one transaction and notifies the Owner. / 6. System shows MSG-FE19-10.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – HOLD: the PM saves HOLD with a reason; the evidence stays unresolved for later review. / AF2 – REJECT: the PM saves REJECT with a reason; the evidence cannot fund any request unless reviewed again. / AF3 – Lock for return: for money that must be returned outside BeefCare, the PM locks the evidence with a reason; later records the outgoing bank reference and result, or unlocks with a reason when no return happened (GBR-74). / AF4 – Look up a transaction: the PM searches SePay transactions by ID or period (API-03); a found transaction is stored as evidence once.
Exceptions | E1: Acceptance blocked (amount, account, window, allocation or manual income conflict): MSG-FE19-11 with the reason; nothing is allocated. / E2: No candidate selected for a mistyped reference: MSG-FE19-11. / E3: Missing reason: MSG-COM-06. / E4: Evidence or request changed meanwhile (for example confirmed by SePay): MSG-COM-05. / E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; no partial result.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Daily or as needed.
Business Rules | GBR-15, GBR-72, GBR-73, GBR-74, GBR-84, GBR-86; BR-D24, BR-D28.
Other Information | Screens SCR-62, SCR-47. API-03. Related: UC-FE19-08, UC-FE20-13.
Assumptions | Bank account operators perform any outgoing return transfer outside BeefCare.
[/TABLE]

[P670 4] 15.4 Confirm Subscription Payment from SePay
[TABLE block 671]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE19-08 — Confirm Subscription Payment from SePay
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 21/10/2026
Primary Actor | SePay | Secondary Actors | Email Delivery Service
Trigger | SePay calls the BeefCare webhook (API-02) for a transaction on the receiving account.
Description | As the platform, I want to authenticate each bank transaction reported by SePay and confirm the matching payment request exactly once, so that farms are activated automatically and safely.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The webhook endpoint and the environment secret are configured.
Postconditions | POST-1: The authenticated transaction is stored once as evidence. / POST-2: When eligible: the request is PAID, the purchased period is created, the farm service is derived (never lifting a suspension), the receipt is available and the Owner is notified. / POST-3: When not eligible: the evidence waits for PM review; nothing is granted.
Normal Flow | 1. SePay sends the transaction with HMAC signature and timestamp headers. / 2. System verifies the signature over timestamp + "." + raw body with the environment secret and a 300-second tolerance. / 3. System stores the evidence keyed by provider, environment, account namespace and transaction ID (duplicates refer to the same evidence) together with a processing job, and replies HTTP 200 {"success": true}. / 4. JOB-06 processes the evidence: incoming direction, receiving account, extracted payment code matching exactly one request, exact amount, bank-paid time within [created, expires) and no allocation (GBR-72). / 5. System allocates the evidence, marks the request PAID with confirmed time, creates the purchased period (GBR-73), derives the service status and saves the audit in one transaction. / 6. System creates the Owner notification and email (JOB-07) and the receipt becomes visible (UC-FE21-01).
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Duplicate delivery: the existing evidence is reused; a committed confirmation is returned without a new period or notice. / AF2 – Not eligible (no or wrong code, amount mismatch, expired window, outgoing transfer): the evidence is marked for review and a reconciliation notification is created for PMs (UC-FE14-04). / AF3 – Request EXPIRED but bank-paid time inside the window: the request becomes PAID. / AF4 – Farm SUSPENDED: the request becomes PAID and the period is created; the farm stays SUSPENDED (GBR-15).
Exceptions | E1: Invalid signature or timestamp: HTTP 401; only restricted delivery metadata is kept; no evidence. / E2: Storage failure before step 3 completes: no success reply, so SePay retries. / E3: Processing failure after storage: JOB-06 retries; the evidence is never lost.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Every subscription transfer.
Business Rules | GBR-13, GBR-15, GBR-72, GBR-73, GBR-74, GBR-84; BR-D23, BR-D24.
Other Information | API-02, JOB-06, JOB-07. Related: UC-FE19-06, UC-FE21-01. NFR-P05.
Assumptions | SePay retries failed deliveries; the same transaction ID is kept across retries.
[/TABLE]

[P702 4] 17.2 Post Finance Record
[TABLE block 703]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE21-06 — Post Finance Record
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 21/10/2026

Normal Flow | 1. PM selects Post; the System asks for confirmation (MSG-FE21-03). / 2. PM confirms. / 3. System revalidates all fields, the source exclusion (GBR-84) and the version. / 4. System sets POSTED with poster and time and saves history and audit in one transaction. / 5. System shows MSG-FE21-02.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Cancel: the record stays DRAFT.
Exceptions | E1: Validation fails: the corresponding field message (MSG-FE21-09, MSG-FE21-10). / E2: Source conflict: MSG-FE21-08. / E3: Changed meanwhile: MSG-COM-05. / E4: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.
Priority | Medium
Frequency of Use | Weekly.
Business Rules | GBR-83, GBR-84, GBR-86; BR-D27, BR-D28.
Other Information | Screens SCR-71, SCR-72.
Assumptions | —
[/TABLE]
[P705 4] 17.3 Void Posted Finance Record
[TABLE block 706]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE21-07 — Void Posted Finance Record
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 21/10/2026
Primary Actor | Platform Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The PM selects "Void" (VOID) on a POSTED record.
Description | As a Platform Manager, I want to void a wrong posted record with a reason, so that later reports exclude it while the original stays traceable.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The PM is signed in with ACTIVE PM access. / PRE-2: The record is POSTED.
Postconditions | POST-1: The record is VOID with reason, voider and time; snapshots generated afterwards exclude it; earlier snapshots keep it as of their time. / POST-2: A bank source claimed by the record is released.
Normal Flow | 1. PM selects Void; the System shows the confirmation with a reason field (MSG-FE21-04). / 2. PM enters the reason and confirms. / 3. System checks the status and version. / 4. System sets VOID, releases its source claim and saves history and audit in one transaction. / 5. System shows MSG-FE21-05 and offers "Create replacement draft" prefilled from the voided record.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Cancel: nothing changes.
Exceptions | E1: Missing reason: MSG-COM-06. / E2: Already VOID or changed: MSG-COM-05. / E3: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.
Priority | Low
Frequency of Use | Rare.
Business Rules | GBR-83, GBR-85, GBR-86; BR-D27.
Other Information | Screens SCR-71, SCR-72.
Assumptions | —
[/TABLE]
[P708 4] 17.4 Export Platform Cash Report
[TABLE block 709]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE21-09 — Export Platform Cash Report
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 21/10/2026
Primary Actor | Platform Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The PM selects "Export XLSX" (Xuất XLSX) on Platform Cash Report.
Description | As a Platform Manager, I want to export the report I am viewing, so that I can share exactly the same figures.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The PM is signed in with ACTIVE PM access. / PRE-2: A report snapshot is displayed and still available.
Postconditions | POST-1: An XLSX file with Summary and Details sheets from the displayed snapshot is downloaded; nothing changes in the data.
Normal Flow | 1. PM selects Export XLSX. / 2. System rechecks PM access and that the snapshot is available. / 3. System builds the file from the snapshot: Summary (period, farm filter, source filter, currency VND, generated time, totals per scope and platform totals) and Details (source type and code, scope, farm, direction, category/description, amount, cash date, status and source references). / 4. System returns the file and shows MSG-FE21-13.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Empty report: the file contains headers and zero totals.
Exceptions | E1: Snapshot expired: MSG-FE21-12; the PM must run the report again. / E2: Export failure: MSG-FE21-14 with Retry. / E3: No PM access: MSG-COM-01.
Priority | Medium
Frequency of Use | Monthly.
Business Rules | GBR-85, BR-D30.
Other Information | Screen SCR-73. Related: UC-FE21-08.
Assumptions | —
[/TABLE]
[P711 3] 18. FE-23 Feed Management
[P712 4] 18.1 Record Feed Receipt
[TABLE block 713]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE23-02 — Record Feed Receipt
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 22/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Owner, Farm Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The user selects "Create feed receipt" (Tạo phiếu nhập kho) on Feed Stock or Feed Receipts, "Receive" (Nhập kho) on a feed row, or "Continue" on a DRAFT receipt.
Description | As a farm owner or manager, I want to record each delivery of feed with its lots, quantities and prices, so that stock, average cost and what the farm owes the supplier are always correct.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is the Owner or a Manager of an ACTIVE farm. / PRE-2: At least one ACTIVE feed item and one ACTIVE supplier exist (or the supplier is added inline).
Postconditions | POST-1: The receipt is POSTED with its lines, totals and initial payment; one RECEIPT stock movement per line adds the quantity in kg; the weighted average cost of each feed is recalculated (GBR-99). / POST-2: REM-10 is resolved for each feed whose stock is now above its minimum level. / POST-3: A DRAFT receipt changes no stock or balance.
Normal Flow | 1. System shows Feed Receipt Form (SCR-79) with the next receipt code and today as receipt date. / 2. User enters the supplier, receipt date, invoice or delivery-note number, receiver, storage location and note. / 3. User selects "+ Add item" and, for each line, the feed item, lot number, expiry date, quantity and purchase unit and unit price; System converts the quantity to kg using the item's conversion and calculates the line amount. / 4. System shows the totals, the stock before → after for each feed and the pre-save checks (feed ACTIVE, quantity > 0, unit price ≥ 0, expiry date after the receipt date, receipt date not in the future). / 5. User attaches the invoice or delivery note and enters the transport fee, amount paid, method and due date of the balance; System shows total payable and outstanding (GBR-91). / 6. User selects "Save & add to stock" (Lưu & cộng kho). / 7. System rechecks the receipt and version and, in one transaction, posts it, records the stock movements, recalculates average costs, records the initial payment and saves the audit entry. / 8. System shows MSG-FE23-01 and resolves or keeps REM-10 for the feeds concerned.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Save draft: the receipt is saved as DRAFT (MSG-FE23-02); stock and balances do not change. / AF2 – Record a later payment (posted receipt): the user selects "Record payment"; System opens popup "Record Voucher Payment"; the payment must not exceed the outstanding amount (MSG-FE06-13, MSG-FE06-14). / AF3 – Void a wrong payment: the user voids it with a reason (MSG-FE06-15); it stays in the history. / AF4 – New supplier: the user adds the supplier inline (name, phone); it is saved as a trading partner with role SUPPLIER (GBR-97). / AF5 – Discard draft / Cancel: nothing changes (MSG-COM-14).
Exceptions | E1: Expiry date not after the receipt date: MSG-FE23-03. / E2: Receipt date in the future: MSG-COM-07. / E3: Missing supplier, feed, quantity or required field: MSG-COM-06. / E4: Amount paid greater than the total payable: MSG-FE06-13. / E5: Feed item inactive: MSG-COM-08. / E6: Changed in another session: MSG-COM-05. / E7: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; nothing is posted.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Several times a week.
Business Rules | GBR-91, GBR-97, GBR-98, GBR-99, GBR-103, GBR-104; BR-D34, BR-D36, BR-D39.
Other Information | Screens SCR-77, SCR-78, SCR-79, popup "Record Voucher Payment". Related: UC-FE23-03, UC-FE23-10.
Assumptions | Feed is stocked in one storage location per farm; lots are recorded for expiry warnings only.
[/TABLE]
[P715 4] 18.2 Create or Update Ration
[TABLE block 716]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE23-06 — Create or Update Ration
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 22/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Owner, Farm Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The user selects "Create ration" (Tạo khẩu phần) or "Edit" (Sửa) on a ration in Rations (SCR-81).
Description | As a farm owner or manager, I want to define how much of each feed every animal of a pen should get per session, and change it from a chosen date, so that daily feeding follows a plan and past feeding records stay unchanged.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is the Owner or a Manager of an ACTIVE farm. / PRE-2: ACTIVE feed items and pens exist.
Postconditions | POST-1: A ration version is saved with its components, pens, sessions and effective date; when it replaces a version, the previous version ends the day before (GBR-101). / POST-2: Planned quantities of feeding sessions from the effective date use the new version; recorded feeding is not changed.
Normal Flow | 1. System shows Ration Editor (SCR-82): for a new ration the next code and version v1; for an edit the current values and the next version number. / 2. User enters the name, herd group, pens, effective-from date (tomorrow or later) and basis or source. / 3. User chooses the number of sessions per day (1, 2 or 3) and their times, and adds feeds with the kg per head for each session. / 4. System shows kg per head per day, the average cost of each feed, the cost per head per day (compared with the previous version), the planned daily quantity and cost for the current headcount of the pens and the days of cover of each feed at current stock. / 5. System shows the checks: feeds ACTIVE with quantity > 0; each pen has only one effective ration per day; feeds whose stock does not cover one day are warned. / 6. User selects "Save ration". / 7. System validates the version and saves it; the previous version of the ration (or another ration of the same pens from that date) ends the day before; System shows MSG-FE23-09.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Cancel a scheduled version: the user cancels a version that has not taken effect; the previous version stays effective. / AF2 – Stop a ration: the user enters an end date (tomorrow or later); the pens have no ration after it. / AF3 – Stock warning (step 5): the warning does not block saving. / AF4 – Cancel: nothing changes (MSG-COM-14).
Exceptions | E1: Effective date earlier than tomorrow: MSG-FE23-10. / E2: A pen has another ration on the date that cannot be ended automatically (a scheduled version of another ration): MSG-FE23-11. / E3: No component or a quantity ≤ 0: MSG-COM-06 / MSG-COM-10. / E4: Changed in another session: MSG-COM-05. / E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.
Priority | Medium
Frequency of Use | Monthly per ration.
Business Rules | GBR-101, GBR-103, GBR-105; BR-D38.
Other Information | Screens SCR-81, SCR-82. Related: UC-FE23-05, UC-FE23-08.
Assumptions | Rations are expressed per head for all animals of the pen; individual feeding is not planned.
[/TABLE]
[P718 4] 18.3 Record Feeding Session
[TABLE block 719]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE23-08 — Record Feeding Session
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 22/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The Manager selects "Record feeding session" (Ghi cữ cho ăn) or an empty session cell on Feeding Log (SCR-83), or opens reminder REM-11.
Description | As a farm manager, I want to record the feed actually given to a pen in each session, so that stock is deducted automatically and consumption and cost can be compared with the ration.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is a Farm Manager of an ACTIVE farm. / PRE-2: The pen is ACTIVE.
Postconditions | POST-1: One VALID feeding record exists for the pen, date and session with its lines, headcount and ration version. / POST-2: One FEEDING stock movement per feed deducts the actual kg at its average cost; REM-11 for the session is resolved; REM-10 is created for feeds at or below their minimum level.
Normal Flow | 1. System shows popup "Record Feeding Session" for the selected date (default today) with pen and session. / 2. Manager selects the pen and session (if not preselected); System shows the feeding time, the effective ration version, the current headcount and, for each feed of the ration, the kg per head, the planned quantity (kg per head × headcount) and the current stock. / 3. Manager enters the actual kg of each feed, or selects "Fill from plan" (Điền theo kế hoạch) and adjusts; optionally adds feeds outside the ration, the leftover kg, the member who fed (Manager or Technician) and a note. / 4. Manager selects "Save & deduct stock" (Lưu & trừ kho). / 5. System validates: no VALID record exists for the pen, date and session; the date is not in the future; each actual quantity ≥ 0 and not more than the stock (GBR-102). / 6. System saves the record and the stock movements in one transaction, resolves REM-11 and evaluates REM-10. / 7. System shows MSG-FE23-12 and updates the Feeding Log.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – No effective ration: MSG-FE23-20; the Manager enters actual feeds without a plan. / AF2 – Feed out of stock: the actual quantity of that feed is 0 and the reason is written in the note; the log shows "out of stock". / AF3 – Cancel: nothing changes (MSG-COM-14).
Exceptions | E1: A record already exists for the pen, date and session: MSG-FE23-13. / E2: Actual quantity above stock: MSG-FE23-14. / E3: Date in the future: MSG-COM-07. / E4: Missing pen, session or quantities: MSG-COM-06. / E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; no stock is deducted.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Two to three times a day per pen.
Business Rules | GBR-102, GBR-103, GBR-104, GBR-105; BR-D36, BR-D37.
Other Information | Screens SCR-83, popup "Record Feeding Session", SCR-86. Medicine mixed into feed is recorded as a treatment in FE-10, not here.
Assumptions | Feeding is recorded per pen, not per animal.
[/TABLE]
[P721 4] 18.4 Correct or Void Feeding Record
[TABLE block 722]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE23-09 — Correct or Void Feeding Record
Created By | G35 Team | Date Created | 22/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The Manager selects "Correct" (Sửa) or "Void" (Hủy) on a recorded session in Feeding Log (SCR-83).
Description | As a farm manager, I want to correct or void a wrong feeding record with a reason, so that stock and consumption are right while the original entry stays traceable.
Preconditions | PRE-1: The user is a Farm Manager of an ACTIVE farm. / PRE-2: The feeding record is VALID.
Postconditions | POST-1: Correct: the new quantities are current, a Record Revision keeps the old values, reason, actor and time, and one FEEDING_CORRECTION movement per changed feed adjusts stock by the difference. / POST-2: Void: the record is VOIDED and one FEEDING_VOID movement per feed returns the deducted quantity to stock; REM-11 is re-evaluated for the session.
Normal Flow | 1. System shows popup "Correct / Void Feeding Record" with the pen, session, date, recorder and time, and the action choice "Correct quantities" / "Void record". / 2. Manager keeps "Correct quantities" and enters the new kg of the feeds to correct; System shows the stock difference of each feed. / 3. Manager enters the reason and selects "Save changes". / 4. System validates that each new quantity ≥ 0, that no feed's stock becomes negative (BR-D36) and the version. / 5. System saves the correction, the Record Revision and the stock movements in one transaction and shows MSG-FE23-15.
Alternative Flows | AF1 – Void record (step 1): the Manager enters the reason and confirms MSG-FE23-16; System voids the record, returns the stock and shows MSG-COM-09. / AF2 – Close: nothing changes.
Exceptions | E1: Stock would become negative: MSG-FE23-14. / E2: Missing reason: MSG-COM-06. / E3: Changed in another session: MSG-COM-05. / E4: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.
Priority | Medium
Frequency of Use | Weekly.
Business Rules | GBR-102, GBR-103; BR-D15, BR-D36.
Other Information | Screens SCR-83, popup "Correct / Void Feeding Record".
Assumptions | —
[/TABLE]
[P724 2] III. Functional Requirements
[P725 ] This part describes the screens of BeefCare grouped by feature (and sub-feature where a feature serves different actors), followed by the background jobs (III.24) and external APIs (III.25). For each screen it gives the mockup reference, the use cases it serves and the field descriptions. Mockups are taken from the BeefCare Figma file (pages 01–10); the figure boxes mark where the final mockups are inserted.
[P726 ] Table III-1 Common field and screen conventions
[TABLE block 727]
Item | Convention
Required field | Marked with * on the label. A required field left empty shows MSG-COM-06 under it.
Text | Leading and trailing spaces are removed. Unless stated otherwise: short text max 100 characters, long text (descriptions, notes, reasons) max 1,000 characters. A reason must contain at least 5 non-space characters.
Date / time | Displayed as dd/mm/yyyy and HH:mm (24 h) in the farm time zone (platform screens: Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh). Date pickers do not allow future dates for events that already happened (MSG-COM-07).
Numbers and money | Vietnamese format: "." thousands separator, "," decimal separator (e.g. 1.200.000 đ; 3,5). Money is whole VND. Weight in kg with at most 1 decimal.
Lists | 20 rows per page with page navigation; sortable by the column headers marked in the field table; filters are kept while the user stays in the session. An empty result shows MSG-COM-04 with "Clear filters". A loading failure shows MSG-COM-03 with Retry.
Status badges | Status values are shown with the codes used in this document (e.g. ACTIVE, PENDING, RESOLVED) and a Vietnamese label on the interface.
Actions by role | Buttons the user is not allowed to use are hidden; the backend still checks every request (GBR-09).
Dialog behaviour | Cancel or ✕ closes without saving; when data was entered, MSG-COM-14 asks for confirmation. Save buttons are disabled while a request is in progress, and a repeated click returns the first result (GBR-88). After a successful save a dialog or form closes, shows its success message and returns to the screen it was opened from (the source screen), which is refreshed. Full-page forms state their destination in their own section.
Home screen by role | After sign-in, after entering a farm and from the logo: Farm Owner and Farm Manager → Farm Dashboard (SCR-48); Farm Technician → Technician Home (SCR-74); Customer Support → Ticket Queue (SCR-67); Platform Manager → Farm Service Overview (SCR-12); System Admin → User Accounts (SCR-16).
Links from email | The verification link opens SCR-07 (or SCR-02 when Owner registration is not pending); the password-reset link opens SCR-06; the invitation link opens SCR-15 after sign-in or registration with the invited email.
Read-only records | An EXITED cattle shows MSG-COM-13 and a VOIDED cattle MSG-COM-18, with no recording buttons; a cattle on a PENDING death voucher shows MSG-COM-17 and only correction and void actions (GBR-26). A farm that is not ACTIVE shows MSG-COM-12 on farm operation screens (GBR-14).
Sign out | Available from the user menu in the top bar of every signed-in screen (UC-FE01-03); ends the session, clears the selected farm and returns to SCR-02.
Farm scope and versions | Every list, count and detail shows only data of the current farm (BR-D01); every update carries the record version (BR-D31, GBR-89).
Phone screens | The same screens adapt to phone width: tables become cards, the sidebar becomes the bottom navigation and dialogs become full-screen sheets (NFR-I02).
[/TABLE]

[TABLE block 731]

[P733 ] This screen allows a user to sign in (UC-FE01-02):
[P734 ] Sign in with Google, or with email and password.
[P735 ] Go to Forgot Password (UC-FE01-05) or Register Farm Owner (UC-FE01-01).
[P736 ] After 5 failed password attempts password sign-in is locked for 15 minutes (MSG-FE01-02). A suspended account is shown the "Account suspended" state with the official contact instead of a session.
[P737 ] Field Description
[TABLE block 738]
Field Name | Description
Continue with Google | Button. Starts Google sign-in (API-05).
Email * | Text, email format, max 254 characters, case-insensitive. Invalid format: MSG-COM-10.
Password * | Masked text with show/hide toggle, max 128 characters.
Remember me | Checkbox, default off. On: session kept up to 30 days; off: session ends after 8 hours of inactivity.
Forgot password? | Link to SCR-05.
Sign in | Button. Wrong email or password: MSG-FE01-01 above the form; locked: MSG-FE01-02; email not verified: MSG-FE01-11 with "Resend verification email". On success the destination is, in this order: the page requested before sign-in when still authorized; SCR-15 when started from an invitation link; SCR-07 when Owner registration is incomplete; the role home screen (Table III-1) of a platform staff member; the farm home screen when the user has exactly one ACTIVE farm; otherwise Select Farm (SCR-09). No farm and no platform role: MSG-FE01-15.
Register | Link "No Owner account yet? Register" to SCR-03.
State: Account suspended | 
Message | MSG-FE01-03 with the account email.
Contact BeefCare | Shows the official support email and phone (GBR-87).
Back to sign in | Link to SCR-02.
[/TABLE]

[P790 3] 2. FE-02 Farm Workspace and Service Management
[P791 4] 2.1 Owner Farm Workspace
[P792 5] 2.1.1 SCR-09 Select Farm
[TABLE block 793]
[Figure to be inserted] Mockup – Figma 02 › "Chọn trang trại"
[/TABLE]
[P795 ] This screen allows a user to see all their farms and enter one (UC-FE02-02), and an Owner to register another farm (UC-FE02-01) or open the subscription of an inactive farm.
[P796 ] Field Description

[P927 4] 5.2 SCR-19 Cattle Profile – Overview
[TABLE block 928]

[/TABLE]
[P930 ] This screen allows farm members to view one cattle (UC-FE05-02). It is the container of the profile tabs: Overview, Timeline (SCR-50), Growth & BCS (SCR-30), Vaccination (SCR-34), Health (SCR-37), Reproduction (SCR-39, females), Pen history (SCR-20) and QR tag (SCR-21). Recording buttons appear only for the roles allowed in each tab.
[P931 ] Field Description
[TABLE block 932]
Field Name | Description
Header | 
Photo | Thumbnail of the cattle's current photo, or a placeholder; select to enlarge.
Display code, name | e.g. BC-000125 "Hoa Đỏ".
Summary line | Ear tag number ("Tag later" when empty) · breed · sex · age · current pen.
Badges | In the order of 3.4.1: herd status (ACTIVE, or Exit pending), herd group, health state, "Cách ly thịt đến dd/mm/yyyy" while active, reproductive status when applicable. An EXITED cattle shows only "Đã xuất · exit type · date" with MSG-COM-13; a VOIDED cattle shows only "Nhập nhầm – đã hủy" with MSG-COM-18; a cattle on a PENDING death voucher shows MSG-COM-17 with a link to the voucher (Owner: SCR-43).
Update profile / Reissue QR | Buttons (Owner, Manager; hidden for EXITED, VOIDED and a PENDING death voucher); open popup "Update Cattle Profile" / popup "Issue / Replace QR Tag".
More ⋯ › Copy internal ID | Copies the Internal Cattle ID for support requests; the ID itself is not displayed.
Identification | 
Display code, Ear tag number | Read-only. The current QR tag is shown in the QR tag tab (SCR-21).
Individual information | 
Sex, Breed | Read-only.
Birth date, Age | Birth date or birth month with its accuracy always visible: exact date; "~" and "(estimated)" for an estimated date; "Unknown" without age.
Entry source | Purchased: entry date, seller, entry weight with basis and voucher code (link to SCR-75 for the Owner and Managers); "Quarantine until dd/mm/yyyy" while that date is today or later. Born on the farm: birth date and dam.
Declared at entry | Purchased cattle only: summary of the pre-entry declaration (vaccination, diseases, reproduction: number of earlier calvings, pregnant about n months), with "Unknown" shown as unknown (GBR-30).
Production purpose | Value, purpose type and "since" date.
Life stage | Value and "since" date; a pending REM-08 stage suggestion is shown as a hint with "Review" (Owner, Manager), which opens popup "Update Cattle Profile" on the Life stage tab.
Current pen | Pen and "since" date.
Dam, Sire | Links to internal cattle; external sire or semen code as text; "Unknown".
Name, Description | Read-only.
Care summary | 
Latest weight, ADG 90 days, BCS | From valid growth records with basis (GBR-39).
Health | Health state; OPEN and CHRONIC case counts with the suspected diagnosis of each, or "No open case"; "Suspected contagious – isolate" when a case has that flag.
Meat withdrawal | "Until dd/mm/yyyy" highlighted while active (GBR-46), otherwise "None". Shown in the care summary so that it is visible before any sale.
Reproduction | Status, breeding eligibility (Met / Not met) and expected calving date with basis; "Not applicable" for males, calves and females outside GBR-54.
Vaccination | Number of overdue doses.
Related reminders | Up to 5 active reminders of the cattle: title, due date; link to SCR-44.
[/TABLE]

[P971 3] 6. FE-06 Herd Entry Management
[P972 ] Every receipt of cattle is recorded as a herd entry voucher (GBR-90): a purchase voucher of one or more bought-in cattle with prices and payments, or a voucher of farm-born calves. A DRAFT voucher has no effect; posting creates the cattle. Payments to the seller are recorded with the shared dialog popup "Record Voucher Payment".
[P973 4] 6.1 SCR-23 Herd Entry Vouchers
[TABLE block 974]
[Figure to be inserted] Mockup – Figma 05 › "Phiếu nhập – Danh sách"
[/TABLE]

[P977 ] Field Description
[TABLE block 978]
Field Name | Description
Pending calves (n) | Button; opens SCR-25 with the PENDING_CONFIRMATION calves.
Create entry voucher | Button; opens SCR-24.
Status tabs | All, Draft, Posted, Voided, each with its count.
Period | Date range of the entry date, default from the start of the previous quarter to today.
Entry type | All, Purchase, Farm-born calves.
Source | All or one trading partner.
Summary tiles | 
Posted vouchers | Count in the period; number of DRAFT vouchers not yet posted.
Cattle entered | Count, split into purchased and farm-born.
Purchase amount | Total payable of POSTED purchase vouchers, including transport fees.
Owed to sellers | Sum of outstanding amounts and the vouchers concerned.
Voucher list | 
Voucher code | PN-YYYY-nnnn (sortable); link to SCR-75 (POSTED, VOIDED) or SCR-24 / SCR-25 (DRAFT).
Entry date | Sortable.
Entry type | Purchase / Farm-born calves.

Cattle | Number of cattle; number with estimated weight.
Total weight | kg.
Total amount (đ) | Total payable; "—" for farm-born calves.
Outstanding (đ) | Amount still owed to the seller.
Status | DRAFT / POSTED / VOIDED (with the void reason).
Actions | "Continue" (DRAFT) or "View".
Note | Read-only: a DRAFT voucher creates no cattle and changes no pen or balance; a POSTED voucher is corrected per cattle (popup "Correct / Void Herd Entry") and voided as a whole only when no cattle has other events (GBR-33).
[/TABLE]

[P1290 3] 13. FE-13 Herd Exit Recording
[P1291 ] Every departure of cattle is recorded as a herd exit voucher created and confirmed by the Owner only (GBR-94). The cattle stay ACTIVE until the voucher is confirmed. A health case closed as Died or Missing creates a PENDING death voucher (GBR-95). Collections from the buyer are recorded with the shared dialog popup "Record Voucher Payment".
[P1292 4] 13.1 SCR-41 Herd Exit Vouchers
[TABLE block 1293]

Create exit voucher | Button (Owner); opens SCR-42.
Status tabs | All, Draft, Pending confirmation, Confirmed, Voided / dismissed, each with its count.
Period | Date range of the exit date, default from the start of the previous quarter to today.
Exit type | All, Sale, Cull, Death, Other.
Summary tiles | 
Cattle exited | Count of cattle on CONFIRMED vouchers in the period, by type.
Sales revenue | Total receivable of CONFIRMED sale and cull vouchers.
To collect | Sum of outstanding amounts and the vouchers concerned.
Awaiting the Owner | Number of PENDING vouchers proposed from health cases.
Voucher list | 
Voucher code | PX-YYYY-nnnn (sortable); link to SCR-76, SCR-42 (DRAFT) or SCR-43 (PENDING).
Exit date | Sortable.

[P1336 3] 14. FE-14 System Reminders and Notifications
[P1337 4] 14.1 Farm Reminders
[P1338 ] Farm reminders are generated by JOB-01 from the rules below (GBR-63). Default intervals come from the farm parameters (SCR-56); a rule whose parameter is missing produces no reminder.
[P1339 ] Table III-3 Reminder Rule Catalogue
[TABLE block 1340]
Rule | Name | Source and condition | Due date / window | Resolved / cancelled when | Audience | Open source → screen
REM-01 | Weighing due | ACTIVE registered cattle. | Latest valid weight date (or herd entry date) + weighing interval (30 days). | Resolved by a new valid weight; cancelled at exit. | Owner, Manager, Technician | SCR-30 (record: popup "Record Weight and BCS")
REM-02 | Breeding window | VALID estrus of a female without an OPEN pregnancy. | Estrus time + 8 h to estrus time + 12 h. | Resolved by a breeding; superseded by a newer estrus; cancelled when the window has passed or the estrus is voided. | Owner, Manager, Technician | SCR-39 (record: popup "Record Breeding")
REM-03 | Pregnancy check due | VALID breeding without a later check, or a pregnancy declared at herd entry without a later check (GBR-30). | Breeding time + 28 days; entry date + 7 days for a declared pregnancy. | Resolved by a check; superseded by a newer breeding; cancelled when the breeding is voided. | Owner, Manager, Technician | SCR-39 (record: popup "Record Pregnancy Check")
REM-04 | Pregnancy re-check | INCONCLUSIVE check. | Check time + 14 days. | Resolved by the next check; cancelled when the check is voided. | Owner, Manager, Technician | SCR-39 (record: popup "Record Pregnancy Check")
REM-05 | Upcoming calving | Expected calving date (TENTATIVE or CONFIRMED, labelled). | Opens 30 days before the expected date; due on the date. | Resolved by a calving; cancelled by a loss, a NOT_PREGNANT check, a void or the exit. | Owner, Manager, Technician | SCR-39 (record: SCR-40)
REM-06 | Vaccination dose | PLANNED or DEFERRED scheduled dose. | Upcoming until 7 days before the planned (or new) date; due within 7 days; overdue after the date. | Resolved by an ON_FARM vaccination; follows a deferral; cancelled when the schedule stops or at exit. | Owner, Manager, Technician | SCR-34 (record: SCR-33; defer: popup "Defer Dose")
REM-07 | Post-calving breeding readiness | Calving of the female. | Calving time + 45 days. | Resolved by an estrus or breeding; cancelled at exit. | Owner, Manager, Technician | SCR-39 (record: popup "Record Estrus", popup "Record Breeding")
REM-08 | Pen move or life-stage suggestion | Pen move: ACTIVE cattle that no longer meets the effective reception criteria of its current pen (purpose type, life stage, sex, minimum age or weight). Life stage: ACTIVE cattle whose age reaches the next stage age (Unweaned calf → Weaned calf at the weaning age, 6 months; Weaned calf → Young stock at the young-stock age, 12 months; Young stock → Adult at the adult age, 24 months), or a female not yet Adult with a first VALID calving. No stage suggestion when the birth date is UNKNOWN. | Pen move: immediately. Life stage: the date the age is reached, or the calving date. | Pen move: resolved by a pen move or when the cattle meets the criteria again. Life stage: resolved by a life-stage change (UC-FE05-03). Both cancelled at exit. Suggestions never change pen or stage by themselves (GBR-25). | Owner, Manager, Technician | popup "Move Pen" or popup "Update Cattle Profile"
REM-09 | Draft calf awaiting confirmation | Calf outcome in PENDING_CONFIRMATION. | Immediately; highlighted after 3 days. | Resolved by registration or "died before registration"; cancelled when the calving is voided. | Owner, Manager | SCR-25
REM-10 | Low feed stock | ACTIVE feed item with a minimum stock level whose stock is at or below that level (GBR-104). | Immediately; shown as "Out of stock" at 0 kg. | Resolved when a posted receipt or adjustment brings the stock above the minimum level; cancelled when the item becomes inactive or its minimum level is removed. | Owner, Manager | SCR-77 (receive: SCR-79)
REM-11 | Feeding session not recorded | Session of the effective ration of an ACTIVE pen with cattle, for today (GBR-104). | Session time + 2 hours. | Resolved by a VALID feeding record for the pen, date and session; cancelled at the end of the day, when the ration ends or the pen has no cattle. | Manager, Technician | SCR-83 (record: popup "Record Feeding Session")
REM-12 | Death or loss awaiting confirmation | PENDING DEATH herd exit voucher created when a health case was closed as DIED or MISSING (GBR-95). | Immediately; highlighted after 3 days. | Resolved when the Owner confirms or dismisses the voucher; cancelled when the voucher is voided or the case closure is corrected. | Owner, Manager | SCR-43 (Owner acts; a Manager sees the status)
REM-13 | Finishing weight reached | ACTIVE cattle with purpose type FATTENING whose latest valid weight is at or above the target finishing weight (farm parameter, GBR-107). | The date of the weight record. | Resolved by a herd exit or a change of purpose; cancelled when a corrected weight falls below the target or the parameter is removed. | Owner, Manager | SCR-30 (Owner may start an exit voucher: SCR-42)
[/TABLE]
[P1342 5] 14.1.1 SCR-44 Farm Reminders
[TABLE block 1343]
[Figure to be inserted] Mockup – Figma 04 › "Nhắc việc trang trại", "Kỹ thuật viên · Nhắc việc"
[/TABLE]
[P1345 ] This screen allows farm members to view reminders and open their source (UC-FE14-01). Reading a reminder never resolves it (GBR-63).
[P1346 ] Field Description
[TABLE block 1347]
Field Name | Description
Type tabs | All, Vaccination, Calving, Breeding, Pregnancy check, Pen move & life stage, Weighing, Draft calves (Owner and Manager), Herd exit & finishing (REM-12, REM-13; Owner and Manager), Feed (low stock for the Owner and Manager; sessions not recorded for the Manager and Technician) – each with count.
Groups | Overdue (n), Today (n), Upcoming within 7 days (n).
Reminder item | 
Title | Rule text with key value (e.g. "FMD dose 2", "Breeding window 14:30–18:30", "Expected calving 22/10 – prepare calving pen").
Cattle | Display code, breed, pen (or group, e.g. "12 cattle · pen B1").

Reminder detail |

Reminder type | Rule name.
Source | Source record summary (e.g. schedule, dose, vaccine, reference dose and route, due date).
Basis | Parameter used and version; TENTATIVE / CONFIRMED label for calving. Missing basis: MSG-FE14-01.
Open cattle profile / Open source | Buttons; open SCR-19 (relevant tab) or the source screen of the rule (last column of Table III-3) after rechecking access. For a REM-08 suggestion the source is popup "Move Pen" or Update Cattle Profile (popup "Update Cattle Profile", Life stage tab), offered to the Owner and Managers only.
[/TABLE]

[P1378 3] 15. FE-15 Farm Dashboard and Reports
[P1379 ] The dashboard and reports use the indicator and report catalogues below (GBR-65). Draft calves are never counted in the active herd; each cattle is counted once per indicator.
[P1380 ] Table III-4 Dashboard indicators and attention lists
[TABLE block 1381]
Code | Indicator | Definition | Basis
M01 | Active cattle | Number of ACTIVE registered cattle, with breakdown by breed. | As-of now
M02 | Cattle needing health attention | Cattle with health state Under treatment or Monitoring (GBR-45); of which Under treatment; of which with an active meat withdrawal. | As-of now
M03 | Vaccination doses due | Doses due within 7 days; of which overdue. | As-of now
M04 | Expected calvings | Females with an expected calving date within 30 days; of which within 7 days. | As-of now
M05 | Herd structure | ACTIVE cattle by herd group (GBR-24): Breeding females (of which PREGNANT), Young stock, Fattening, Calves, Breeding bulls, Other; the groups add up to M01. | As-of now
M06 | Average daily gain | Average ADG of cattle with a computable ADG in the period, by pen and herd group (GBR-39). | Period
M07 | Weighing overdue | Cattle with an overdue REM-01. | As-of now
M08 | Pen occupancy | Headcount / capacity per ACTIVE pen and in total. | As-of now
A01 | Needs attention today | Overdue vaccination doses, breeding windows today, pregnancy checks due, calvings within 7 days, cases opened or worsened today. | As-of now
A02 | Draft calves awaiting confirmation | Calf outcomes in PENDING_CONFIRMATION. | As-of now
A03 | Pending herd exit vouchers | PENDING death vouchers waiting for the Owner (GBR-95, REM-12). | As-of now
A04 | Finishing weight reached | FATTENING cattle at or above the target finishing weight (REM-13), with pen and latest weight. | As-of now
M09 | Reproduction at a glance | Average calving interval and overall conception rate of the last 12 months, and females overdue for conception (GBR-106). | Last 12 months / as-of now
[/TABLE]
[P1383 ] Table III-5 Management report catalogue
[TABLE block 1384]
Code | Report | Content | Unit and period
RPT-01 | Herd summary | Head count by herd group, pen, breed, purpose and life stage at the end date; entries and exits in the period by source and type. | Head; as-of end date and period
RPT-02 | Growth performance | Number of valid weighings, average weight and ADG by pen, herd group and life stage; share of estimated weights. | Weighings, kg, kg/day; period
RPT-03 | Vaccination compliance | Per schedule and dose: planned, done, deferred, overdue in the period; on-farm vaccinations by vaccine. | Doses; period
RPT-04 | Health and treatment | Cases opened, moved to chronic monitoring and closed by outcome and severity; CHRONIC cases at the end date; treatments by medicine; cattle with an active meat withdrawal (from treatments or vaccinations) at the end date. | Cases, treatments, head; period
RPT-05 | Reproduction performance | Females tracked (reproductive status applicable, GBR-54) by status; breedings by method; checks by result; calvings, live calves and stillbirths; and the indicators of GBR-106 with their n: average calving interval, average days open, services per conception, first-service and overall conception rate, pregnancy loss rate, average age at first calving, and the list of females overdue for conception. | Females, services, checks, calvings; period
RPT-06 | Herd movement | Herd entries by source, exits by type, pen moves by pen. | Events; period
RPT-07 | Fattening performance | For FATTENING cattle by pen and in total (GBR-107): average head, cattle included, average start and end weight, total gain, ADG, feed used, feed per kg gain, feed cost per kg gain, cattle that reached the target finishing weight and the average projected days to the target; cattle without enough weights are counted as "Insufficient weights". | Head, kg, kg/day, kg feed/kg gain, đ/kg gain; period
[/TABLE]

[P1416 3] 18. FE-18 Farm Master Data and Reference Configuration
[P1417 ] A new farm starts with the starter catalogue of Table III-6 and the default parameter version, so that the first herd entry, vaccination schedule and feeding record can be made without setting up every list first (GBR-109). All starter items are ordinary farm items that the Owner or a Manager can edit or deactivate.
[P1418 ] Table III-6 Starter catalogue created with a new farm
[TABLE block 1419]
Catalogue | Code · name | Type / group | Starter values

Production purpose | MD-DG · Đực giống | Sire | ACTIVE
Production purpose | MD-CXD · Chưa xác định | Undecided | ACTIVE; default purpose of farm-born calves
Breed | VANG · Bò Vàng Việt Nam | Local | ACTIVE when chosen as a main breed at registration, otherwise INACTIVE
Breed | LAISIND · Lai Sind | Crossbred | As above
Breed | ZEBU-D · Zebu đỏ | Zebu crossbred | As above
Breed | BRAHMAN-D · Brahman đỏ | Zebu | As above
Breed | BRAHMAN-T · Brahman trắng | Zebu | As above
Breed | DROUGHT · Droughtmaster | Crossbred | As above
Breed | F1-BBB · F1 BBB × Lai Sind | Crossbred | As above
Breed | F1-CHA · F1 Charolais × Lai Sind | Crossbred | As above

Vaccine | VX-LMLM · Vắc-xin Lở mồm long móng | Foot-and-mouth disease | ACTIVE; dose, route and booster empty, withdrawal 0 – to be completed from the product label

[/TABLE]
[P1421 ] Reference medicines are not pre-created: dose and meat withdrawal depend on the product and must be entered from the label by the farm. The farm parameters are created with the default values of SCR-56.
[P1422 ] All catalogue screens share one page with the tabs Breeds (SCR-52), Vaccines (SCR-53), Reference medicines (SCR-54), Production purposes (SCR-55) and State & reminder parameters (SCR-56); the page opens on Breeds from the menu Catalogues & Parameters. Each catalogue has a list, a search box, a status filter (All, ACTIVE, INACTIVE) and an editor panel. Codes are unique per catalogue (BR-D12) and referenced items can only be deactivated (GBR-67).

[P1601 3] 23. FE-23 Feed Management
[P1602 ] Feed Management is a page of the Care menu with the tabs Stock, Feed Receipts, Stock Count, Rations, Feeding Log, Catalogue and Report. Stock is kept in kg per feed item and changes only through posted receipts, stock adjustments and feeding records (GBR-99); it is never negative (BR-D36). The Owner and Managers manage stock, rations and the catalogue; only Managers record feeding; Technicians view rations and the feeding log (GBR-103).
[P1603 4] 23.1 Feed Stock
[P1604 5] 23.1.1 SCR-77 Feed Stock

4. Payment | Goods amount, transport fee, total payable, amount paid (≤ total payable), outstanding, method, balance due date (GBR-91).
Summary panel | Number of items, total kg, stock before → after for each feed, status; information when a feed leaves the Low state.
Checks before saving | Feeds ACTIVE; quantity > 0 and unit price ≥ 0; expiry after the receipt date; receipt date not in the future.
Save & add to stock / Save draft / Cancel | Buttons; MSG-FE23-01; MSG-FE23-02; MSG-COM-14. After saving the receipt list (SCR-78) opens.
[/TABLE]
