[P1693 3] 24. Background Job Inventory
[P1694 4] 24.1 Evaluate Farm Reminders (JOB-01)
[P1695 ] Trigger: Scheduled every 15 minutes, and event-driven after each committed change of a source record (weight, estrus, breeding, check, calving, dose, schedule, pen criteria, profile, parameters, feed stock movement, ration, feeding record).
[P1696 ] Purpose: Keep the farm reminders REM-01 to REM-13 consistent with the source records and the effective parameters (Table III-3, GBR-63).
[P1697 ] Processing steps:
[P1698 ] 1. Collect the farms and sources to evaluate: the changed sources for an event run, all ACTIVE farms for a scheduled run.
[P1699 ] 2. Apply parameter versions whose effective time has been reached and load the effective version of each farm.
[P1700 ] 3. For each rule, find the candidate sources and calculate the due date or window; for REM-08 compare each ACTIVE cattle with the criteria of its current pen, and its age (from the birth date) with the life-stage ages, and check first calvings of females not yet Adult; for REM-10 compare the stock of each ACTIVE feed item with its minimum level; for REM-11 compare the sessions of each effective ration whose time + 2 hours has passed today with the feeding records.
[P1701 ] 4. Create or update each reminder by (rule, source, occurrence key) (BR-D22); unchanged reminders are not rewritten.
[P1702 ] 5. Resolve, cancel or supersede reminders whose condition no longer holds.
[P1703 ] 6. Log the number of created, updated, resolved and cancelled reminders per farm.
[P1704 ] Failure behavior:
[TABLE block 1705]
Scenario | System behavior
Database unreachable | Retries 3 times at 1-minute intervals, then logs an error. The next run recomputes everything; reminders are idempotent.
Parameter missing for a rule | The rule is skipped for that farm; screens show MSG-FE14-01. Other rules continue.
Error in one farm | The farm is skipped and logged; other farms are processed.
[/TABLE]
[P1707 4] 24.2 Expire Farm Invitations (JOB-02)
[P1708 ] Trigger: Scheduled every 15 minutes.
[P1709 ] Purpose: Mark invitations that passed their validity as EXPIRED (GBR-16).
[P1710 ] Processing steps:
[P1711 ] 1. Find PENDING invitations whose expiry time is before now.
[P1712 ] 2. Set them EXPIRED and save the history.
[P1713 ] 3. Log the number of expired invitations.
[P1714 ] Failure behavior:
[TABLE block 1715]
Scenario | System behavior
Job fails or is delayed | Acceptance and resend check the expiry time in real time, so an expired link never grants access. The next run expires the remaining invitations.
No invitation to expire | The job ends without action.
[/TABLE]
[P1717 4] 24.3 Expire Unpaid Payment Requests (JOB-03)
[P1718 ] Trigger: Scheduled every 5 minutes.
[P1719 ] Purpose: Mark PENDING payment requests older than 24 hours as EXPIRED and inform the Owner (GBR-70).
[P1720 ] Processing steps:
[P1721 ] 1. Find PENDING requests whose expiry time is before now.
[P1722 ] 2. Set them EXPIRED (never a PAID request) and create the Owner notification.
[P1723 ] 3. Log the number of expired requests.
[P1724 ] Failure behavior:
[TABLE block 1725]
Scenario | System behavior
Job fails or is delayed | Request creation and confirmation check the expiry in real time. Evidence paid inside the original window still confirms the request (UC-FE19-08 AF3).
Concurrent confirmation | The confirmation and the expiry are serialized per farm; a PAID request is never set EXPIRED.
[/TABLE]
[P1727 4] 24.4 Evaluate Subscription Coverage (JOB-04)
[P1728 ] Trigger: Scheduled every 15 minutes.
[P1729 ] Purpose: Update the farm service status when a purchased period starts or ends (GBR-13).
[P1730 ] Processing steps:
[P1731 ] 1. Find farms with a purchased period that started or ended since the previous run.
[P1732 ] 2. Recalculate the service status: ACTIVE when a period covers now, EXPIRED when coverage has ended, PENDING_PAYMENT when no period was ever accepted; SUSPENDED is never changed by the job.
[P1733 ] 3. Save the status change and audit entry; create an "expired" notification for the Owner.
[P1734 ] Failure behavior:
[TABLE block 1735]
Scenario | System behavior
Job fails or is delayed | Every request derives the service status from the periods in real time, so access is always correct; only notifications are delayed.
Database unreachable | Retries at the next run.
[/TABLE]
[P1737 4] 24.5 Generate Subscription Notices (JOB-05)
[P1738 ] Trigger: Scheduled daily at 07:00 (Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh).
[P1739 ] Purpose: Inform Owners about renewal and expiry of each farm's coverage.
[P1740 ] Processing steps:
[P1741 ] 1. For each farm with a confirmed end date, determine whether today is the renewal opening day (end − 90 days), 30, 7 or 1 day before the end, or the day coverage expired.
[P1742 ] 2. Create one in-app notification and one email per notice type, farm, period and Owner (deduplicated).
[P1743 ] 3. Log the number of notices.
[P1744 ] Failure behavior:
[TABLE block 1745]
Scenario | System behavior
Job fails | The next run creates missed notices for the current day only; no duplicates are created.
Owner changed meanwhile | Notices go to the current Owner of the farm.
[/TABLE]
[P1747 4] 24.6 Process Received Bank Evidence (JOB-06)
[P1748 ] Trigger: Queue; runs continuously for evidence stored by API-02 or API-03.
[P1749 ] Purpose: Run the shared payment confirmation for each authenticated evidence exactly once (GBR-72, GBR-73).
[P1750 ] Processing steps:
[P1751 ] 1. Take the next evidence with pending processing.
[P1752 ] 2. Lock processing per farm and evaluate eligibility (direction, receiving account, payment reference, amount, bank-paid time window, allocation, manual income conflict).
[P1753 ] 3. Eligible: commit allocation, PAID, purchased period, service status, receipt and audit in one transaction; create the Owner notification.
[P1754 ] 4. Not eligible: mark the evidence NEEDS_REVIEW with the reason and create a PM reconciliation notification.
[P1755 ] 5. Mark the processing done.
[P1756 ] Failure behavior:
[TABLE block 1757]
Scenario | System behavior
Transient error | Retries after 1, 5 and 15 minutes, then hourly for 24 hours; then a PM notification is created. Evidence is never lost.
Repeated processing | Returns the committed result; no second period, receipt or notification (GBR-88).
[/TABLE]
[P1759 4] 24.7 Dispatch Notifications and Emails (JOB-07)
[P1760 ] Trigger: Scheduled every minute.
[P1761 ] Purpose: Deliver notifications and emails for committed business events (GBR-64).
[P1762 ] Processing steps:
[P1763 ] 1. Read undelivered events from the outbox written with each business change.
[P1764 ] 2. Resolve the current recipients and recheck their access.
[P1765 ] 3. Create the in-app notifications and send the emails (API-04); deduplicate by recipient and event.
[P1766 ] 4. Create a reminder notice to the recipient of an internal request that has been OPEN for 4 hours.
[P1767 ] 5. Mark the events delivered.
[P1768 ] Failure behavior:
[TABLE block 1769]
Scenario | System behavior
Email provider error | Retries with increasing delay for up to 24 hours; the in-app notification is still created; the business change is never rolled back.
Recipient lost access | The notification is not created; the event is closed.
[/TABLE]
[P1771 4] 24.8 Purge Expired Tokens and Snapshots (JOB-08)
[P1772 ] Trigger: Scheduled daily at 02:00.
[P1773 ] Purpose: Remove technical data that is no longer needed.
[P1774 ] Processing steps:
[P1775 ] 1. Delete verification, reset and invitation link token hashes that expired more than 7 days ago (invitation records and statuses are kept).
[P1776 ] 2. Mark cash report snapshots older than 30 days EXPIRED and delete their rows.
[P1777 ] 3. Process in batches of up to 5,000 records and log the counts.
[P1778 ] Failure behavior:
[TABLE block 1779]
Scenario | System behavior
Timeout during a batch | The batch is rolled back; the job continues at the next run. No business record is affected.
[/TABLE]
[P1781 3] 25. External API Inventory
[P1782 4] 25.1 GET: VietQR image (API-01)
[TABLE block 1783]
Item | Value
URL | GET https://qr.sepay.vn/img?acc={account_number}&bank={bank_code}&amount={amount}&des={payment_reference}
Purpose | Render the VietQR image of a payment request on Transfer Instructions (SCR-59).
Caller | The Owner's browser loads the image URL built by the BeefCare backend from the immutable request snapshot (BeefCare → SePay QR service).
Auth | None (public image service); the URL contains no secret. Displaying the QR confirms nothing.
[/TABLE]
[P1785 4] 25.2 POST: SePay transaction webhook (API-02)
[TABLE block 1786]
Item | Value
URL | POST /api/v1/payments/sepay/webhook (one endpoint per environment: Sandbox, Live)
Purpose | Receive each bank transaction on the receiving account, store it as evidence and start payment confirmation (UC-FE19-08).
Caller | SePay.
Auth | HMAC-SHA256: headers X-SePay-Signature (sha256=hex) and X-SePay-Timestamp; signature over "{timestamp}.{raw body}" with the environment secret; 300-second tolerance.
Data | id (deduplication key), gateway, transactionDate (bank time, Vietnam time), accountNumber, code, content, transferType (in/out), transferAmount, referenceCode, description. Response HTTP 200 with body {"success": true} within 30 seconds after the evidence is stored.
[/TABLE]
[P1788 4] 25.3 GET: SePay transaction lookup (API-03)
[TABLE block 1789]
Item | Value
URL | GET https://my.sepay.vn/userapi/transactions/list (filters: account number, transaction date range, limit) and GET https://my.sepay.vn/userapi/transactions/details/{id}
Purpose | Let the PM find a transaction that was not delivered by webhook and store it as evidence for reconciliation (UC-FE19-06).
Caller | BeefCare backend, on request from the PM workbench (SCR-62).
Auth | Bearer API token per environment, stored server-side only.
[/TABLE]
[P1791 4] 25.4 POST: Transactional email (API-04)
[TABLE block 1792]
Item | Value
URL | Provider send-mail API or SMTP submission (provider to be selected at deployment).
Purpose | Send invitation, verification, password-reset, password-changed, subscription and support emails (JOB-07).
Caller | BeefCare backend.
Auth | Provider API key or SMTP credentials stored server-side; TLS.
[/TABLE]
[P1794 4] 25.5 OAuth 2.0 / OpenID Connect: Google sign-in (API-05)
[TABLE block 1795]
Item | Value
URL | GET https://accounts.google.com/o/oauth2/v2/auth (scope openid email profile, state, nonce, PKCE); POST https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token (code exchange); callback GET /api/v1/auth/google/callback
Purpose | Authenticate a person with Google for registration, sign-in and invitation acceptance (UC-FE01-01, UC-FE01-02, UC-FE03-04).
Caller | The user's browser (redirects) and the BeefCare backend (code exchange).
Auth | OAuth client ID and secret stored server-side; the ID token signature and claims (iss, aud, exp, nonce, email_verified) are validated.
[/TABLE]
[P1797 2] IV. Non-Functional Requirements
[P1798 ] The requirements below are acceptance targets for the capstone deployment. They apply to the responsive BeefCare web application, its backend and its integrations.
[P1799 3] 1. External Interfaces
[P1800 4] 1.1 User Interfaces
[TABLE block 1801]
ID | Requirement
NFR-I01 | BeefCare is one responsive web application for desktop (≥ 1280 px), tablet (≥ 768 px) and phone (≥ 360 px) browsers; all authorized workflows are available without a native application.
NFR-I02 | Forms, dialogs and navigation adapt to the device: on phones tables become cards, the sidebar becomes a bottom navigation and dialogs open full screen; required actions and validation messages are never hidden. Wide tables may scroll inside their own container.
NFR-I03 | Cattle QR scanning uses the device camera through the browser (HTTPS); when the camera is unavailable or the tag unreadable, the user can enter the display code, ear tag or tag code (SCR-22).
NFR-I04 | Cattle QR tags and payment VietQR codes are named and displayed differently; a payment QR never opens a cattle and a cattle QR never starts a payment (OR-03).
NFR-I05 | The farm interface is in Vietnamese with consistent terminology for roles, statuses and events across devices (OR-01).
[/TABLE]
[P1803 4] 1.2 Hardware Interfaces
[TABLE block 1804]
ID | Requirement
NFR-I06 | Only standard devices are needed: a phone with a camera and a modern browser for field use, a desktop or laptop for management, and any printer able to print the QR ear tag layout (OR-02). No IoT sensor, RFID reader or scale integration is required.
[/TABLE]
[P1806 4] 1.3 Software Interfaces
[TABLE block 1807]
ID | Requirement
NFR-I07 | Google Identity (API-05): OAuth 2.0 / OpenID Connect sign-in; BeefCare validates the ID token and the verified email and never receives Google passwords.
NFR-I08 | SePay (API-01 to API-03): bank-transaction webhooks for the subscription receiving account only, transaction lookup and VietQR images. Sandbox and Live use separate endpoints, accounts, tokens and secrets with the same business rules (OR-09).
NFR-I09 | Email Delivery Service (API-04): transactional emails sent after the business change is committed, with retry; delivery never changes business state.
NFR-I10 | Supported browsers: the two latest major versions of Chrome, Edge, Firefox and Safari; Chrome on Android 10+ and Safari on iOS 15+.
NFR-I11 | Technology platform of the capstone implementation: Java 21 with Spring Boot 3 (backend), Next.js with TypeScript (frontend), PostgreSQL (database), Docker containers.
[/TABLE]
[P1809 4] 1.4 Communications Interfaces
[TABLE block 1810]
ID | Requirement
NFR-I12 | All browser and integration traffic uses HTTPS (TLS 1.2 or higher) with a valid certificate; the SePay Live webhook endpoint is public HTTPS.
NFR-I13 | Dates exchanged with SePay are interpreted in Vietnam time (Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh); the backend stores timestamps with time zone.
[/TABLE]
[P1812 3] 2. Quality Attributes
[P1813 4] 2.1 Usability
[TABLE block 1814]
ID | Requirement
NFR-U01 | A trained Technician opens the correct cattle profile by scanning its tag in at most 2 taps from the home screen, without searching lists.
NFR-U02 | A Farm Manager records a routine event (weight, estrus, vaccination of one animal) from the open profile in under 60 seconds; field forms show only the fields needed and mark required fields.
NFR-U03 | Touch targets on phone screens are at least 44 × 44 px; all actions are also usable with a keyboard on desktop.
NFR-U04 | Measured and estimated weights, UNKNOWN and negative history, TENTATIVE and CONFIRMED dates, and scheduled and performed doses are always labelled distinctly (OR-10).
NFR-U05 | Error messages say what to correct, do not expose internal or other-tenant information, and a failed or pending save is never shown as successful.
NFR-U06 | Buttons for actions the role cannot perform are hidden; the current farm and role are always visible in the top bar; switching farm clears the previous farm's data and filters.
NFR-U07 | Subscription status, payment status and suspension are shown separately; PAID never implies that a SUSPENDED farm is usable.
NFR-U08 | A new Farm Owner completes registration, farm registration and the payment request in at most 4 screens (SCR-03 / SCR-07, SCR-10, SCR-58, SCR-59).
[/TABLE]
[P1816 4] 2.2 Performance
[P1817 ] Targets are measured on the capstone deployment with a reference data set of 3 farms of about 300 active cattle each and 2 years of history.
[TABLE block 1818]
Requirement | Target | Condition
NFR-P01 Page load | Standard pages display usable content within 3 seconds | Up to 100 concurrent authenticated users, normal 4G or office network
NFR-P02 QR resolution | A valid scan opens the cattle profile within 2 seconds after the code is read | Same as NFR-P01
NFR-P03 Form submission | The system returns success or validation errors within 2 seconds | Same as NFR-P01
NFR-P04 Dashboard and reports | Dashboard and reports with normal filters complete within 5 seconds | A farm of about 300 active cattle with 2 years of history
NFR-P05 Payment confirmation | An eligible SePay transaction updates the payment and service status within 10 seconds of receipt | Normal backend load
NFR-P06 Concurrency | At least 100 concurrent authenticated users without loss of core functionality or the targets above | Single-region capstone deployment
NFR-P07 Reminder freshness | Reminders reflect a saved source change within 1 minute (event run) and within 15 minutes in any case | JOB-01 running
[/TABLE]
[P1820 4] 2.3 Security
[TABLE block 1821]
Requirement | Description
NFR-S01 Authentication required | Every page and action requires sign-in except the public landing page, sign-in, registration, email verification, password reset and the SePay webhook (which uses its own authentication).
NFR-S02 Authorization on every request | The backend checks account status, tenant, membership, role, platform role, assignment and farm service status on every protected request; hidden buttons are never the only protection (GBR-09).
NFR-S03 Tenant isolation | No query, list, count, export, cache, notification or link returns data of a farm the user cannot access; cross-tenant access is treated as a critical defect.
NFR-S04 Password protection | Passwords are stored only as salted Argon2id or bcrypt hashes and are never logged or returned; 8–128 characters; lockout of password sign-in for 15 minutes after 5 failures (GBR-02).
NFR-S05 Links and tokens | Verification, reset and invitation tokens are random (≥ 128 bits), single-use, time-limited and stored only as hashes (GBR-03).
NFR-S06 Session security | Sessions use secure, HTTP-only cookies; sessions end on sign-out, password change (other sessions), account suspension and inactivity (8 hours, or 30 days with Remember me).
NFR-S07 Secrets | Provider tokens, webhook secrets and OAuth secrets are kept server-side in environment configuration, never in the client bundle or source control; Sandbox and Live secrets are separate.
NFR-S08 Webhook authenticity | SePay webhooks are accepted only with a valid HMAC-SHA256 signature within 300 seconds; invalid calls create no evidence (API-02).
NFR-S09 QR is not authorization | A scanned code is resolved only inside a farm the user may access (GBR-28).
NFR-S10 Least privilege for platform staff | CS, PM and SA access only the data of their use cases; no platform role sees cattle, care or reproduction records; internal requests expose only allowlisted context (GBR-11, GBR-81).
NFR-S11 Audit | Important changes are written to the audit log in the same transaction with actor, role, time and allowlisted before/after values; audit entries cannot be modified (GBR-86).
NFR-S12 Sensitive logging | Logs and error messages never contain passwords, tokens, secrets, full bank evidence payloads or ticket bodies.
NFR-S13 Access withdrawal | Membership deactivation, role change, account suspension and platform access revocation take effect at the next request of existing sessions and old notification links.
[/TABLE]
[P1823 4] 2.4 Reliability and Data Integrity
[TABLE block 1824]
Requirement | Description
NFR-R01 Atomic changes | Each command saves the record, its history, its audit and its outbox event in one transaction; a failure leaves no partial change (GBR-88).
NFR-R02 Idempotency | Repeated submissions, webhook retries and job re-runs return the first result without duplicates (GBR-88, BR-D23, BR-D24).
NFR-R03 Concurrency control | Updates use optimistic versions (GBR-89); changes to the same female's pregnancy and to the same farm's payments are serialized (GBR-52, GBR-72).
NFR-R04 Availability | Target availability 99 % per month during the capstone pilot, excluding announced maintenance.
NFR-R05 Backup | The database is backed up daily with 14 days of retention; a restore is tested before the pilot.
NFR-R06 Time handling | All timestamps are stored with time zone; farm dates use the farm time zone and platform finance uses Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh (GBR-66).
[/TABLE]
[P1826 2] V. Requirement Appendix
[P1827 3] 1. Business Rules
[P1828 ] The business rules below are referenced by the business flows (I.2), use case specifications (II) and functional requirements (III). Data integrity rules that follow directly from the conceptual model are listed separately in I.3.3 (BR-Dxx).
[P1829 4] 1.1 Access, Accounts and Roles
[TABLE block 1830]
GBR-ID | Rule Statement | Applies To | Rationale | Enforced By
GBR-01 | One person has one global BeefCare account, identified by a verified email (case-insensitive). A Google identity and a BeefCare password with the same verified email belong to the same account. A contact phone number is contact information only and is never used for identity or access. | FE-01, FE-03 | Prevents duplicate accounts and keeps one history per person across farms. | System, on registration, sign-in and profile update
GBR-02 | A BeefCare password has 8–128 characters and must not equal the email address. It is stored only as a salted one-way hash and is never displayed, logged or returned. After 5 consecutive failed password sign-ins, password sign-in is locked for 15 minutes; Google sign-in stays available. | UC-FE01-01, 02, 05, 06 | Protects accounts against guessing attacks without locking out Google users. | System, on every password operation
GBR-03 | An email-verification link is valid for 24 hours and a password-reset link for 30 minutes. Each link is random, single-use and stored only as a hash; issuing a new link invalidates the previous one of the same type. The reset request always returns the same response whether or not the email is registered. | UC-FE01-01, UC-FE01-05 | Limits the value of a leaked link and prevents account discovery. | System, on link issue and use
GBR-04 | Only an ACTIVE account can sign in. A SUSPENDED account is blocked for every sign-in method and its existing sessions are rejected on the next request. Restoring an account does not restore any revoked role or membership. | FE-01, UC-FE03-08 | Suspension must take effect immediately and safely. | System, on every request
GBR-05 | A Guest becomes a Farm Owner only by completing Owner registration (full name, terms acceptance). Owner registration grants no farm, membership, platform role or service; a farm is created only through UC-FE02-01. | UC-FE01-01, UC-FE02-01 | Separates personal account creation from farm tenancy. | System, on save
GBR-06 | Each farm role has a fixed permission set; there are no per-member permission grants. Farm Owner: views all farm data and performs the Owner-only actions in GBR-07 plus farm-structure actions (pens, pen moves, herd entry, cattle profile and QR, draft-calf confirmation, announcements, all farm catalogues (breeds, vaccines, reference medicines, production purposes) and farm parameters). Farm Manager: records and corrects all care and reproduction events, maintains pens, herd entry, cattle profile and QR, catalogues and parameters, confirms draft calves and writes announcements to Technicians. Farm Technician: read-only access to cattle, pens, care and reproduction data, reminders and received announcements. | FE-03 to FE-18 | Predictable, easy-to-explain roles for small farms; matches the field division of work. | System, on every request (I.4.4)
GBR-07 | Owner-only actions: invite members and manage membership and roles, manage the subscription and payments, create, confirm, dismiss and void herd exit vouchers and record their collections, change a cattle's production purpose and update the farm's registration and operating information. These actions cannot be performed by any other role. | FE-02, FE-03, FE-05, FE-13, FE-19 | Ownership decisions and money stay with the legal owner of the farm. | System, on every request
GBR-08 | Care and reproduction events (growth, vaccination, health, estrus, breeding, pregnancy check, pregnancy update, calving) are recorded, corrected and voided only by a Farm Manager. The Farm Owner views them. A Farm Technician can be selected as the observer or performer of an event that a Manager records. | FE-08 to FE-12 | One accountable recorder per event; the Owner supervises rather than enters field data. | System, on save
GBR-09 | Every protected request rechecks the account status, the user's current membership and role in the selected farm (or current platform role) and the farm service status. The selected farm in the browser, a link, a notification or a QR code is never proof of permission. | All authenticated UCs | Access changes must apply immediately, including to open sessions. | System, on every request
GBR-10 | Platform staff access (CS, PM, SA) is granted, changed, revoked or restored only by a System Admin, only to an existing account with a verified email, and is independent of farm membership. A user has at most one ACTIVE platform role. The first System Admin is created at deployment and at least one ACTIVE System Admin must always remain. | UC-FE03-07 to 09 | Keeps platform administration controlled and recoverable. | System, on save
GBR-11 | Platform roles never receive farm operational access. CS, PM and SA see only the farm data their own use cases require (service status, subscription and payment data, ticket context, account summary); cattle, care and reproduction records are never shown to them. | FE-02, FE-17, FE-19 to FE-21 | Farm data privacy. | System, on every request
[/TABLE]
[P1832 4] 1.2 Farm, Service and Membership
[TABLE block 1833]
GBR-ID | Rule Statement | Applies To | Rationale | Enforced By
GBR-12 | One farm is one independent tenant with exactly one Owner. One Owner may own several farms; each farm has its own members, data, subscription and service status. Data of one farm is never shown in the context of another farm. | All farm UCs | Multi-farm owners must keep farms separate. | System, on every request
GBR-13 | Farm service status is derived: PENDING_PAYMENT from registration until the first accepted payment; ACTIVE while an accepted purchased period covers the current time; EXPIRED when coverage has ended; SUSPENDED while a Platform Manager suspension is in force, regardless of payment. | FE-02, FE-19 | One clear rule for service availability. | System, on every request and JOB-04
GBR-14 | Farm operations (all farm UCs except those below) require service status ACTIVE. While the farm is not ACTIVE the Owner can still select the farm, view and update farm information, manage the subscription and payments, use support, cancel pending invitations and deactivate members. Managers and Technicians cannot open a farm that is not ACTIVE. | FE-02 to FE-21 | The Owner must be able to pay, get help and withdraw access while service is inactive. | System, on every request
GBR-15 | Suspension and payment are independent: an accepted payment never lifts a suspension, and restoring a suspended farm never creates or extends a purchased period. After restore the status is derived again from the subscription (GBR-13). Suspend and restore require a reason and are kept in Farm Service History. | UC-FE02-05, FE-19 | Administrative and commercial decisions must not override each other. | System, on save
GBR-16 | Only the Owner invites members, only for an ACTIVE farm, with role Farm Manager or Farm Technician. At most one PENDING invitation exists per farm and email. An invitation is valid for the period chosen by the Owner (3, 7 or 14 days; default 7) counted from the last send, unless accepted or cancelled; resending keeps the same invitation and restarts its validity. ACCEPTED, CANCELLED and EXPIRED are final. | UC-FE03-01 to 03 | Controlled, traceable onboarding of staff. | System, on save and JOB-02
GBR-17 | An invitation is accepted only by a signed-in account whose verified email equals the invited email, while the invitation is PENDING and unexpired and the farm is ACTIVE. Acceptance creates exactly one membership; a user has at most one membership per farm, and repeating the acceptance returns the existing membership. | UC-FE03-04 | Prevents forwarded links from granting access to the wrong person. | System, on acceptance
GBR-18 | The Owner membership cannot be changed or deactivated. The Owner may switch a member between Farm Manager and Farm Technician and deactivate or reactivate a membership, each with an effective date and reason. Deactivation is always allowed; role change and reactivation require an ACTIVE farm. A membership is never deleted. | UC-FE03-06 | Keeps history of who did what while allowing staff changes. | System, on save
[/TABLE]
[P1835 4] 1.3 Pens, Cattle Identity and Herd Lifecycle
[TABLE block 1836]
GBR-ID | Rule Statement | Applies To | Rationale | Enforced By
GBR-19 | Pen code is unique within a farm and capacity is a whole number of head of at least 1. A pen with placement history is never deleted; it is made INACTIVE instead. Capacity cannot be reduced below the current headcount. | FE-04 | Staff identify pens by the code on the barn sign; physical limits are real. | System, on save
GBR-20 | A placement or move into an INACTIVE pen, or one that would exceed the pen capacity, is blocked. Stopping new placements never moves the cattle already in the pen. | UC-FE04-04, UC-FE04-05, UC-FE06-01 | Capacity and availability are hard physical constraints. | System, on save
GBR-21 | Pen reception criteria (accepted purpose types, life stages, sex and optional minimum age or weight) are advisory. When a cattle does not meet them, the system shows a warning and the user may continue only after entering a reason, which is stored with the placement. A change of criteria creates a new version that applies to future placements and suggestions only. | FE-04, UC-FE06-01 | Farms sometimes need exceptions; the reason keeps them traceable. | System, on save
GBR-22 | A cattle has at most one open pen assignment. A move closes the current assignment and opens the new one in one transaction. The move date is not earlier than the start of the current assignment and not later than today, so periods never overlap. A mistaken move is corrected by a reverse move with the reason "Correction". | UC-FE04-05 | Accurate pen history and occupancy. | System, on save
GBR-23 | The Internal Cattle ID is generated once, never changes and is never reused. The display code (BC-nnnnnn) is generated once from a farm sequence. The secondary ear-tag number is editable and must be unique among ACTIVE cattle of the farm. | FE-05, FE-06 | One permanent identity per animal for its whole life. | System, on save
GBR-24 | Herd status (ACTIVE, EXITED, VOIDED), production purpose, life stage, current pen, health state and reproductive status are separate attributes (3.4.1). Exit pending, current pen, health state (GBR-45), meat withdrawal (GBR-46), reproductive status (GBR-54) and herd group are derived from their source records and are never edited on the profile; they are shown only for ACTIVE cattle. An ACTIVE cattle always has exactly one open pen assignment; an EXITED or VOIDED cattle has none. The herd group of an ACTIVE cattle is the first that matches, in this order: Calves (life stage UNWEANED_CALF or WEANED_CALF); Fattening (purpose type FATTENING); Breeding bulls (male, purpose type SIRE); Breeding females (female, ADULT, purpose type BREEDING); Young stock (YOUNG_STOCK); Other. Herd groups are used by the herd list, vaccination targets and herd reports. | FE-05, FE-09, FE-15 | Each state has one source of truth; every animal is counted once. | Design rule
GBR-25 | Production purpose is changed only by the Owner; life stage, ear tag number, name, colour/markings and notes by the Owner or a Manager. Life stage is physiological, from a fixed list: UNWEANED_CALF, WEANED_CALF, YOUNG_STOCK, ADULT. Each production purpose in the farm catalogue has one purpose type (BREEDING, FATTENING, SIRE, UNDECIDED), chosen when it is created and fixed once the purpose is referenced; rules use the type, never the purpose name. A purpose of type SIRE can be given only to a male and a purpose of type BREEDING only to a female, at herd entry, at calving registration and on profile change (MSG-FE05-12). A purpose or stage change needs an effective date and reason and is kept as history. The life stage is proposed from age at herd entry; later stage changes are suggested by REM-08 when the cattle reaches the weaning, young-stock or adult age (farm parameters) or has a first calving. Suggestions never change purpose, stage or pen by themselves. | UC-FE05-03, UC-FE06-01, FE-14 | Purpose is a business decision; stage follows the animal's age; the history explains every change. | System, on save
GBR-26 | An EXITED or VOIDED cattle is read-only: no new event, profile change, pen move or tag change is accepted, and its full history stays visible. Voiding the exit (UC-FE13-03) returns an EXITED cattle to ACTIVE; VOIDED is final. While a cattle is on a PENDING death voucher (GBR-95) no new care or reproduction event, pen move, profile change or exit line is accepted (MSG-COM-17); corrections and voids of existing records remain possible until the Owner confirms or dismisses the voucher. | FE-04 to FE-13, FE-16 | No data is added to an animal that has left the farm. | System, on save
GBR-27 | A cattle has at most one ACTIVE QR tag. Replacing a tag needs a reason (LOST, DAMAGED, OTHER) and marks the old tag REPLACED; a replaced tag is never reactivated. Scanning a replaced tag opens the same cattle with a notice. Replacement never changes the Internal Cattle ID or history. | UC-FE05-04, UC-FE05-05 | A lost tag must not break traceability. | System, on save and scan
GBR-28 | Possessing a QR code is not authorization. A scan resolves a cattle only within a farm the user may access; a cattle of another accessible farm opens only after switching farm; an unknown code, a code of another farm or a payment VietQR reveals no cattle data. When the tag address is opened outside the app, the person must sign in first; there is no public cattle page. | UC-FE05-05 | Data isolation between farms. | System, on scan
GBR-29 | Herd entry source is EXTERNAL (bought or brought in, on a PURCHASE voucher) or FARM_BORN (a live calf from a recorded calving, on a FARM_BORN voucher). Each herd entry is one line of a herd entry voucher (GBR-90). Each cattle has exactly one herd entry and each live calf outcome is registered at most once; a repeated submission returns the first result. An animal that left the herd and returns is registered on a new purchase voucher with a new Internal Cattle ID. | FE-06 | One entry per animal; no duplicates from double submission. | System, on save
GBR-30 | Pre-entry history is declared per category (vaccination, disease, reproduction) as KNOWN, NONE or UNKNOWN; UNKNOWN is never shown as negative. Known vaccinations are saved as vaccination records with source HISTORICAL and create no scheduled dose. Past treatments are not recorded at entry. For a female the reproduction declaration may give the number of earlier calvings, which is added to her parity, and a pregnancy at entry with estimated months; a declared pregnancy is shown on the profile as "Declared pregnant ~n months (not checked)", creates REM-03 and opens no pregnancy episode until a PREGNANT check is recorded (GBR-53). | UC-FE06-01 | Honest records of what is and is not known about bought-in cattle. | System, on save
GBR-31 | For a farm-born calf, birth date = calving time, dam = the calving dam, sire = the sire of the breeding linked to the pregnancy (UNKNOWN when none), and the proposed pen = the pen chosen at calving. Only a LIVE outcome can be registered. | UC-FE06-01, UC-FE12-04 | Links the calf to its parents without re-entry. | System, on save
GBR-32 | Posting a herd entry voucher creates, for each line, the cattle, its herd entry, history declarations, first pen assignment and QR tag in one transaction; an error on any line posts nothing. After posting the system lists the vaccination schedules whose target criteria match the new cattle; a cattle joins a schedule only when a Manager adds it (UC-FE09-03). | UC-FE06-01, FE-09 | No partial animal; vaccination is never planned silently. | System, on save
GBR-33 | The herd entry of one cattle (entry date, source information, sex, breed, birth date or estimated age, entry weight, unit price, history declarations) is corrected by the Owner or a Manager with a mandatory reason; old and new values, actor and time are kept, the voucher totals are recalculated and the Internal Cattle ID never changes. A herd entry can be voided only when the cattle has no recorded event other than its entry and first pen placement; a whole voucher can be voided only when this holds for every cattle on it. A voided cattle gets herd status VOIDED (final): its pen assignment is closed, its QR tag becomes RETIRED, it disappears from active lists, counts and reports (shown only with the status filter VOIDED), its line amount leaves the voucher total and its ID and code are not reused. | UC-FE06-03, UC-FE06-05 | Data-entry mistakes must be fixable without losing traceability. | System, on save
GBR-34 | Only the Owner creates, confirms, dismisses or voids a herd exit voucher. Exit type is SALE, CULL, DEATH or OTHER; exit date and reason or buyer are mandatory; the exit date is not in the future and not earlier than the herd entry date or the latest recorded event of any cattle on the voucher. A health case closed as DIED or MISSING creates a PENDING death voucher (GBR-95); the cattle stays ACTIVE until the Owner confirms or dismisses it. | FE-13, UC-FE10-03 | Removing an animal from the herd is an ownership decision. | System, on save
GBR-35 | Confirming an exit voucher, in one transaction for every cattle on it: sets herd status EXITED; closes the open pen assignment at the exit date; cancels scheduled doses and reminders dated after the exit; closes OPEN and CHRONIC health cases with outcome "Closed by herd exit"; closes an open pregnancy as "Dam exited". Before confirmation the Owner sees, per cattle, warnings for an open pregnancy, open health cases, an active withdrawal period, future doses and pending calves of the dam. Pending calves of the dam stay pending. | UC-FE13-01 | A complete, consistent end of the animal's record. | System, on confirmation
GBR-36 | Voiding a CONFIRMED exit voucher needs a mandatory reason; the voucher becomes VOIDED and is never deleted or voided twice. Every cattle on it returns to ACTIVE with a new pen assignment from the void date in its last pen; when that pen is INACTIVE or lacks places, the Owner chooses an ACTIVE pen with free places for each affected cattle. Items closed or cancelled by the exit are listed for review and are not restored automatically; recorded collections stay in the history. | UC-FE13-03 | A mistake is corrected without losing history. | System, on save
GBR-37 | The cattle activity timeline is a read-only view built from the source records of FE-04 to FE-06 and FE-08 to FE-13; it stores no copy and never creates or edits an event. Events are ordered by occurrence time and show both occurrence and entry time. Corrected records show their effective value with a "Corrected" marker; excluded or voided records are hidden unless "Show corrections" is selected. Schedules, upcoming doses and reminders are not timeline events. | UC-FE16-01 | One trustworthy history per animal. | Design rule
[/TABLE]
[P1838 4] 1.4 Growth, Vaccination and Health
[TABLE block 1839]
GBR-ID | Rule Statement | Applies To | Rationale | Enforced By
GBR-38 | A growth assessment contains a weight, a BCS or both. A weight is a positive number stored in kg with a method (electronic scale, measuring tape, other estimate) and a basis (MEASURED or ESTIMATED; tape and other estimates are always ESTIMATED). BCS uses the farm BCS scale (default 1.0–5.0 in steps of 0.5). The measurement time cannot be in the future or before the herd entry. | UC-FE08-01 | Comparable and honest growth data. | System, on save
GBR-39 | Growth indicators use valid records only. The current weight is the latest valid MEASURED weight, or the latest valid ESTIMATED weight when no measured weight exists; every displayed value shows its basis. A tape measurement stores heart girth and body length and an ESTIMATED weight = 88.4 × girth (m)² × length (m), which the user may overwrite. ADG (average daily gain) for a window = (last valid weight − first valid weight in the window) ÷ days between them, shown only when the two weights are at least 14 days apart. | FE-08, FE-15 | Transparent calculations that never hide estimates. | System, on calculation
GBR-40 | A wrong growth record is corrected (new values) or excluded with a mandatory reason. The original values, reason, actor and time are kept; excluded records are not used in calculations. | UC-FE08-03 | Mistakes are fixed without rewriting history. | System, on save
GBR-41 | A vaccination schedule targets an explicit set of ACTIVE cattle (selected by pen, herd group, life stage or individually; the selection is resolved to cattle when the schedule is saved) with one vaccine, a start date, number of doses, days between doses and an optional booster interval. The system creates one scheduled dose per cattle and dose number. Changing a schedule affects only doses not yet given; stopping it cancels its PLANNED and DEFERRED doses. Doses already given never change. | UC-FE09-02 to 04 | Planning is explicit; history is never rewritten. | System, on save
GBR-42 | A vaccination record is saved per cattle. ON_FARM records require vaccine, batch number and administration time and may close the matching scheduled dose (DONE); HISTORICAL records come from herd entry or a past document, may have unknown details and never close a scheduled dose. A batch recording saves one record per selected cattle; cattle marked "Defer" in the batch follow GBR-43 instead. | UC-FE09-05 | Every dose is traceable to one animal and one batch. | System, on save
GBR-43 | A scheduled dose can be deferred with a mandatory reason and an optional new expected date; it becomes DEFERRED and its reminder follows the new date. A deferred dose can be deferred again or recorded later. Due status is derived: upcoming (> reminder lead time), due (within lead time), overdue (past its date and not done). | UC-FE09-06 | Real-world delays are recorded instead of silently ignored. | System, on save and JOB-01
GBR-44 | A wrong vaccination record is corrected or voided by a Manager with a mandatory reason. Original values are kept; voiding an ON_FARM record returns its scheduled dose to PLANNED (or DEFERRED with its previous date) unless the schedule has stopped. | UC-FE09-08 | Fixes errors while keeping the schedule consistent. | System, on save
GBR-45 | An abnormal observation either opens a new health case or is added to an OPEN or CHRONIC case of the same cattle. A cattle may have several cases for different problems. Case status: OPEN (active problem) ⇄ CHRONIC (long-term monitoring) → CLOSED with outcome RECOVERED, DIED, MISSING or CLOSED_BY_EXIT. A Manager moves a case between OPEN and CHRONIC; a treatment recorded on a CHRONIC case returns it to OPEN. Health state of an ACTIVE cattle: UNDER_TREATMENT (Đang điều trị) when an OPEN case has at least one treatment; otherwise MONITORING (Theo dõi) when any case is OPEN or CHRONIC; otherwise HEALTHY (Khỏe mạnh). It is not shown for an EXITED or VOIDED cattle (transitions in 3.4.4). | FE-10 | Health status always comes from cases and separates sick, watched and healthy animals. | System, on save
GBR-46 | A treatment record is the treatment actually given: medicine from the farm catalogue, dose, route, time and performer. A daily course of the same medicine, dose and route may be recorded once with its number of days. The withdrawal end date = time of the last administration (first administration + course days − 1) + withdrawal days (default from the medicine, editable per treatment); an ON_FARM vaccination record likewise has withdrawal days (default from the vaccine, editable per record) and a withdrawal end date. The cattle's meat withdrawal end date is the latest end date among its VALID treatments and ON_FARM vaccination records; while it is today or later the profile and herd list show "Meat withdrawal until dd/mm/yyyy" and herd exit shows a warning. Meat withdrawal is a separate flag, not a health state. A suspected diagnosis and a contagious flag on a case are the user's own assessment. A wrong treatment or follow-up is corrected or voided by the Manager with a reason (UC-FE10-05, BR-D15), never edited silently; the withdrawal end date and health state are then recalculated from the VALID records. BeefCare does not diagnose or prescribe. | UC-FE10-04, UC-FE09-05, UC-FE13-01 | Food-safety traceability; clinical decisions stay with people. | System, on save
GBR-47 | Closing a case with outcome DIED or MISSING creates a PENDING death voucher for the Owner (GBR-34, GBR-95); the cattle is not exited automatically. A reaction after vaccination is recorded as a health case linked to the vaccination record. | UC-FE10-03, FE-09, FE-13 | Death is confirmed by the Owner; reactions stay traceable. | System, on save
[/TABLE]
[P1841 4] 1.5 Reproduction
[TABLE block 1842]
GBR-ID | Rule Statement | Applies To | Rationale | Enforced By
GBR-48 | Reproduction events belong to one female cattle of the farm. Linked records (estrus, breeding, check, episode, health case, sire) must belong to the same farm; an internal sire is a different, male cattle of that farm. All recording actions start from the female's profile (Reproduction tab). | FE-11, FE-12 | Prevents cross-farm and impossible links. | System, on save
GBR-49 | Estrus, breeding, pregnancy check, pregnancy update and calving are distinct real-world events. A suggestion, reminder or elapsed time never proves that an event happened. Each event stores its occurrence time separately from the entry time, and the recorder separately from the observer/performer; unknown values stay empty. Occurrence times cannot be in the future. | FE-11, FE-12, FE-14 | Factual records that support late entry. | System, on save
GBR-50 | Linking a breeding to an estrus event and a pregnancy check to a breeding is optional; the system proposes the latest eligible event. The first check never requires an existing pregnancy episode. | UC-FE11-03, UC-FE12-02 | Farms often record breeding late or without an observed estrus. | System, on save
GBR-51 | Breeding never establishes pregnancy. Only a valid PREGNANT check opens (or confirms) a pregnancy episode; NOT_PREGNANT returns the female to AWAITING_BREEDING; INCONCLUSIVE keeps the current status and creates a re-check reminder (REM-04). | UC-FE12-02 | Pregnancy must be confirmed by an actual check. | System, on save
GBR-52 | A female has at most one OPEN pregnancy episode. Competing changes for the same female are processed one at a time; older evidence never overwrites a newer independent episode. A closed (CALVED, LOST) or voided episode is never reopened by a normal update. | FE-12 | Prevents contradictory pregnancy states. | System, on save
GBR-53 | Expected calving date: after a valid breeding it is TENTATIVE = breeding time + gestation length (farm parameter, default 283 days); after a PREGNANT check it becomes CONFIRMED, based on the linked breeding. A PREGNANT check without a linked breeding but with estimated months pregnant gives an ESTIMATED date = checked date + gestation length − months × 30 days; a later check with a linked breeding replaces it. A later estrus or a NOT_PREGNANT check cancels the tentative date; a newer breeding replaces it. Without a breeding date or estimated months no expected date is calculated. | FE-11, FE-12, FE-14 | Planning information with an explicit basis. | System, on save
GBR-54 | Reproductive status applies to an ACTIVE female whose life stage is YOUNG_STOCK or ADULT and whose purpose type is BREEDING or UNDECIDED, and to any female with an OPEN pregnancy episode; for all other cattle it is shown as "Not applicable". For an applicable female it is derived from her latest VALID events (transitions in 3.4.3): AWAITING_BREEDING (Chờ phối: no current estrus, breeding, pregnancy or post-calving period, including after a NOT_PREGNANT check or a pregnancy loss), IN_ESTRUS (estrus within the last 24 hours, no later breeding), BRED (breeding without a later estrus or check result; also after an INCONCLUSIVE check), PREGNANT (OPEN episode; labelled "Sắp đẻ" within the calving preparation lead time before the expected date), POST_CALVING (calved within the post-calving interval, no later estrus or breeding). The recording actions offered follow the status: AWAITING_BREEDING → Record estrus, Record breeding; IN_ESTRUS → Record estrus, Record breeding; BRED → Record estrus, Record pregnancy check, Record calving; PREGNANT → Record pregnancy check, Record pregnancy update, Record calving; POST_CALVING → Record estrus, Record breeding. For any ACTIVE female at stage YOUNG_STOCK or ADULT, "More actions" also offers Record pregnancy check and Record calving (untracked pregnancy), so that a real event can always be recorded. | FE-11, FE-12 | Users see only the actions that make sense now. | System, on display and save
GBR-55 | Breeding eligibility is an advisory indicator, separate from reproductive status: "Met" when all breeding conditions (minimum age and minimum weight from farm parameters, using the latest valid weight; no OPEN health case) are met, otherwise "Not met" with the failed conditions. It is shown for females in status AWAITING_BREEDING, IN_ESTRUS or POST_CALVING on the profile and in the reproduction worklist. A Manager may still record a breeding that happened. | UC-FE11-01, UC-FE11-03, UC-FE18-17 | Decision support, not a hard block on a real event. | System, on display
GBR-56 | A pregnancy update is OBSERVATION, COMPLICATION or LOSS against the OPEN episode. A LOSS closes the episode as LOST without creating calves; the female becomes AWAITING_BREEDING (or the status of any later event) and the loss is counted in the reproduction indicators (GBR-106). A complication may be linked to an existing health case of the same cattle; creating or treating a case uses FE-10. | UC-FE12-03 | Separates reproduction from health management. | System, on save
GBR-57 | A calving saves dam, birth time, data source, calving course, dam condition and one outcome per calf (LIVE or STILLBORN) in one transaction. A birth weight needs a unit and a MEASURED/ESTIMATED basis; unknown sex or weight stays empty. A calving may be recorded without a tracked episode (pregnancy never checked); the episode link is then empty and no earlier event is invented. Saving closes the episode as CALVED and sets the dam to POST_CALVING. | UC-FE12-04 | Complete and consistent birth records. | System, on save
GBR-58 | For each live calf the Manager chooses "Save as draft calf" or "Register now". Register now creates the cattle immediately (UC-FE06-01 logic). A draft calf stays PENDING_CONFIRMATION and creates reminder REM-09 until the Owner or a Manager confirms it in UC-FE06-01. A draft calf's ear-tag/QR code must be unique in the farm; a draft calf receives no other events until registered; a calf that dies before registration is marked DIED_BEFORE_REGISTRATION and is never changed from LIVE to STILLBORN. | UC-FE12-04, UC-FE06-01 | Calves can be registered at once or after checking details, without duplicates. | System, on save
GBR-59 | Only a Farm Manager corrects or voids a reproductive record, with a mandatory reason and the current record version; immutable before/after values are kept. A correction or void must not orphan or contradict later checks, episodes, updates, calvings or registered calves; unresolved dependencies block the change and history is never cascade-deleted. | UC-FE11-04, UC-FE12-05, UC-FE12-06 | Field errors can be fixed while protecting data integrity. | System, on save
[/TABLE]

GBR-67 | Farm catalogue codes (breed, vaccine, medicine, production purpose) are unique within the farm. A referenced catalogue item is deactivated, never deleted; a vaccine that has never been referenced may be deleted. Editing an item never rewrites past records, which keep a snapshot of the name used. Inactive items cannot be chosen for new records. The purpose type of a production purpose cannot be changed once the purpose is referenced by a cattle or a pen criteria version. | FE-18 | Stable master data with true history. | System, on save
GBR-68 | Farm state and reminder parameters are saved as a new version with a source note and effective date; a version affects only reminders and suggestions evaluated after it takes effect. GBR-70 | A payment request (quote) is immutable: farm, term, amount, price version, receiving account, unique payment reference and VietQR. It is valid for 24 hours from creation. GBR-72 | A payment becomes PAID only through the shared confirmation operation used by both the SePay webhook and PM acceptance. It requires authenticated bank evidence that is incoming, has the exact amount, the configured receiving account, a bank-paid time inside the quote window [created, expires) and is not already allocated, and that identifies exactly one request: automatically by the exact unique payment reference in the transfer content, or, in PM review only, by the PM explicitly selecting the single candidate request with a reason when the reference is mistyped. One evidence funds one payment; amounts are never combined, split or used to infer a term. Before acceptance all service dates are estimates. | UC-FE19-06, UC-FE19-08 | Predictable coverage; early renewal never loses days. | System, on confirmation
GBR-74 | Every authenticated bank transaction is retained as evidence, deduplicated by (provider, environment, account namespace, provider transaction ID), including unmatched, partial, excess and late transfers. Unaccepted evidence never grants service or produces a receipt. A PM decision on evidence is ACCEPT (re-runs GBR-72), HOLD (stays unresolved) or REJECT, each with a reason. BeefCare does not refund money; evidence to be returned outside the system is locked so it can never fund a payment. | UC-FE19-05, UC-FE19-06 | Full traceability of money received. | System, on save
GBR-75 | The PM publishes plan prices as a complete version (all three terms, positive whole VND) effective immediately or at a future time. Published versions are immutable. New quotes and the public page use the version effective at that time; issued quotes and accepted periods never change. | UC-FE19-07, UC-FE22-01 | Price changes are controlled and traceable. | System, on save

[P1856 4] 1.10 Herd Entry and Exit Vouchers
[TABLE block 1857]
GBR-ID | Rule Statement | Applies To | Rationale | Enforced By
GBR-90 | A herd entry voucher is of type PURCHASE or FARM_BORN and has one line per cattle. A DRAFT is visible to the Owner and Managers, can be edited or discarded, and creates no cattle, pen change, balance or reminder effect. Posting rechecks every line and the header and applies GBR-32; the voucher then becomes POSTED and its lines can no longer be added or removed (corrections per cattle follow GBR-33). Every cattle receives a QR tag; printing is optional. A repeated post returns the first result. | UC-FE06-01, UC-FE06-05 | One document per receipt of cattle; no half-entered animals. | System, on save and post
GBR-91 | Line amount = unit price × weight (per kg) or unit price (per head), in whole VND. Entry voucher and feed receipt: total payable = goods amount + transport fee. Exit voucher: total receivable = cattle amount − deductions. Outstanding = total − sum of RECORDED payments; payment status (Unpaid, Partly paid, Paid) is derived. A payment or collection is a positive whole-VND amount, dated not in the future and not before the voucher date, with method Cash or Bank transfer, and never exceeds the outstanding amount (BR-D39). A wrong payment is voided with a reason, never edited. Voiding a voucher keeps its payments in the history; refunds are handled outside BeefCare. The Owner and Managers record payments of entry vouchers and feed receipts; only the Owner records collections of exit vouchers. These amounts are farm bookkeeping, not invoices, tax or accounting documents, and are not part of platform finance (OR-08). | UC-FE06-01, UC-FE06-04, UC-FE13-01, UC-FE13-04, UC-FE23-02 | The farm knows what it owes and is owed per voucher. | System, on save
GBR-92 | The pricing basis (per kg or per head) applies to all lines of a voucher; per-kg pricing requires a weight on every line. Each weight has a basis MEASURED or ESTIMATED and an estimated weight is labelled "UT". The default exit weight is the latest valid weight with basis ESTIMATED; entering a weighed value makes it MEASURED. Entry and exit weights are kept on the voucher line and are not growth assessments. | UC-FE06-01, UC-FE13-01 | Prices match how cattle are actually traded. | System, on save
GBR-93 | A FARM_BORN voucher contains only live calves in PENDING_CONFIRMATION whose dam is in the farm. For each calf the entry date is its birth date, the life stage is UNWEANED_CALF, parentage and birth data come from the calving (GBR-31) and the production purpose defaults to the first ACTIVE purpose of type UNDECIDED. The voucher has no amounts. "Register now" at calving (UC-FE12-04) creates and posts a FARM_BORN voucher automatically. A calf marked "died before registration" (with a reason) leaves the pending list and resolves REM-09. | UC-FE06-01, UC-FE12-04 | Calves enter the herd with the same traceable document as bought cattle. | System, on save
GBR-94 | A herd exit voucher is created, edited and confirmed by the Owner only. Only ACTIVE cattle that are on no other DRAFT, PENDING or CONFIRMED exit line can be added (BR-D35), from a filtered list or by QR scan. A DRAFT keeps its cattle ACTIVE and has no effect. Before confirmation the system shows the checks of GBR-35 per cattle; a SALE or CULL voucher dated before the end of a cattle's meat withdrawal period is highlighted (GBR-46). Warnings never block; the Owner must tick the confirmation. Confirming applies GBR-35 to every line; if any cattle is no longer ACTIVE, nothing is confirmed. | UC-FE13-01 | Selling or culling is an ownership decision taken with full information. | System, on save and confirmation
GBR-95 | A health case closed as DIED or MISSING creates one PENDING DEATH voucher for the cattle with the case, reporter and date. The Owner completes the cause and, for a death, the carcass disposal (destroyed or buried on the farm, handed to the veterinary service, other) and confirms it, or dismisses it with a mandatory reason, in which case the cattle stays ACTIVE and the case keeps its outcome. A DEATH voucher has no amounts. The Owner may also record a DEATH voucher directly. While the voucher is PENDING the cattle shows "Chờ xác nhận chết / mất" and accepts no new event (GBR-26), and REM-12 reminds the Owner until it is confirmed or dismissed. | UC-FE13-01, UC-FE10-03 | Deaths and losses are confirmed with evidence. | System, on case closure and confirmation
GBR-96 | Cattle lines can be imported into a DRAFT purchase voucher from the BeefCare Excel template (max 200 rows). Imported lines can be edited before posting. | UC-FE06-01 | Fast entry of large purchases without bypassing validation. | System, on import
GBR-97 | Trading partners (sellers, buyers, feed suppliers) belong to the farm, have a unique name and no BeefCare account. [P1859 4] 1.11 Feed Management
[TABLE block 1860]
GBR-ID | Rule Statement | Applies To | Rationale | Enforced By
GBR-98 | A feed item has a code (TA-nn) unique in the farm, a group, the stock unit kg, an optional purchase unit with its kg conversion and a minimum stock level. Code and stock unit cannot change after the first stock movement; an item with movements is made inactive, never deleted; inactive items cannot be used in new receipts, rations or feeding records. Editing an item never changes past documents. | UC-FE23-10 | Stable units keep stock and cost correct. | System, on save
GBR-99 | Feed stock changes only through stock movements created by posting a receipt, voiding a receipt, posting an adjustment and saving, correcting or voiding a feeding record; stock = sum of movements and never becomes negative (BR-D36). A DRAFT receipt has no effect. Posting a receipt adds each line in kg and recalculates the weighted average cost = (stock value + received value) ÷ (stock + received kg). A POSTED receipt is voided, with a reason, only while the stock of every feed covers its received quantity; otherwise a stock adjustment is used. | UC-FE23-01, UC-FE23-02, UC-FE23-03 | Stock is always explainable by documents. | System, on post and void
GBR-100 | A stock adjustment compares counted stock with system stock at the count date; every non-zero difference needs a reason and creates one movement valued at the weighted average cost. Posted adjustments are immutable and never change receipts or feeding records; a mistake is corrected with a new adjustment. | UC-FE23-04 | Physical counts are reconciled without rewriting history. | System, on post
GBR-101 | A ration defines, for its pens, the kg per head of each feed for each daily session (1–3 sessions). Changing a ration saves a new version effective from tomorrow or later; the previous version ends the day before. At most one ration version is effective per pen and date (BR-D38). A scheduled version can be cancelled and an effective ration stopped from a future date. Recorded feeding is never changed by a new version. | UC-FE23-06 | The plan can evolve while the record of what was planned stays true. | System, on save
GBR-102 | A feeding record is kept per pen, date and session (BR-D37), not in the future. Planned kg per feed = kg per head of the effective ration × headcount of the pen at recording time. Actual kg per feed ≥ 0 and not above its stock. Saving deducts the actual kg from stock at the weighted average cost. Only a Manager records, corrects or voids feeding; a correction or void needs a reason, keeps the original values (BR-D15) and adjusts stock by the difference. The person who fed may be a Technician. | UC-FE23-08, UC-FE23-09 | Daily feeding is recorded once and reflected in stock. | System, on save
GBR-103 | Feed permissions: the Owner and Managers view stock and manage receipts, payments to suppliers, adjustments, rations, the catalogue and the report; only Managers record, correct and void feeding records; Technicians view rations (Today's Rations) and the feeding log. Feed management requires an ACTIVE farm service (GBR-14). | FE-23 | Feeding follows the farm's care responsibilities. | System, on every request
GBR-104 | REM-10 (low feed stock) is raised for an ACTIVE feed item whose stock is at or below its minimum level, for the Owner and Managers. REM-11 (feeding session not recorded) is raised for each session of today's effective ration of an occupied pen with no VALID record 2 hours after the session time, for Managers and Technicians. Both follow GBR-63. | FE-23, JOB-01 | Shortages and missed feedings are seen in time. | JOB-01
GBR-105 | Feed cost of a feeding record = Σ actual kg × weighted average cost at recording time. Days of cover = stock ÷ average daily use of the last 7 days. Stock value = Σ stock × average cost. Average head of a period = mean daily headcount from pen assignments. Variance versus ration = (actual − planned) ÷ planned over recorded sessions. Losses from stock adjustments are reported separately and not allocated to herd groups. Rations show estimated cost from current average costs. | UC-FE23-01, UC-FE23-05, UC-FE23-11 | Reproducible feed consumption and cost figures. | System, on calculation
[/TABLE]
[P1862 4] 1.12 Herd Performance
[TABLE block 1863]
GBR-ID | Rule Statement | Applies To | Rationale | Enforced By
GBR-106 | Reproduction indicators use VALID events only and are shown with the number of females or events (n); a value based on fewer than 3 is shown as "Insufficient data". Definitions: (1) calving interval = days between two consecutive calvings of the same female; (2) days open = days from a calving to the breeding that led to the next confirmed pregnancy (the breeding linked to the PREGNANT check); (3) services per conception = number of breedings in a cycle (since the last calving, or since the first breeding of a heifer) up to and including the conceiving breeding; (4) first-service conception rate = first services of a cycle with a PREGNANT result ÷ first services with a PREGNANT or NOT_PREGNANT result; (5) overall conception rate = breedings with a PREGNANT result ÷ breedings with a PREGNANT or NOT_PREGNANT result (INCONCLUSIVE checks are excluded); (6) pregnancy loss rate = episodes closed LOST ÷ episodes closed CALVED or LOST; (7) age at first calving = age in months at the first calving; (8) overdue for conception = applicable females not PREGNANT more than (post-calving interval + 3 × estrus cycle length) days after their last calving (farm parameters, default 108 days). Herd values are averages or ratios over the events that end in the report period. | UC-FE15-01, UC-FE15-02, UC-FE11-01, UC-FE12-01 | Breeding herds are judged by calving interval, conception and losses; the figures come from the events already recorded. | System, on calculation
GBR-107 | Fattening performance covers cattle with purpose type FATTENING during the period. The weight of a cattle at a date is interpolated linearly between the nearest VALID weights before and after it when both are within 60 days; otherwise the nearest VALID weight within 7 days is used; otherwise the weight is unknown. Gain = weight at the end of the period (or exit) − weight at the start (or entry); ADG = gain ÷ days. The feed of a pen on a day (VALID feeding records) is shared equally among the cattle in the pen that day (pen assignments), and its cost likewise (GBR-105). Feed per kg gain = attributed feed (kg as fed) ÷ gain, and feed cost per kg gain = attributed feed cost ÷ gain, over the cattle with a known start and end weight; the others are reported as "Insufficient weights (n head)". Projected finishing date = latest valid weight date + (target finishing weight − latest weight) ÷ ADG of the last 60 days; it is not shown without a target, without two valid weights at least 14 days apart, or when that ADG is not positive. A cattle at or above the target shows "Target reached" and raises REM-13. No purchase price, sale price or profit is calculated. | UC-FE15-02, UC-FE08-02, FE-14 | Feeding decisions need gain, feed use and feed cost per kg of gain, computed from data already recorded. | System, on calculation
GBR-109 | A new farm is created with its default farm parameter version and the starter catalogue of Table III-6: four production purposes (one per purpose type, ACTIVE), the common breeds of Vietnam (ACTIVE when chosen as main breeds at registration, otherwise INACTIVE), vaccines by disease with empty dose and route (to be completed from the product label) and common feed items without stock. Reference medicines are not pre-created because dose and meat withdrawal depend on the product. Starter items belong to the farm and follow GBR-67; changing them affects no other farm. | UC-FE02-01, FE-18, FE-23 | A new farm can record its first cattle and care events at once, with lists in local terms. | System, on farm registration
[/TABLE]
[P1865 3] 2. System Messages
[P1866 ] Message content is given in English; the interface shows the Vietnamese translation with the same meaning (OR-01). Values in braces are filled at run time; text in square brackets is a button or link. Messages report committed results only and never replace backend checks.

[P1869 3] 3. Other Requirements
[TABLE block 1870]
ID | Other Requirement
OR-01 | Language: the farm interface uses Vietnamese terminology suitable for the target farms; this specification uses English names with the Vietnamese interface titles in brackets. Translated labels and messages keep the same roles, conditions and meaning.
OR-02 | QR ear tag layout: the printed tag shows the display code (BC-nnnnnn) above the QR code; the QR resolves the permanent Internal Cattle ID and stays valid when the ear tag number changes.
OR-03 | QR distinction: cattle QR tags and payment VietQR codes are named and displayed distinctly; a payment QR shows the receiving account, amount and payment reference; a cattle QR opens an authorized cattle profile.
OR-04 | Data retention: cattle, herd entry and exit, care, reproduction, membership, payment and evidence records needed for traceability remain available to authorized users after cattle exit, membership deactivation or service expiry. No automatic deletion period is defined in this release.
OR-05 | Responsive web: all roles use the same responsive web application on desktop, tablet and phone; no separate native mobile application is built.
OR-06 | Decision support only: BeefCare shows conditions, reminders, warnings and summaries; it never decides breeding, sale, culling, treatment or recovery and never records an event by itself. A person with the right role records or confirms every event.
OR-07 | External professional input: veterinarians, AI technicians and buyers do not have accounts; their advice, results and names are recorded as information by the Farm Manager or Owner. BeefCare provides no diagnosis or prescription.
OR-08 | Scope: one Owner may own several independent farms; organisation-level consolidation of farms is out of scope. Feed management (FE-23: feed stock, feed receipts, rations and feeding log) and the values, payments and collections of herd entry and exit vouchers are in scope as farm bookkeeping; general inventory of other goods (medicines, equipment), purchase orders, invoices and tax documents, farm accounting, payroll, IoT devices and native mobile applications are out of scope. Platform support, payment reconciliation, basic platform cash records and account administration are in scope.
OR-09 | SePay environments: Sandbox uses simulated transactions and Live uses real bank transfers. Endpoints, receiving accounts, account namespaces, API tokens and webhook secrets are separate per environment; the same business rules apply in both.
OR-10 | Record presentation: screens and reports distinguish occurrence time from entry time, UNKNOWN history from a negative finding, estimates from measurements, scheduled activities from performed ones, and payment status from service status and suspension.
OR-11 | Identity and email: people sign in with Google or with a verified email and a BeefCare password (API-05, GBR-01); Google manages the security and recovery of Google accounts, BeefCare manages BeefCare passwords. A phone number is contact information only. Emails (invitations, links, notices) are sent through the Email Delivery Service (API-04), provider to be selected at deployment.
OR-12 | Subscription billing: one functional plan per farm with 1-, 2- and 3-calendar-year prices; an immutable 24-hour payment request per purchase; renewal requests open in the last 90 days of confirmed coverage; service dates are estimates until the payment is accepted (Policy A, GBR-69 to GBR-75).
OR-13 | Support and finance traceability: ticket conversations, status and resolution history, and finance posting and voiding history are kept for authorized review. Legal accounting compliance is not claimed.
OR-14 | Public content: the landing page shows the official project name BeefCare, the introduction, implemented features, the current 1/2/3-year prices, the FAQ and the public contact email tuanpqhe181869@fpt.edu.vn and phone 0386671863, without a postal address. No unpublished price, private record or unsupported feature is shown.
[/TABLE]
